## 630275
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいえ。レムオン様を待ちわびて<line-break>食事ものどを通りませんでしたわ。<end>"
EN: "No. I longed so desperately to see you, Lord Lemghon, that I could scarcely eat.<end>"
KO: "아니요. 레무온 님을 기다리느라 식사도 목에 넘어가지 않았어요.<end>"

## 630276
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほう、とてもそうは見えないが。<end>"
EN: "Oh? You certainly don't look it.<end>"
KO: "호오, 전혀 그렇게 보이지 않는데.<end>"

## 630277
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まぁ、意地悪ですこと。<line-break>これでも、お食後のデザートは<line-break>ひかえていますのに。<end>"
EN: "My, how unkind. And here I have even been denying myself dessert after dinner.<end>"
KO: "어머, 심술궂으시네요. 이래 봬도 식후 디저트는 참고 있는데요.<end>"

## 630278
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほぅ、ウェディングドレスの<line-break>仮縫いでも始まるのか？　　　<end>"
EN: "Oh? Have the fittings for your wedding dress begun?<end>"
KO: "호오, 웨딩드레스 가봉이라도 시작하나?<end>"

## 630279
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また、そのお話？<line-break>レムオン様でも怒りますよ。<end>"
EN: "That again? Keep it up and even you will make me angry, Lord Lemghon.<end>"
KO: "또 그 이야기인가요? 레무온 님이라도 화내실 거예요.<end>"

## 630280
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それはいい。<line-break>ティアナの怒った顔も<line-break>一度は見てみたいものだ。<line-break>ついでに絵描きを呼んで、<line-break>肖像画を描かせよう。<line-break>怒った顔をした記念にな。<end>"
EN: "Good. I should like to see Tiana angry at least once. We could summon a painter and have a portrait made to commemorate the occasion.<end>"
KO: "그거 좋군. 티아나가 화난 얼굴도 한 번 보고 싶어. 내친김에 화가를 불러 초상화를 그리게 하자. 화난 얼굴 기념으로.<end>"

## 630281
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それでは、そのとなりに<line-break>レムオン様が大笑いした顔を<line-break>描かせましょうか？<end>"
EN: "Then shall we have the painter put a portrait of you laughing heartily beside it, Lord Lemghon?<end>"
KO: "그럼 그 옆에 레무온 님이 크게 웃는 얼굴을 그리게 할까요?<end>"

## 630282
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やれやれ。王妃ではなく<line-break>王妃の娘にやり込められるようでは、<line-break>リューガ家に勝ち目はないな。<end>"
EN: "Good grief. If I can be bested by the queen's daughter rather than the queen herself, House Ryuga stands no chance.<end>"
KO: "원 참. 왕비가 아니라 왕비의 딸에게 당할 정도라면 류가 가문에 승산은 없겠군.<end>"

## 630283
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まぁ、レムオン様が弱音を…。<line-break>めずらしいこと。<end>"
EN: "My, Lord Lemghon showing weakness... How unusual.<end>"
KO: "어머, 레무온 님께서 약한 소리를…. 드문 일이네요.<end>"

## 630284
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "形勢不利のときは退くに限る。<line-break>そろそろ、退散しようか。<end>"
EN: "When the tide turns against you, retreat is the wisest course. Perhaps it is time we withdrew.<end>"
KO: "형세가 불리할 때는 물러나는 게 상책이지. 슬슬 퇴각할까.<end>"

## 630285
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あら、まだこれからですわ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, but we're only getting started.<end>"
KO: "어머, 아직 이제부터랍니다.<end>"

## 630286
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "残念ながら政務に追われる身だ。<line-break>今日のところは、これで失礼する。<end>"
EN: "Regrettably, affairs of state keep me occupied. I shall take my leave for today.<end>"
KO: "유감이지만 정무에 쫓기는 몸이라서. 오늘은 이만 실례하지.<end>"

## 630287
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "仕方ありませんね。<line-break>では、ごきげんよう。<end>"
EN: "Very well. Good day, then.<end>"
KO: "어쩔 수 없군요. 그럼, 안녕히 계세요.<end>"

## 630288
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おふたりが並んでいらっしゃると、<line-break>とても兄妹には見えませんわね。<line-break>まるで、友達同士のようですわ。<end>"
EN: "When I see the two of you together, you hardly look like brother and sister. You seem more like a pair of friends.<end>"
KO: "두 분이 나란히 계시면 정말 남매처럼 보이지 않네요. 마치 친구 사이 같아요.<end>"

## 630289
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ただの友達なら、<line-break>どんなに気が楽なことか。<end>"
EN: "If we were merely friends, how much easier it would be.<end>"
KO: "그냥 친구라면 얼마나 마음이 편할까.<end>"

## 630290
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まぁ。　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>様、<line-break>意地悪なお兄様に似ていなくて<line-break>よかったですね。<end>"
EN: "My. <value:$28>, I'm glad you don't take after your mean brother.<end>"
KO: "어머. <value:$28>님은 짓궂은 오라버니를 닮지 않아서 다행이네요.<end>"

## 630291
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふふ。女ふたりを敵に回しては、<line-break>勝ち目はなくってよ。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. With two women against you, you haven't a chance.<end>"
KO: "후후후. 여자 둘을 적으로 돌리고서는 승산이 없답니다.<end>"

## 630292
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "となれば、ティアナのフィアンセ殿を<line-break>味方につける以外にないかな。<end>"
EN: "Then perhaps my only recourse is to enlist Tiana's fiance as an ally.<end>"
KO: "그렇다면 티아나의 약혼자님을 우리 편으로 끌어들이는 수밖에 없겠군.<end>"

## 630293
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうぞ、ご自由に。　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>その代わり、この部屋には<line-break>二度とお通ししませんわ。<end>"
EN: "Do as you please. But if you do, you will never be admitted to this room again.<end>"
KO: "마음대로 하세요. 대신 이 방에는 두 번 다시 들이지 않겠습니다.<end>"

## 630294
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何より、レムオン様が<line-break>あんな野蛮な人と手を組むとは<line-break>思えませんけど。<end>"
EN: "Besides, I cannot imagine you joining forces with such a savage, Lord Lemghon.<end>"
KO: "무엇보다 레무온 님이 그런 야만적인 사람과 손을 잡았다고는 생각되지 않아요.<end>"

## 630295
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "完敗だ。<line-break>さすがはエリス王妃のご息女だな。<end>"
EN: "A complete defeat. You truly are Queen Eris's daughter.<end>"
KO: "완패다. 역시 에리스 왕비의 따님답군.<end>"

## 630296
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふふ。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee.<end>"
KO: "후후후.<end>"

## 630297
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "長居した。<line-break>そろそろ失礼しよう。<end>"
EN: "I have stayed too long. It is time I left.<end>"
KO: "오래 머물렀군. 이만 실례하지.<end>"

## 630298
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まぁ、もうお帰りですの？<line-break>お忙しいのですね。<end>"
EN: "My, you're leaving already? You must be very busy.<end>"
KO: "어머, 벌써 돌아가시는 건가요? 바쁘시군요.<end>"

## 630299
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティアナを見習って、少しは<line-break>女らしい言葉遣いを覚えてはどうだ？<end>"
EN: "Why not take a lesson from Tiana and learn to speak a little more like a lady?<end>"
KO: "티아나를 본받아서 조금은 여자다운 말투를 익히는 게 어떠냐?<end>"

## 630300
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、<line-break>まぁ、似合わぬだろうがな。<end>"
EN: "Heh. Though I doubt it would suit you.<end>"
KO: "후후, 뭐, 어울리진 않겠지만.<end>"

## 630301
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あら？　この音はもしや…。<end>"
EN: "Oh? Could that sound be...?<end>"
KO: "어머? 이 소리는 혹시….<end>"

## 630302
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まぁ、やはり<value:$28>様<line-break>でしたのね。今度は堂々と正面から<line-break>お入りくださいと申しあげましたのに。<end>"
EN: "Why, it was you after all, <value:$28>. I did ask you to come openly through the front entrance this time.<end>"
KO: "역시 <value:$28>님이셨군요. 이번엔 당당히 정문으로 들어오시라 말씀드렸는데요.<end>"

## 630303
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うふふ。<value:$28>様が<line-break>うらやましいですわ。王女の部屋に<line-break>忍び込むだなんて、楽しそうですもの。<line-break>それで、人形は持ち主の女の子に<line-break>返していただけたのですね？<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. How I envy you, <value:$28>. Sneaking into a princess's room sounds like such fun. And you returned the doll to the girl it belongs to, didn't you?<end>"
KO: "후후. <value:$28>님이 부러워요. 왕녀의 방에 몰래 들어가다니 재미있었겠네요. 그래서 인형은 주인인 여자아이에게 돌려주셨죠?<end>"

## 630304
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うふふ。やはり<value:$28>様<line-break>でしたのね。この間は追い返すような<line-break>まねをして申しわけありませんでした。<line-break>それで、人形は持ち主の女の子に<line-break>返していただけたのですね？<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. It was you after all, <value:$28>. I'm sorry I practically chased you away the other day. And you returned the doll to the girl it belongs to, didn't you?<end>"
KO: "우후후. 역시 <value:$28> 님이셨군요. 지난번에는 쫓아내는 듯한 행동을 해서 죄송했습니다. 그래서 인형은 주인인 여자아이에게 돌려주셨나요?<end>"

## 630305
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はい。どなたです？<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>まぁ、<value:$28>様ですのね。<line-break>どうぞ、お入りください。<end>"
EN: "Yes? Who is it? Oh, <value:$28>. Please, come in.<end>"
KO: "네. 누구시죠? 어머, <value:$28>님이시군요. 들어오세요.<end>"

## 630306
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うふふ。今度はちゃんと<line-break>正面からお入りになったのね。<line-break>　<line-break>でも私、今度はいつノーブル伯が<line-break>クローゼットから現れるのかと<line-break>楽しみにしていたんですよ。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. You came properly through the front entrance this time. But I was rather looking forward to seeing when the Count of Noble would emerge from my wardrobe again.<end>"
KO: "우후후. 이번에는 제대로 정문으로 들어오셨군요. 하지만 이번엔 언제 노블 백작님이 옷장에서 나타날지 기대하고 있었어요.<end>"

## 630307
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うふふ。冗談です。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>それで、人形は持ち主の女の子に<line-break>返していただけたのですね？<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. I'm only teasing. And you returned the doll to the girl it belongs to, didn't you?<end>"
KO: "후후, 농담이에요. 그래서 인형은 주인인 여자아이에게 돌려주셨죠?<end>"

## 630308
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まぁ、そのことを<line-break>伝えに来てくださったのですか？<line-break>ありがとうございます。<end>"
EN: "Oh, you came to tell me that? Thank you.<end>"
KO: "어머, 그걸 전하러 와 주신 건가요? 감사합니다.<end>"

## 630309
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですか。<line-break>女の子は喜んでくれたのですね。　　<line-break>よかったです。<line-break>本当なら私が直接お会いして<line-break>おわびしなければなりませんのに…。<end>"
EN: "I see. The girl was pleased, then. I'm glad. Though I really ought to have met her and apologized in person...<end>"
KO: "그렇군요. 여자아이가 기뻐해줬군요. 다행이에요. 원래라면 제가 직접 만나 사과해야 하는데…<end>"

## 630310
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ！　どなたか来られたみたいだわ。<end>"
EN: "Ah! I think someone's coming.<end>"
KO: "아! 누가 오신 것 같아.<end>"

## 630311
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様、<line-break>申しわけありませんが、また　<line-break>クローゼットの中へ。<line-break>親が決めたこととは言え、<line-break>私には婚約者がおりますから。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry, <value:$28>, but I must ask you to get back into the wardrobe. Though Mother decided the match, I am betrothed.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님, 죄송하지만 다시 옷장 안으로 들어가 주세요. 부모님이 정한 일이라 해도 제겐 약혼자가 있으니까요.<end>"

## 630312
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その私と噂になってもよいと<line-break>おっしゃるなら、構いませんけど。<line-break>うふふ。<end>"
EN: "Of course, if you don't mind people gossiping about the two of us, I have no objection. Hee hee.<end>"
KO: "그런 저와 소문이 나도 괜찮다고 하신다면 상관없지만요. 우후후.<end>"

## 630313
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうしますか、<value:$28>様？<line-break>よろしければ、またクローゼットを<line-break>お貸ししますわ。うふふ。<end>"
EN: "What would you like to do, <value:$28>? If you wish, you're welcome to use my wardrobe again. Hee hee.<end>"
KO: "어떻게 하시겠어요, <value:$28>님? 괜찮으시면 또 옷장을 빌려드릴게요. 후후.<end>"

## 630314
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様、<line-break>申しわけありませんが、また　　　<line-break>クローゼットの中へ。<line-break>王家に縁もゆかりもない者が<line-break>王女の部屋へ忍び込んだと知れたら<line-break>無事ではすみませんよ。うふふ。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry, <value:$28>, but back into the wardrobe you go. If word got out that someone with no ties to the royal family had slipped into a princess's room, you wouldn't escape unscathed. Hee hee.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 님, 죄송하지만 다시 옷장 안으로 들어가 주세요. 왕가와 아무 인연도 없는 사람이 왕녀의 방에 숨어들었다는 걸 들키면 무사히 끝나지 않을 겁니다. 후후.<end>"

## 630315
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、でも今度は私が呼ぶまで<line-break>待っていてくださいね。<line-break>もう少しお話ししたいですから。<end>"
EN: "But this time, please wait until I call for you. I'd like to talk a little longer.<end>"
KO: "아, 그래도 이번에는 제가 부를 때까지 기다려 주세요. 조금 더 이야기하고 싶으니까요.<end>"

## 630316
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "공작님이시군요. 잘 오셨습니다.<end>"

## 630317

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 630318

## 630320
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お母様ですら一目置く<line-break>レムオン様のおいでですもの。<line-break>当然のおもてなしですわ。<end>"
EN: "Even Mother considers you someone to be reckoned with, Lemghon. Such a welcome is only natural.<end>"
KO: "어머님조차 한눈에 인정하시는 레무온 님이 오셨으니 당연한 대접이에요.<end>"

## 630321
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "笑える冗談だ。<end>"
EN: "That's a fine joke.<end>"
KO: "웃기는 농담이군.<end>"

## 630322
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、どういうものかな？<line-break>俺のような者を部屋に入れるとは。<end>"
EN: "Still, is it wise to admit someone like me to your room?<end>"
KO: "하지만 어떻게 된 일이지? 나 같은 사람을 방에 들이다니.<end>"

## 630323
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フィアンセが聞いたら<line-break>怒るのではないか？　　　　　　　<end>"
EN: "Wouldn't your betrothed be angry if word got out?<end>"
KO: "약혼자가 들으면 화내지 않을까?<end>"

## 630325
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だいたい、酒とバクチにおぼれて<line-break>宮廷に近寄ろうともしない方を、<line-break>ティアナは婚約者とは認めません。<end>"
EN: "Besides, someone mired in drink and gambling and unwilling even to come near the court is no betrothed of Tiana's.<end>"
KO: "애초에 술과 도박에 빠져 궁정에는 가까이도 하지 않는 분을 티아나는 약혼자로 인정하지 않습니다.<end>"

## 630326
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それに私は、ファーロス家を<line-break>発展させるための道具ではありません。<end>"
EN: "And I am not a tool for advancing House Pharos.<end>"
KO: "그리고 저는 파로스 가문을 발전시키기 위한 도구가 아닙니다.<end>"

## 630327
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やれやれ、エリスも哀れだな。　<end>"
EN: "Well, poor Eris.<end>"
KO: "이런, 에리스도 불쌍하군.<end>"

## 630328
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ファーロス家発展のために<line-break>知略の限りを尽くしたところが、<end>"
EN: "She has employed every stratagem at her command to advance House Pharos,<end>"
KO: "파로스 가문의 발전을 위해 온갖 지략을 다 짜낸 결과가,<end>"

## 630329
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "娘のわがままのせいで<line-break>その綿密な計画も水の泡か。<end>"
EN: "only for her meticulous plans to come to nothing because of her daughter's selfishness.<end>"
KO: "딸의 제멋대로인 행동 때문에 그 치밀한 계획도 물거품인가.<end>"

## 630330
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ひどいわ、レムオン様！<line-break>わがまま、だなんて。　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>これでも、国のことは、<line-break>いろいろと考えているつもりです。<end>"
EN: "That's cruel, Lemghon! Selfish, you say? I do give a great deal of thought to the affairs of the realm.<end>"
KO: "너무하세요, 레무온 님! 제멋대로라니요. 이래 봬도 나라 일은 여러 가지로 생각하고 있다고요.<end>"

## 630331
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これは失礼、ティアナ王女。<line-break>さすがは、ファーロスの…<end>"
EN: "My apologies, Princess Tiana. Just what one would expect of Pharos's...<end>"
KO: "이거 실례했습니다, 티아나 왕녀님. 역시 파로스의….<end>"

## 630332
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええ、ファーロスの雌狐の娘です！<end>"
EN: "Yes, I am the daughter of the Vixen of Pharos!<end>"
KO: "네, 파로스의 암여우 딸이에요!<end>"

## 630333
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺はいつかエリスの専政を打ち破る。<end>"
EN: "One day, I will overthrow Eris's autocratic rule.<end>"
KO: "언젠가 에리스의 전제를 무너뜨리겠다.<end>"

## 630334
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そして広場の千年樹に記されたとおり<line-break>貴族共和の政治を復活させる。<end>"
EN: "Then, as inscribed on the Thousand-Year Tree in the square, I will restore aristocratic republican government.<end>"
KO: "그리고 광장의 천년수에 기록된 대로 귀족 공화 정치를 부활시킨다.<end>"

## 630335
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "悲しいですわ。<line-break>ティアナはそのとき…　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>お母様と一緒に<line-break>ファーロスの一族として、<line-break>粛清されてしまうのですね…。<end>"
EN: "How sad. So when that day comes... Tiana and Mother will be purged as members of House Pharos...<end>"
KO: "슬프군요. 티아나는 그때… 어머니와 함께 파로스 일족으로서 숙청당하고 마는 거군요….<end>"

## 630336
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "心配するな。幼なじみのよしみで<line-break>特別に大きな墓を建ててやる。<end>"
EN: "Don't worry. Since we're childhood friends, I'll have an especially large grave built for you.<end>"
KO: "걱정 마. 소꿉친구 정으로 특별히 큰 무덤을 만들어주지.<end>"

## 630337
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう、レムオン様！<end>"
EN: "Oh, Lemghon!<end>"
KO: "정말, 레무온 님!<end>"

## 630338
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "冗談だ。王家と排除するべき敵の<line-break>区別は付いている。<line-break>ティアナをどうこうするつもりはない。<end>"
EN: "I'm joking. I know the difference between the royal house and the enemies that must be eliminated. I have no intention of harming Tiana.<end>"
KO: "농담이다. 왕가와 제거해야 할 적 정도는 구분하고 있어. 티아나에게 무슨 짓을 할 생각은 없다.<end>"

## 630339
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふ、大事な幼なじみですものね。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. I am your dear childhood friend, after all.<end>"
KO: "후후, 소중한 소꿉친구잖아요.<end>"

## 630340
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだな。<line-break>大事な幼なじみ…だからな。<end>"
EN: "Yes. You're a dear childhood friend... that's why.<end>"
KO: "그래. 소중한 소꿉친구…니까.<end>"

## 630341
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おっと、そういえば侍従長殿から<line-break>伝言があったのを忘れていた。<line-break>　<line-break>いつまでたっても王女が<line-break>来られないから呼んでくるようにと<line-break>言われていたのだ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, I nearly forgot. The chief chamberlain had a message for me. The princess was taking so long that I was told to come fetch you.<end>"
KO: "아, 그러고 보니 시종장님께 전언이 있었던 걸 잊고 있었군. 아무리 기다려도 공주님이 오시지 않으니 모셔 오라고 하셨다.<end>"

## 630342
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ！　うっかりしてましたわ。<line-break>うふふ。もうカンカンに<line-break>怒っているでしょうね。<end>"
EN: "Oh! I'd completely forgotten. Hee hee. The chief chamberlain must be furious by now.<end>"
KO: "아! 깜빡했네요. 후후. 벌써 잔뜩 화가 나 있겠죠.<end>"

## 630343
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ。それはもう。<line-break>はやく行かれた方がよいだろうな。<line-break>　<line-break>…長居した。<line-break>今日はこれで失礼する。<end>"
EN: "Oh, very much so. You should hurry. ...I've lingered too long. I'll take my leave for today.<end>"
KO: "그래. 그럼 서둘러 가는 편이 좋겠군. …오래 머물렀어. 오늘은 이만 실례하지.<end>"

## 630344
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "レムオン様。<line-break>どうぞ、またいらしてください。<end>"
EN: "Lemghon, please do come again.<end>"
KO: "레무온 님. 꼭 다시 와 주세요.<end>"

## 630345
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様、<line-break>もう出てきてもよろしいですよ。<end>"
EN: "You may come out now, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님, 이제 나오셔도 됩니다.<end>"

## 630346
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "申しわけありません。<line-break>お聞きのように私はこれから<line-break>侍従長に会いにいかねばなりません。<line-break>せっかく待っていただいたのに…。<line-break>また、いらしてくださるって<line-break>約束していただけますか？<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry. As you heard, I must go and see the chief chamberlain now. And after you were kind enough to wait for me... Will you promise to come again?<end>"
KO: "죄송합니다. 들으신 대로 저는 이제 시종장님을 만나러 가야 합니다. 모처럼 기다려 주셨는데…. 다시 와 주겠다고 약속해 주시겠어요?<end>"

## 630347
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうございます。<line-break>必ず、いらしてくださいね。<line-break>お待ちしてますから。<end>"
EN: "Thank you. Please, you must come again. I'll be waiting for you.<end>"
KO: "감사합니다. 꼭 와 주세요. 기다리고 있을게요.<end>"

## 630348
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうですよね。<line-break>もう、わがままは言いません。<line-break>気をつけてお帰りください。<end>"
EN: "...I understand. I won't be selfish anymore. Please take care on your way home.<end>"
KO: "…그렇네요. 이제 억지는 부리지 않을게요. 조심해서 돌아가세요.<end>"

## 630349
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また、いらしてくださいね。<line-break><value:$28>様とは<line-break>ゆっくりとお話ししたいですから。<end>"
EN: "Please come again, <value:$28>. I'd like to have time for a proper talk with you.<end>"
KO: "또 와 주세요. <value:$28>님과는 천천히 이야기하고 싶으니까요.<end>"

## 630350
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの…私、お待ちしていますから。<line-break>もし気が向くことがあれば、<line-break>またお越しください。<end>"
EN: "Um... I'll be waiting. If you ever feel like coming back, please do.<end>"
KO: "저… 기다리고 있을게요. 혹시 마음이 내키시면 다시 와 주세요.<end>"

## 630351
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それでは、<value:$28>様。<line-break>ごきげんよう。<end>"
EN: "Well then, <value:$28>. Good day.<end>"
KO: "그럼 <value:$28>님. 안녕히 계세요.<end>"

## 630359
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "きっと尊重すると心から思っている<end>"
EN: "I truly believe you would.<end>"
KO: "분명 존중할 거라고 진심으로 생각해.<end>"

## 630360
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんなこと考えられない<end>"
EN: "That never crossed my mind.<end>"
KO: "그런 건 생각할 수 없어.<end>"

## 630361
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ、クズどもが<line-break>コソコソ隠れやがって…。<line-break>さては、陰で俺を笑ってるんだな！<end>"
EN: "Damn you, hiding away like that... You're laughing at me behind my back, aren't you?!<end>"
KO: "큭, 쓰레기들이 숨어서 꼼지락거리기는…. 설마 뒤에서 날 비웃고 있는 거냐!<end>"

## 630362
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くそっ！　どいつもこいつも<line-break>俺のことバカにしやがって！<line-break>なめやがって！　なめやがってエ！<end>"
EN: "Damn you! Every last one of you is making a fool of me! How dare you! How dare you!<end>"
KO: "젠장! 이놈이고 저놈이고 전부 날 바보 취급해! 얕보지 마! 얕보지 말라고오!<end>"

## 630363
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（あの子に人形を返さなくては。）<end>"
EN: "(I have to return the doll to that girl.)<end>"
KO: "(그 아이에게 인형을 돌려줘야 해.)<end>"

## 630364
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "約束する<end>"
EN: "I promise<end>"
KO: "약속한다<end>"

## 630365
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんなこと約束できない<end>"
EN: "I can't promise that<end>"
KO: "그런 약속은 할 수 없어.<end>"

## 630366
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………。<line-break>今日はお引き取り願えませんか？<end>"

KO: "………. 오늘은 돌아가 주시겠습니까?<end>"

## 640001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まあ、<value:$28>様、　　　<line-break>よくいらしてくださいました。　　<line-break>　<line-break>ひとりで退屈していたところです。<line-break>ティアナのおしゃべりに<line-break>つき合っていただこうかしら。<end>"
EN: "Why, <value:$28>! How lovely of you to come. I was growing bored all by myself. Perhaps you could stay and keep Tiana company for a while.<end>"
KO: "어머, <value:$28> 님, 잘 오셨어요. 혼자 심심하던 참이었어요. 티아나의 수다에 어울려 주실래요?<end>"

## 640002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お待ちください！<line-break>勝手に入られては困ります！<end>"
EN: "Please wait! You cannot simply barge in!<end>"
KO: "잠깐만요! 마음대로 들어오시면 곤란합니다!<end>"

## 640003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "貴様、メイドの分際で指図する気か！<line-break>この俺がティアナに会いに来たのだ。<line-break>引っ込んでろ！<end>"
EN: "You dare order me about, you mere maid? I have come to see Tiana. Stand aside!<end>"
KO: "네놈, 하녀 주제에 내게 명령할 셈이냐! 이 내가 티아나를 만나러 왔다. 비켜!<end>"

## 640004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大変、タルテュバ様だわ！<end>"
EN: "Oh no, it's Lord Tartuba!<end>"
KO: "큰일이야, 타르튜바 님이야!<end>"

## 640005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様、<line-break>早く隠れてください！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, you must hide, quickly!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님, 빨리 숨으세요!<end>"

## 640006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今、王女様にお取り次ぎしますから<line-break>お待ちください。<end>"
EN: "Please wait here while I inform the princess of your arrival.<end>"
KO: "지금 왕녀님께 말씀드릴 테니 기다려 주세요.<end>"

## 640007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そこをどけ！<line-break>これ以上逆らうと死刑だぞ！<end>"
EN: "Out of my way! Defy me again and I'll have you executed!<end>"
KO: "비켜라! 더 거역하면 사형이다!<end>"

## 640008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、まぁ、タルテュバ様。<line-break>ごきげんよう。<end>"
EN: "O-oh, Lord Tartuba. Good day.<end>"
KO: "어, 어머, 타르튜바 님. 안녕하세요.<end>"

## 640009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そろそろ俺に会いたくなるころだと<line-break>思ったんだが…<line-break>違うかい？<end>"
EN: "I thought you must be longing to see me by now. Was I wrong?<end>"
KO: "슬슬 나를 보고 싶어질 때라고 생각했는데… 아닌가?<end>"

## 640010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え、ええ。<line-break>おっしゃるとおりです。<end>"
EN: "N-no. You were quite right.<end>"
KO: "네, 네. 말씀하신 대로예요.<end>"

## 640011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ククク…。<line-break>なにしろ俺は<line-break>狙った獲物は逃がさないからな。<line-break>俺の趣味を知っているだろ？<end>"
EN: "Heh heh heh... Once I set my sights on prey, I never let it escape. You know my favorite pastime, don't you?<end>"
KO: "크크크…. 난 한번 노린 사냥감은 놓치지 않거든. 내 취미 알잖아?<end>"

## 640012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう、狩猟だ。<line-break>貴族のたしなみだが<line-break>俺の腕はプロ級さ。<line-break>…そういえば、<line-break>あの日の狩りは最高だったなあ。<end>"
EN: "Yes―hunting. It's a nobleman's pastime, but I'm as good as any professional. Ah, that reminds me. The hunt that day was magnificent.<end>"
KO: "그래, 사냥이다. 귀족의 소양이지. 하지만 내 실력은 프로급이야. …그러고 보니 그날 사냥은 최고였지.<end>"

## 640013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "珍しい獲物を追ったのさ。<line-break>知りたいかい？<end>"
EN: "I was after some unusual prey. Would you like to know what?<end>"
KO: "희귀한 사냥감을 쫓았지. 알고 싶어?<end>"

## 640014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まぁ、何かしら？<end>"
EN: "My, what could it have been?<end>"
KO: "어머, 무슨 일이죠?<end>"

## 640015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "クククク…。<line-break>さすがのティアナも<line-break>これだけはわからないだろうな。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh heh heh... Even you will never guess this one, Tiana.<end>"
KO: "크크크크… 아무리 티아나라도 이것만큼은 모르겠지.<end>"

## 640016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人間だ。<line-break>卑しい平民どもだよ。ククク…<end>"
EN: "Humans. Filthy commoners. Heh heh heh...<end>"
KO: "인간이다. 천한 평민들이지. 크크크…<end>"

## 640018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0中年の男に、リルビーの娘…。<line-break>それに、さえない若造もいたっけな。<line-break>とにかく薄汚い奴らだった。<end><if><value:$29><equal>%1中年の男に、リルビーの娘…。<line-break>それに、あか抜けない小娘もいたっけな。<line-break>とにかく薄汚い奴らだった。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0There was a middle-aged man, a Lilubee girl... and some unimpressive young fellow, as I recall. A filthy-looking lot, all of them.<end><if><value:$29><equal>%1There was a middle-aged man, a Lilubee girl... and some unsophisticated little chit, as I recall. A filthy-looking lot, all of them.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0중년 남자에 릴비 여자애…. 게다가 별 볼 일 없는 젊은 녀석도 있었지. 어쨌든 다들 꾀죄죄했어.<end><if><value:$29><equal>%1중년 남자에 릴비 여자애…. 게다가 촌스러운 어린 여자애도 있었지. 어쨌든 다들 꾀죄죄했어.<end>"

## 640019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ところが、俺より先に<line-break>そいつらを狙ってる冒険者がいたのさ。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "But an adventurer had already chosen them as prey before I did.<end>"
KO: "그런데 나보다 먼저 그 녀석들을 노리는 모험자가 있었어.<end>"

## 640020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "自分は伝説の勇者だと<line-break>ほざいてやがった。<end>"
EN: "The fool was spouting some nonsense about being a legendary Hero.<end>"
KO: "자기가 전설의 용자라고 떠들어 댔어.<end>"

## 640021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかも、獲物を倒すどころか　　　<line-break>逆に獲物にやられて<line-break>意識を失ったのさ。<line-break>クズがクズにやられる…傑作だろ？<line-break>俺は名場面に花を添えてやろうと<line-break>草陰から弓を射た。<end>"
EN: "And instead of bringing down the prey, the fool was beaten senseless by it! One piece of filth felled by another―priceless, wouldn't you say? I decided to add a finishing touch to the spectacle, so I loosed an arrow from the grass.<end>"
KO: "게다가 사냥감을 쓰러뜨리기는커녕 오히려 사냥감에게 당해 의식을 잃었지. 쓰레기가 쓰레기에게 당한다… 걸작이지? 난 그 명장면에 꽃을 더해 주려고 풀숲에서 화살을 쐈어.<end>"

## 640022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "見事、命中だ！　　　　　　　　<line-break>しかもシロウラギリの毒が<line-break>たっぷりと塗ってあった。<line-break>薄汚い商人は、無様に倒れたまま<line-break>起きあがることもできない。<end>"
EN: "A perfect hit! And the arrow was generously coated with Shirouragiri poison. The filthy merchant fell in a pathetic heap and could not even get back up.<end>"
KO: "멋지게 명중이다! 게다가 시로우라기리의 독이 잔뜩 발라져 있었지. 지저분한 상인은 꼴사납게 쓰러진 채 일어나지도 못한다.<end>"

## 640023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そしたら、まわりの奴ら<line-break>バカ勇者がやったと思い込んで大騒ぎ。<line-break>バカだろ？　傑作だろ？　クハハハ…<end>"
EN: "Then all the others assumed that idiot Hero had done it and made a tremendous fuss. Stupid, isn't it? Hilarious, isn't it? Khahahaha...<end>"
KO: "그러자 주변 녀석들이 바보 용사가 한 짓이라고 착각해서 난리였지. 바보 같지? 걸작이지? 크하하하….<end>"

## 640025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ティアナ、　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>なぜ俺がその一行を<line-break>獲物に選んだかわかるかい？<line-break>やつら、用心棒を雇っていたんだ。<line-break>役立たずの用心棒の正体は…<line-break>…ゼネテスさ。<end>"
EN: "Tiana... do you know why I chose that particular band as my prey? They had hired a bodyguard. And that useless bodyguard was... Xenetes.<end>"
KO: "…티아나, 내가 왜 그 일행을 사냥감으로 골랐는지 알아? 녀석들, 경호원을 고용했더군. 쓸모없는 경호원의 정체는… 제네테스였어.<end>"

## 640027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ううっ…。<line-break>な、なんだ貴様は！<end>"
EN: "Ugh... Wh-who the devil are you?!<end>"
KO: "으윽…. 뭐, 뭐냐 네놈은!<end>"

## 640028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ひいっ！<line-break>お、俺はタルテュバ様だぞっ！<end>"
EN: "Eek! I-I am Lord Tartuba!<end>"
KO: "히익! 내, 내가 타르튜바 님이시다!<end>"

## 640029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "や、や、やめろっ！<end>"

KO: "그, 그, 그만둬!<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 640033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 640036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…話してくださらなくても　　　　　　<line-break>構いません。<line-break>　<line-break>でも、宮中で私闘を演じては<line-break>罪になります。<line-break>あなたを罪人にはしたくはありません。<end>"
EN: "You need not tell me if you would rather not. But fighting within the palace is a crime. I don't want you to become a criminal.<end>"

## 640037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…お願いです。<line-break>あんな真似は二度となさらないで。<end>"
EN: "Please. Don't ever do anything like that again.<end>"
KO: "…부탁이에요. 그런 짓은 두 번 다시 하지 말아 주세요.<end>"

## 640038

## 640044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml

## 640045

## 640046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アトレイア？<end>"

## 640047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私の父上の兄に当たる<line-break>先王様のご息女、アトレイア王女。<line-break>…目がご不自由なのです。<end>"
EN: "Princess Atleia. She is the daughter of the former king, my father's elder brother. She... cannot see.<end>"
KO: "제 아버님의 형이신 선왕의 따님, 아트레이아 왕녀님입니다. …눈이 보이지 않으십니다.<end>"

## 640048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "先王の娘だと！？<line-break>だからどうした？<line-break>ノコノコとでてきやがって…。<end>"
EN: "The former king's daughter?! So what? You come strolling out here...<end>"
KO: "선왕의 딸이라고!? 그래서 어쨌다는 거냐? 제 발로 나타나다니…<end>"

## 640049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前みたいな女、<line-break>ティアナとは段違いなんだよ。<line-break>部屋に帰って鏡でも見てこい！<end>"
EN: "A woman like you isn't even in Tiana's league. Go back to your room and take a look in the mirror!<end>"
KO: "너 같은 여자는 티아나와는 차원이 달라. 방에 돌아가서 거울이나 보고 와!<end>"

## 640050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よう、タルテュバ。　　　　　　<line-break>頭は冷えたかい。<line-break>　<line-break>のぼせあがって、なに言ってるか<line-break>わかってなかったようだから<line-break>さましてやったのさ。礼はいいぜ。<end>"
EN: "Hey, Tartuba. Cooled off yet? You were so worked up, you didn't seem to know what you were saying, so I cooled you down. No need to thank me.<end>"
KO: "여어, 타르튜바. 머리는 좀 식었어? 흥분해서 무슨 말을 하는지도 모르는 것 같길래 식혀 준 거야. 감사는 됐어.<end>"

## 640051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ゼネテス。<line-break>こ、この…。<line-break>く、くそっ！　覚えていろ！<end>"
EN: "...Xenetes. Y-you... Damn you! You'll pay for this!<end>"
KO: "…제네테스. 이, 이 자식… 제, 젠장! 두고 봐!<end>"

## 640052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "礼はいいって言ってるのに。<line-break>タルテュバも義理堅いことだな。<end>"
EN: "I said there was no need to thank me. How very conscientious of you, Tartuba.<end>"
KO: "감사는 됐다고 했는데. 타르튜바도 의리가 참 깊군.<end>"

## 640053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Princess. Here, take my hand.<end>"
KO: "공주님. 자, 손을.<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml

## 640056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml

KO: "다친 데 없나? 귀여운 공주님.<end>"

## 640057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 640058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ファーロス総司令、<line-break>アトレイア王女まで<line-break>くどいてらっしゃるのですか？<end>"
EN: "Commander-in-Chief Pharos, are you trying to woo Princess Atleia too?<end>"
KO: "파로스 총사령관님, 아트레이아 왕녀님께까지 작업을 거시는 겁니까?<end>"

## 640059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "花を見ればキレイだと思う。　<line-break>かわいい女の子を見れば<line-break>声をかける。<line-break>そして、困っている人がいれば<line-break>手をさしのべる。<line-break>人間の自然な動きじゃないか？<end>"
EN: "When I see a flower, I think it's beautiful. When I see a pretty girl, I talk to her. And when someone is in trouble, I lend a hand. Isn't that only natural?<end>"
KO: "꽃을 보면 예쁘다고 생각한다. 귀여운 여자아이를 보면 말을 건다. 그리고 곤란한 사람이 있으면 손을 내민다. 인간의 자연스러운 행동 아닌가?<end>"

## 640060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "無責任に声をかけないで！<line-break>あなたにとって、なんでもないことでも<line-break>心を乱される人だっているの！<end>"
EN: "Don't flirt with people so carelessly! It may mean nothing to you, but you can still stir someone's heart!<end>"
KO: "무책임하게 말 걸지 마! 당신에겐 아무것도 아닌 일이어도 마음이 흔들리는 사람도 있어!<end>"

## 640061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…わかった、ティアナ。<line-break>箱入り娘のアトレイアには<line-break>ちょっと、失礼があったかもな。<line-break>機会があったら<line-break>謝っておいてくれ。<line-break>じゃな。<end>"
EN: "All right, Tiana. Maybe I was a little forward with a sheltered girl like Atleia. Apologize to her for me if you get the chance. See you.<end>"
KO: "…알겠어, 티아나. 곱게 자란 아트레이아에게는 내가 좀 실례했을지도 모르겠군. 기회가 있으면 대신 사과해 줘. 그럼.<end>"

## 640062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかってない…。<line-break>ぜんぜん…わかってない…。<end>"
EN: "You don't understand... You don't understand at all...<end>"
KO: "모르고 있어…. 전혀… 모르고 있어….<end>"

## 640063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今日は…ありがとうございます。<line-break>タルテュバ様に迫られていて<line-break>どうしようかと思っていました。<line-break>…つかれました。<line-break>申しわけありませんが、<line-break>今日はこれで部屋で休みます。<end>"
EN: "Thank you for your help today. Lord Tartuba was pressuring me, and I didn't know what to do. I'm tired. I'm sorry, but I think I'll go rest in my room now.<end>"
KO: "오늘은… 감사합니다. 타르튜바 님께서 다가오셔서 어쩌나 했어요. …지쳤습니다. 죄송하지만 오늘은 이만 방으로 돌아가 쉬겠습니다.<end>"

## 640064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また、<line-break>いらっしゃったのですね。<end>"
EN: "You've come again.<end>"
KO: "또 오셨군요.<end>"

## 640065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ…、<value:$28>様。<end>"
EN: "Oh... <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "아…, <value:$28> 님.<end>"

## 640066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…私の周りには<line-break>いつも、たくさんの人がいると<line-break>みんな言います。<line-break>ですが、それは<line-break>王女の周りに人がいるだけです。<line-break>私の周りには、誰もいない…。<end>"
EN: "…Everyone says I am always surrounded by people. But they gather around the princess, not me. There is no one by my side…<end>"
KO: "…제 주변에는 언제나 많은 사람이 있다고 다들 말합니다. 하지만 그건 공주 주변에 사람이 있을 뿐이에요. 제 주변에는 아무도 없어…<end>"

## 640067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私が王女でなくても<line-break>私のそばにいてくれる…。<line-break>そんなお方がいるのでしょうか？<line-break>………………。<end>"
EN: "Would anyone stay by my side even if I weren't a princess? ...<end>"
KO: "제가 왕녀가 아니어도 제 곁에 있어 줄 분이 있을까요? ……………….<end>"

## 640068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…いっそ、ロストールなど　　　　　　<line-break>滅んでしまえばいいと<line-break>思うときがあります。<line-break>…こんな状況で<line-break>口にしてよいセリフではありませんね。<line-break>ウソでも…王女なのですから。<end>"
EN: "…Sometimes I find myself wishing Rostorl would simply perish. That is hardly something I should say at a time like this. Even if it is a lie… I am still a princess.<end>"
KO: "…차라리 로스톨 따위 멸망해 버렸으면 좋겠다고 생각할 때가 있어요. …이런 상황에서 입에 담을 말은 아니네요. 거짓이라도… 왕녀니까요.<end>"

## 640069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また…、<line-break>いらしたのですか…。<end>"
EN: "You've come again...?<end>"
KO: "또… 오셨군요….<end>"

## 640070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…私なんかに<line-break>何かご用ですか？<end>"
EN: "Did you need something from... someone like me?<end>"
KO: "…저 같은 사람에게 무슨 볼일이신가요?<end>"

## 640071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私にご用なんて<line-break>ないんでしょう？<line-break>　<line-break>私はしょせん<line-break>お母様の…。<end>"
EN: "You don't need anything from me, do you? After all, I'm only Mother's…<end>"
KO: "저한테 볼일은 없으시겠죠? 저는 어차피 어머님의….<end>"

## 640072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "話しかけるお方が<line-break>違うのではありませんか？<end>"
EN: "Should you not be speaking to someone else?<end>"
KO: "말을 거실 상대를 잘못 고르신 것 아닌가요?<end>"

## 640073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…いっそ　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>ロストールなんて<line-break>滅んでしまえばいい。<line-break>みんな…みんな…、<line-break>何もかも消えてしまえばいい…。<line-break>そうなれば…私…。<end>"
EN: "…Rostorl should simply perish. Everyone… everyone… everything should disappear… If that happened… I…<end>"
KO: "…차라리 로스톨 따위 멸망해 버리면 좋겠어. 모두… 모두…, 전부 사라져 버리면 좋겠어…. 그렇게 되면… 나….<end>"

## 640074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "未使用<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "미사용<end>"

## 640075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この間は…<line-break>申しわけありませんでした。<end>"
EN: "About the other day... I'm very sorry.<end>"
KO: "지난번에는… 죄송했습니다.<end>"

## 640076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よかった…。　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>様に嫌われたらと<line-break>思うと…ティアナは、心が<line-break>しめつけられるようだったのです。<end>"
EN: "What a relief... Just thinking that you might hate me, <value:$28>, made Tiana's heart ache.<end>"
KO: "다행이다…. <value:$28>님께 미움받을까 생각하니 티아나는 가슴이 조여 오는 것 같았어요.<end>"

## 640077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うふふ…。あ、笑っていますが、<line-break>反省していないわけではないのです。<line-break>ティアナは移り気に過ぎるようです。<line-break>あなたの言葉を聞くまでは<line-break>涙すら浮かんでいたこの顔に、<line-break>笑みが自然と浮かび上がってきます。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee... Oh, but just because I'm laughing doesn't mean I'm not sorry. Tiana's moods change far too quickly, it seems. Until I heard what you had to say, there were tears in my eyes. Now I can't help but smile.<end>"
KO: "우후후…. 아, 웃고 있지만 반성하지 않는 건 아니에요. 티아나는 너무 변덕스러운가 봐요. 당신의 말을 듣기 전까지 눈물까지 맺혔던 이 얼굴에 자연스럽게 미소가 떠오르네요.<end>"

## 640078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、どうぞお通りください。　　　　<line-break><value:$28>様。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Oh, please come in, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "아, 지나가십시오. <value:$28> 님.<end>"

## 640079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、<value:$28>様。今日は<line-break>何度もいらしてくださるのですね。<line-break>　<line-break>ティアナは、<line-break>…うれしいです。<line-break>どうぞ、奥に。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>. You've come to see me several times today. Tiana is... very happy. Do come in.<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28>님. 오늘은 여러 번 와 주시는군요. 티아나는… 기뻐요. 안으로 들어오세요.<end>"

## 640080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>様！<line-break>　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>いらしてくださるなら、<line-break>どうして先に、来ると<line-break>おっしゃってくださらないのです？<end>"
EN: "...<value:$28>! If you were coming, why didn't you tell me beforehand?<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28>님! 와 주실 거라면 왜 미리 오신다고 말씀해 주지 않으셨어요?<end>"

## 640081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうしたら、お茶の支度をして<line-break>きれいなドレスに着替えて<line-break>お待ちすることができたのに…。<line-break>あ、すみません。<line-break>どうぞ、お入りになってください。<end>"
EN: "Then I could have prepared tea, changed into a lovely dress, and been waiting for you... Oh, I'm sorry. Please come in.<end>"
KO: "그랬다면 차 준비도 하고 예쁜 드레스로 갈아입고 기다릴 수 있었을 텐데…. 아, 죄송해요. 들어오세요.<end>"

## 640082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さぁ、今日はどんなお話を<line-break>聞かせてくださるのです？<end>"
EN: "Now then, what story will you tell me today?<end>"
KO: "자, 오늘은 어떤 이야기를 들려주실 건가요?<end>"

## 640083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うふふ…、お話もないのに<line-break>会いにきてくださったんですね。<line-break>　<line-break>フフ、いけませんわ。<line-break>ティアナは<line-break>期待してしまいますよ。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee... You came to see me even though you had no story to tell. Oh, you shouldn't do that. Tiana might start getting her hopes up.<end>"
KO: "후후… 할 이야기도 없는데 만나러 와 주셨군요. 후후, 그러시면 안 돼요. 티아나는 기대하게 된답니다.<end>"

## 640084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様が<line-break>ティアナのことを気にかけて　　<line-break>くださっているって…。<end>"
EN: "That you really do care about Tiana, <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 님이 티아나를 신경 써 주고 계시다고….<end>"

## 640085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう帰ってしまうのですか？　　　<end>"
EN: "Are you leaving already?<end>"
KO: "벌써 돌아가시는 건가요?<end>"

## 640086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また来てくださいね。<line-break>ティアナは、ずっと待っています。<end>"
EN: "Please come again. Tiana will be waiting for you.<end>"
KO: "또 와 주세요. 티아나는 계속 기다리고 있을게요.<end>"

## 640089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おかしいですね。<line-break>私…いつもはおしゃべりなのに。　　<line-break>　<line-break>今日は、なんだか胸がいっぱいに…。<line-break><value:$28>様のお顔を見たら<line-break>なんだか…。<end>"
EN: "How strange. I'm usually so talkative, but today... I'm simply overcome. And when I saw you, <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "이상하네요. 저는… 평소엔 수다스러운데. 오늘은 왠지 가슴이 벅차서…. <value:$28>님의 얼굴을 보니 왠지….<end>"

## 640090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今日は…お帰りくださいませんか？<end>"
EN: "Could you... go home for today?<end>"
KO: "오늘은… 돌아가 주시지 않겠어요?<end>"

## 640091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "甘えだということはわかっています。<line-break>今度は…笑顔でお迎えいたします。<end>"
EN: "I know I'm being selfish. Next time... I'll greet you with a smile.<end>"
KO: "응석이라는 건 알고 있습니다. 다음에는… 웃는 얼굴로 맞이하겠습니다.<end>"

## 640092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…すみません。<end>"
EN: "...I'm sorry.<end>"
KO: "…죄송합니다.<end>"

## 640093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "先ほど、レムオン様が<line-break>ここへお見えになりました。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>レムオン様ったら<line-break>なんて言ったと思います？<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Lemghon came to see me a little while ago. What do you suppose he said?<end>"
KO: "아까 레무온 님이 여기 오셨어요. 레무온 님이 뭐라고 했는지 아세요?<end>"

## 640094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>は純情だから、<line-break>あんまり色目を使うなですって。<line-break>くすっ。<line-break>あのレムオン様が<line-break>そんなに弟思いだなんて<line-break>知りませんでした。ふふふ。<end>"
EN: "He said, \"<value:$28> is innocent, so don't flirt too much.\" Hee hee. I never knew Lemghon was so protective of his little brother. Hee hee.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>는 순진하니까 너무 추파를 던지지 말래요. 후훗. 그 레무온 님이 그렇게 동생을 아끼는 줄은 몰랐네요. 후후.<end>"

## 640095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ごめんなさい。<line-break>お部屋がお酒臭くありませんか？　　　<line-break>　<line-break>先ほど、あの人が来たのです。<line-break>ヒゲも剃らずに、だらしない服装で<line-break>…どういうおつもりかしら。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry. Does the room smell of alcohol? That person was here earlier, unshaven and dressed like a slob... What was the idea, turning up like that?<end>"
KO: "미안해요. 방에서 술 냄새가 나지 않나요? 아까 그 사람이 왔어요. 수염도 깎지 않고 흐트러진 옷차림으로… 대체 무슨 생각인지.<end>"

## 640096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もちろん、すぐお帰りいただきました！<line-break>婚約者だからって、<line-break>居座られたら困りますもの。<end>"
EN: "Of course I sent my betrothed away at once! An engagement is no license to linger.<end>"
KO: "물론 바로 돌아가시게 했어요! 약혼자라고 눌러앉으면 곤란하잖아요.<end>"

## 640097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え？　もう、お帰りになるのですか？<line-break>ティアナのお相手は退屈ですか？<end>"
EN: "What? You're leaving already? Is Tiana such dull company?<end>"
KO: "네? 벌써 돌아가시려고요? 티아나와 함께 있는 게 지루하신가요?<end>"

## 640098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様に嫌われるなんて<line-break>考えただけで、胸が苦しくなります。<line-break>　<line-break>だから、ティアナは<line-break>わがままは申しません。<line-break>…どうか、お気をつけて。<end>"

## 640100

## 640104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうしたら、あなたのために<line-break>きれいなドレスを用意して<line-break>お待ちすることができたのに…。　<line-break>あ、すみません。<line-break>どうぞ、お入りになってください。<end>"
EN: "Then I could have had a lovely dress ready for your visit and been waiting for you... Oh, I'm sorry. Please come in.<end>"
KO: "그랬다면 당신을 위해 예쁜 드레스를 준비하고 기다릴 수 있었을 텐데…. 아, 죄송해요. 들어오세요.<end>"

## 640105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私、<value:$28>様と<line-break>お話しているときが一番楽しいのです。<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>様が<line-break>私のお姉様だったら、<line-break>どんなにいいかしら。<end>"
EN: "I'm happiest when I'm talking with you, <value:$28>. If only <value:$28> were my older sister.<end>"
KO: "저는 <value:$28>님과 이야기할 때가 제일 즐거워요. <value:$28>님이 제 언니라면 얼마나 좋을까요.<end>"

## 640106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様が王女なら、<line-break>この王宮も変えられるような<line-break>気がします。<end>"
EN: "If you were a princess, <value:$28>, I think you could change even this palace.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 님이 왕녀가 되신다면 이 왕궁도 바꿀 수 있을 것 같아요.<end>"

## 640107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですね。<line-break>ここは、まるで宝石でできたオリ…。<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>様にとって<line-break>ここは狭いし、<line-break>きっと退屈すぎるわ…。<end>"
EN: "Yes. This place is like a cage made of jewels... It must feel cramped to you, <value:$28>, and far too dull.<end>"
KO: "그렇군요. 이곳은 마치 보석으로 만든 새장…. <value:$28>님께는 너무 좁고 분명 지루한 곳일 거예요….<end>"

## 640108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう帰ってしまうのですか？　　　<end>"
EN: "Are you leaving already?<end>"
KO: "벌써 돌아가시는 건가요?<end>"

## 640109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また来てくださいね。<line-break>ティアナは、ずっと待っています。<end>"
EN: "Please come again. Tiana will be waiting for you.<end>"
KO: "또 와 주세요. 티아나는 계속 기다리고 있을게요.<end>"

## 640112
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おかしいですね。<line-break>私…いつもはおしゃべりなのに。　　<line-break>　<line-break>今日は、なんだか胸がいっぱいに…。<line-break><value:$28>様のお顔を見たら<line-break>なんだか…。<end>"
EN: "How strange. I'm usually so talkative, but today... I'm simply overcome. And when I saw you, <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "이상하네요. 저는… 평소엔 수다스러운데. 오늘은 왠지 가슴이 벅차서…. <value:$28>님의 얼굴을 보니 왠지….<end>"

## 640113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今日は…お帰りくださいませんか？<end>"
EN: "Could you... go home for today?<end>"
KO: "오늘은… 돌아가 주시지 않겠어요?<end>"

## 640114
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "甘えだということはわかっています。<line-break>今度は…笑顔でお迎えいたします。<end>"
EN: "I know I'm being selfish. Next time... I'll greet you with a smile.<end>"
KO: "응석이라는 건 알고 있습니다. 다음에는… 웃는 얼굴로 맞이하겠습니다.<end>"

## 640115
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…すみません。<end>"
EN: "...I'm sorry.<end>"
KO: "…죄송합니다.<end>"

## 640116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "先ほど、レムオン様が<line-break>いらっしゃったのです。　　<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>様のことを<line-break>本当に得意げに<line-break>お話しされてゆかれました。<end>"
EN: "Lemghon was here earlier. He spoke so proudly of you, <value:$28>, before he left.<end>"
KO: "방금 레무온 님께서 다녀가셨습니다. <value:$28>님 이야기를 정말 자랑스럽게 하고 가셨어요.<end>"

## 640117
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "レムオン様は<line-break>とても妹思いなのですね。　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Lemghon is very devoted to his younger sister, isn't he?<end>"
KO: "레무온 님은 정말 여동생을 아끼시는군요.<end>"

## 640119
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え、もう帰ってしまうの？<line-break>お忙しいから仕方ないですね。<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>様、<line-break>また、お顔を見せてくださいね。<line-break>楽しみにしています。<end>"
EN: "Oh, you're leaving already? Well, I know you're busy. Please come and see me again, <value:$28>. I'll look forward to it.<end>"
KO: "네, 벌써 돌아가시나요? 바쁘시니 어쩔 수 없네요. <value:$28>님, 또 얼굴 보여 주세요. 기대하고 있을게요.<end>"

## 640120
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ごめんなさい。<line-break>このお部屋、お酒臭いでしょう？　　<line-break>　<line-break>先ほど、あの人が来たのです…。<line-break>ヒゲも剃らずに、だらしない服装で。<line-break>どういうおつもりかしら。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry. This room smells of alcohol, doesn't it? That person was here earlier... unshaven and dressed like a slob. What was the idea, turning up like that?<end>"
KO: "미안해요. 이 방, 술 냄새 나죠? 아까 그 사람이 왔어요…. 수염도 깎지 않고 흐트러진 옷차림으로. 대체 무슨 생각인지.<end>"

## 640121
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もちろん、すぐに<line-break>お帰りいただいたわ！　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>婚約者だからって、<line-break>居座られたら困りますもの。<line-break>本当に嫌になってしまう…。<end>"
EN: "Of course I sent my betrothed away at once! An engagement is no license to linger. I am truly sick of it all...<end>"
KO: "물론 바로 돌아가시게 했죠! 약혼자라고 눌러앉으면 곤란하잖아요. 정말 싫어져 버려….<end>"

## 640122
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え？　もう、お帰りですの？<line-break>そんな…。<line-break>　<line-break>せっかく来ていただいたのに<line-break>変な話をしてごめんなさい。<line-break>また、いらしてくださいね。<end>"
EN: "What? You're leaving already? Oh, no... I'm sorry I spoke of such odd things when you were kind enough to visit. Please come again.<end>"
KO: "네? 벌써 돌아가시나요? 그런…. 모처럼 와 주셨는데 이상한 이야기를 해서 죄송해요. 또 와 주세요.<end>"

## 640123
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "慈愛の神ライラネートよ。　　　<end>"
EN: "O Lailanate, deity of compassion.<end>"
KO: "자애의 신 라이라네트여.<end>"

## 640124
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様の旅が<line-break>つらいものになりませんように。<end>"
EN: "May no hardship befall <value:$28> on the journey.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님의 여행이 힘든 길이 되지 않기를.<end>"

## 640125
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "再び、無事に私の所へ<line-break>戻って来られますように。　　　<end>"
EN: "And may a safe return to me be granted once more.<end>"
KO: "다시 무사히 제 곁으로 돌아오시기를….<end>"

## 640126
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…あ、<value:$28>様、<line-break>この前は申しわけありませんでした。<end>"
EN: "...Oh, <value:$28>, I'm sorry about the other day.<end>"
KO: "…아, <value:$28>님. 지난번엔 죄송했습니다.<end>"

## 640127
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よかった…！　<value:$28>様に<line-break>許していただけて…。　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>私、誰かに嫌われているということが<line-break>耐えられないのです…。<line-break>それがあなたなら、なおさらです。<end>"
EN: "What a relief...! I'm so glad you've forgiven me, <value:$28>. I cannot bear to be disliked by anyone... least of all by you.<end>"
KO: "다행이에요…! <value:$28> 님께 용서받아서…. 저는 누군가가 저를 싫어한다는 걸 견딜 수 없어요…. 그게 당신이라면 더더욱요.<end>"

## 640128
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これで、胸のつかえが取れました。　<line-break>さ、どうぞ、<value:$28>様。<line-break>お通りください。<end>"
EN: "That's a weight off my mind. Now, please come in, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "이제 마음의 응어리가 풀렸습니다. 자, <value:$28>님. 지나가십시오.<end>"

## 640129
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、<value:$28>様。<line-break>また来てくださったんですね。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>. You've come back to see me.<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28>님. 또 와 주셨군요.<end>"

## 640130
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、<value:$28>様、<line-break>どうぞ、こちらへ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>. This way, please.<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28>님, 이쪽으로 오세요.<end>"

## 640131
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様！<line-break>ようこそ、いらっしゃいました。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>! Welcome. I'm so glad you've come.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님! 어서 오십시오.<end>"

## 640132
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…以前にくらべて、ずっと<line-break>たくましくなられましたね。<end>"
EN: "...You've grown much stronger than you were before.<end>"
KO: "…예전보다 훨씬 듬직해지셨네요.<end>"

## 640133
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティアナの目は確かですわ。<end>"
EN: "Tiana has a keen eye.<end>"
KO: "티아나의 안목은 확실해요.<end>"

## 640134
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "以前は、冒険者と言うより<line-break>神官の子という感じでした。<end>"

KO: "예전에는 모험자라기보다 신관의 자식 같은 느낌이었어요.<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティアナの目は確かですわ。<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ただ、今でも、どこか雰囲気が<line-break>地上の者ではないような感じがします。<end>"
EN: "Even now, though, there is still something about you that seems not of the surface world.<end>"
KO: "…다만 지금도 어딘가 분위기가 지상의 존재가 아닌 듯한 느낌이 듭니다.<end>"

## 640159
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティアナの目は確かですわ。<end>"
EN: "Tiana has a discerning eye.<end>"
KO: "티아나의 안목은 확실해요.<end>"

## 640160
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "以前は、冒険者と言うより<line-break>流浪の旅人という感じでした。<end>"
EN: "Back then, you seemed more like a wandering traveler than an adventurer.<end>"
KO: "예전엔 모험자라기보다 떠도는 여행자 같은 느낌이었어요.<end>"

## 640161
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふふ…、ごめんなさい。<line-break>本気になさらないで。　　　　<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>様といると、<line-break>とても自由な気持ちに<line-break>なってしまうのです。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee... Forgive me. Please don't take it seriously. When I'm with you, <value:$28>, I feel so free.<end>"
KO: "후후후… 미안해요. 진지하게 받아들이지 마세요. <value:$28>님과 있으면 정말 자유로운 기분이 들어 버려서요.<end>"

## 640162
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それで、つい冗談を。　　　　<line-break><value:$28>様って<line-break>不思議な方ですね。<end>"
EN: "So I couldn't resist a little joke. You really are a most unusual person, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그래서 그만 농담을 해버렸어요. <value:$28> 님은 참 신기한 분이네요.<end>"

## 640163
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、もうお帰りなのですか？<end>"
EN: "Oh, are you leaving already?<end>"
KO: "아, 벌써 돌아가시나요?<end>"

## 640164
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "残念です。<line-break>また、いらしてくださいね。<end>"
EN: "What a pity. Please visit me again.<end>"
KO: "아쉽네요. 다음에 또 와 주세요.<end>"

## 640165
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様は<line-break>私のことなどお忘れかと思いましたわ。<line-break>　<line-break>宮廷の噂話には<line-break>飽き飽きしていたところです。<line-break>冒険のお話を聞かせてください。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, I thought you might have forgotten all about me. I was growing thoroughly tired of court gossip. Tell me about your adventures.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님께서 저 같은 건 잊으신 줄 알았어요. 궁정의 소문 이야기는 이제 질렸답니다. 모험 이야기를 들려주세요.<end>"

## 640166
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "自由って素敵ですね。<line-break>私も世界を旅してみたい…。<end>"
EN: "Freedom is a wonderful thing. I would love to travel the world myself...<end>"
KO: "자유란 멋지네요. 저도 세계를 여행해 보고 싶어요….<end>"

## 640167
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、もうお帰りなのですか？<end>"
EN: "Oh, are you leaving already?<end>"
KO: "아, 벌써 돌아가시나요?<end>"

## 640168
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "残念です。<line-break>また、いらしてくださいね。<end>"
EN: "What a pity. Please visit me again.<end>"

## 640169
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様は、レムオン様と<line-break>似ていらっしゃらないのですね。　　<end>"
EN: "You and Lemghon aren't much alike, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님은 레무온 님과 닮지 않으셨군요.<end>"

## 640170
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、その方がよろしかったですわ。<line-break>レムオン様みたいに口が悪くては<line-break>敵ばかり作ってしまう…。<line-break>ふふふ。レムオン様には<line-break>今の話は内緒ですよ。<end>"
EN: "But that's for the best. If you were as sharp-tongued as Lemghon, you'd make nothing but enemies... Hee hee. Don't tell Lemghon I said that.<end>"
KO: "그래도 그편이 나았어요. 레무온 님처럼 입이 험하면 적만 만들게 되니까…. 후후, 레무온 님께는 방금 이야기는 비밀이에요.<end>"

## 640171
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様。<line-break>…今日はおひとりですのね。　　　<line-break>安心しました。<line-break>いえ、お酒とバクチに明け暮れた<line-break>あの野蛮な人とご一緒かと思って。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>. You're alone today... What a relief. Oh, only because I thought you might be with that brute who lives for drink and gambling.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 님. …오늘은 혼자시군요. 안심했습니다. 아니, 술과 도박에 빠진 그 야만적인 사람과 함께일까 봐요.<end>"

## 640172
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいのです。あの人の<line-break>肩を持つ必要はありませんわ。　　<line-break>　<line-break>だいたい<line-break>私、あの人を婚約者だなんて<line-break>認めておりません。<end>"
EN: "There's no need to defend my so-called betrothed. I have never accepted the engagement in the first place.<end>"
KO: "괜찮아요. 그 사람 편을 들어 줄 필요는 없어요. 애초에 전 그 사람을 약혼자라고 인정하지도 않아요.<end>"

## 640173
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お母様が勝手に決めたことです。<line-break>…お母様にとって、ティアナは<line-break>勢力拡大のための道具にすぎない。　<line-break>私の幸せなんて<line-break>取るに足らないことなのです。<line-break>だから、あんな男と…。<end>"
EN: "Mother decided it all on her own. To Mother, Tiana is nothing more than a tool for extending her influence. My happiness is of no consequence. That is why she would betroth me to a man like that...<end>"
KO: "어머님이 멋대로 정하신 일이에요. …어머님께 티아나는 세력을 넓히기 위한 도구일 뿐이에요. 제 행복 따윈 아무래도 좋은 거죠. 그래서 그런 남자와….<end>"

## 640174
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ごめんなさい。<line-break>みっともない話を聞かせてしまって。<line-break>　<line-break>でも…王宮なんて、そんな所です。<line-break>外見は立派でも、中身は…。<end>"
EN: "...I'm sorry. I shouldn't have made you listen to such an undignified tale. But... that is what a royal palace is like. Grand on the outside, but inside...<end>"
KO: "…미안해요. 보기 흉한 이야기를 들려드렸네요. 하지만… 왕궁이란 그런 곳이에요. 겉은 훌륭해도 속은….<end>"

## 640175
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "外の様子はどうです？<line-break>変わりはありませんか？　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>貴族は、この国のことをもっと<line-break>知るべきです。ですが、<line-break>あまり知ろうとしません。<end>"
EN: "How are things outside? Has anything changed? The nobility should know more about this country, but few make any effort to learn.<end>"
KO: "밖은 어떻습니까? 달라진 건 없나요? 귀족은 이 나라를 더 알아야 합니다. 하지만 알려고 하지 않죠.<end>"

## 640176
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "実は、私も<line-break>この城を出たことがありません。　<line-break>そして、これからも…。<end>"
EN: "The truth is, I have never once left this castle. And I never will...<end>"
KO: "사실 저도 이 성을 나가 본 적이 없습니다. 그리고 앞으로도….<end>"

## 640177

## 640179
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "旅はどうですか？<line-break>何か、めずらしいことに出会いまして？<end>"
EN: "How are your travels? Have you come across anything unusual?<end>"
KO: "여행은 어때요? 뭔가 신기한 일을 만났나요?<end>"

## 640180
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私も、<value:$28>様のように<line-break>自由に生きてみたい…。<end>"
EN: "I wish I could live as freely as you do, <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "저도 <value:$28>님처럼 자유롭게 살아 보고 싶어요….<end>"

## 640181

## 640183
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何かお飲物をおもちしましょうか？<end>"

## 640184
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "相変わらず、<line-break>お忙しいのですね。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>でも、安心しました。<line-break>とてもお元気そうで。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "You're as busy as ever, I see. Still, I'm relieved. You look very well.<end>"
KO: "여전히 바쁘시군요. 그래도 안심했어요. 아주 건강해 보이셔서요.<end>"

## 640185
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あまり、お引き留めしては<line-break>いけませんね。<line-break>では、お気をつけて。<end>"
EN: "I mustn't keep you too long. Take care, then.<end>"
KO: "너무 붙잡아 두면 안 되겠네요. 그럼 조심히 다녀오세요.<end>"

## 640186
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "レムオン様は、<line-break>ご一緒ではないのですね？　　　<line-break>　<line-break>レムオン様は、いつも<line-break>あのような調子だから<line-break>誤解されやすいのですが、<end>"
EN: "Lemghon isn't with you, then? He is so often misunderstood because of the way he behaves, but...<end>"
KO: "레무온 님은 함께 계시지 않군요? 레무온 님은 늘 그런 식이라 오해받기 쉽지만,<end>"

## 640187
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "本当はやさしいお方なのです。<line-break>ただ、それを表現するのが<line-break>ヘタなのですわ。<end>"
EN: "The truth is, he's a kind man. He's simply very bad at showing it.<end>"
KO: "사실은 다정한 분이에요. 다만 그걸 표현하는 게서투르실 뿐이죠.<end>"

## 640188
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0ごめんなさい。実の弟でいらっしゃる<line-break><value:$28>様に向かって。<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>様は、<line-break>私などより、ずっと<line-break>レムオン様をご存じなはずですね。<end>ごめんなさい。実の妹でいらっしゃる<line-break><value:$28>様に向かって。<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>様は<line-break>私などよりずっと、<line-break>レムオン様をご存じなはずですね。<end>"

You must know Lemghon far better than I do, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 640189
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの方は、今日もどこかで、<line-break>飲んでおいでですの？<end>"
EN: "Is a certain someone out drinking somewhere again today?<end>"
KO: "그분은 오늘도 어딘가에서 술을 드시고 계신가요?<end>"

## 640190
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "決まっています。<line-break>ファーロス総司令のことですわ。<line-break>　<line-break>仮にも私の婚約者でありながら<line-break>めったに王宮に顔を<line-break>出さないんですもの。<end>"
EN: "Commander-in-Chief Pharos, of course. For someone who is supposed to be my betrothed, Pharos hardly ever comes to the palace.<end>"
KO: "정해져 있죠. 파로스 총사령관 이야기입니다. 명색이 제 약혼자이면서 왕궁에는 좀처럼 얼굴도 비치지 않으니까요.<end>"

## 640191
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もちろん、<line-break>あの方の妻になる気など<line-break>少しもありませんけど。　<line-break>ああ…、考えただけで<line-break>気分が悪くなってきたわ。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Of course, I have no intention whatsoever of becoming Pharos's wife. Oh... I feel ill just thinking about it.<end>"
KO: "물론 그분의 아내가 될 생각은 조금도 없지만요. 아아… 생각만 해도 속이 안 좋아지네요.<end>"

## 640192
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ごめんなさい。<line-break>せっかく<value:$28>様が<line-break>来てくださったというのに…。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry. You were kind enough to come and see me, <value:$28>, and yet...<end>"
KO: "죄송해요. 모처럼 <value:$28> 님이 와 주셨는데….<end>"

## 640193
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "外の様子はどうです？<line-break>変わりはありませんか？　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>貴族は、この国のことをもっと<line-break>知るべきです。ですが、<line-break>あまり知ろうとしません。<end>"
EN: "How are things outside? Has anything changed? The nobility should know more about this country, but few make any effort to learn.<end>"
KO: "밖은 어떻습니까? 달라진 건 없나요? 귀족은 이 나라를 더 알아야 합니다. 하지만 알려고 하지 않죠.<end>"

## 640194
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "実は、私も<line-break>この城を出たことがありません。　<line-break>そして、これからも…。<end>"
EN: "The truth is, I have never once left this castle. And I never will...<end>"
KO: "사실 저도 이 성을 나가 본 적이 없습니다. 그리고 앞으로도….<end>"

## 640195
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ…、もうお帰りなのですか？<line-break>また、いらしてくださいね。<end>"
EN: "Oh... you're leaving already? Please come again.<end>"
KO: "아… 벌써 돌아가시나요? 또 와 주세요.<end>"

## 640197
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何度言わせるの？<line-break>頭が痛くて<line-break>とてもお食事をする気分じゃないわ。<end>"
EN: "How many times must I say it? I have a headache, and I simply don't feel up to dining.<end>"
KO: "몇 번을 말해야 해? 머리가 아파서 도저히 식사할 기분이 아니야.<end>"

## 640198
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ですが、今日は七竜家とのご会食。<line-break>すでに皆様おそろいです。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "But today's dinner is with the Seven Dragon Houses. Everyone is already assembled.<end>"
KO: "하지만 오늘은 칠룡가와의 회식이 있습니다. 이미 모두 와 계십니다.<end>"

## 640199
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "気分がすぐれないのよ。<line-break>お母様にそう伝えてちょうだい。<end>"
EN: "I'm not feeling well. Please tell Mother that.<end>"
KO: "기분이 좋지 않아. 어머니께 그렇게 전해 줘.<end>"

## 640200
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そのようにお伝えしましたが、<line-break>これも王女としての義務だと<line-break>王妃様はおっしゃっておられました。<end>"
EN: "I did, but Her Majesty said this too is part of your duty as a princess.<end>"
KO: "그렇게 전해 드렸지만 왕비님께서는 이것도 왕녀로서의 의무라고 하셨습니다.<end>"

## 640201
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…わかりました。<end>"
EN: "...Very well.<end>"
KO: "…알겠습니다.<end>"

## 640202
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "先に始めていただいて。<line-break>すぐに行きますから。<end>"
EN: "Please ask them to begin without me. I'll join them shortly.<end>"
KO: "먼저 시작하고 계세요. 곧 갈 테니까요.<end>"

## 640203
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、着替えのお手伝いを…。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Then allow me to help you change...<end>"
KO: "그럼 갈아입는 걸 도와드리겠습니다….<end>"

## 640204
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そのぐらい、ひとりでできます。<end>"
EN: "I can manage that much on my own.<end>"
KO: "그 정도는 혼자 할 수 있습니다.<end>"

## 640205
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの…、王妃様が<line-break>お仕立てになられたばかりの<line-break>水色のドレスをお召しになるようにと。<end>"
EN: "Um... Her Majesty said you were to wear the new light-blue dress she had made for you.<end>"
KO: "저… 왕비님께서 막 새로 지으신 하늘색 드레스를 입으시라고 하셨습니다.<end>"

## 640206
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様…<line-break>いつからそこに？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... How long have you been there?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님… 언제부터 거기에?<end>"

## 640207
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これから会食のお約束があるのです。<line-break>お話なら次の機会に…。<end>"
EN: "I have a dinner engagement shortly. If you wish to speak with me, it will have to wait until another time.<end>"
KO: "이제부터 회식 약속이 있습니다. 이야기는 다음 기회에…<end>"

## 640208
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "王女の義務って何？<line-break>私に王家の血が流れていなければ<line-break>自由になれるの？<line-break>…私が本当にお父様の娘かどうかなんて<line-break>誰にもわからないのに…。<end>"
EN: "What does duty as a princess even mean? If royal blood didn't run in my veins, would I be free? No one even knows whether I truly am Father's daughter...<end>"
KO: "왕녀의 의무가 뭐지? 내게 왕가의 피가 흐르지 않는다면 자유로워질 수 있는 거야? …내가 정말 아버지의 딸인지 아무도 모르는데….<end>"

## 640209
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここからはロストールの街が見える。<line-break>でも、私にとってあの自由な街は<line-break>壁にかかった絵と同じね。<end>"
EN: "You can see the city of Rostorl from here. But to me, that city of freedom is no different from a painting on the wall.<end>"
KO: "여기서는 로스톨 시내가 보여. 하지만 내게 저 자유로운 거리는 벽에 걸린 그림과 똑같아.<end>"

## 640211
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もしもあの街の小さな家に<line-break>生まれていたら…。<end>"
EN: "If I'd been born in a little house in that town...<end>"
KO: "만약 그 마을의 작은 집에서 태어났다면….<end>"

## 640212
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…なぜなのです？<end>"
EN: "...Why?<end>"
KO: "…어째서인가요?<end>"

## 640213
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なぜ、この間、<line-break>私ではなく、アトレイア様を<line-break>かばわれたのですか？<end>"
EN: "Why did you shield Lady Atleia instead of me the other day?<end>"
KO: "왜 지난번에는 저 대신 아트레이아 님을 감싸신 건가요?<end>"

## 640214
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "彼女が先王の娘だから？<line-break>彼女は正真正銘、<line-break>先王の娘だから？<end>"
EN: "Because she's the former king's daughter? Because she truly is his daughter?<end>"
KO: "그녀가 선왕의 딸이라서요? 그녀가 틀림없는 선왕의 딸이라서요?<end>"

## 640215
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…フフ、ごめんなさい。<line-break>軽蔑しましたか？<line-break>もう、言いません…。<end>"
EN: "Heh... I'm sorry. Have you lost all respect for me? I won't say any more.<end>"
KO: "…후후, 미안해요. 경멸하셨나요? 이제 말하지 않을게요….<end>"
