## 640222
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様…、<line-break>ティアナを外へ連れ出して<line-break>いただけませんか？<line-break>直接お会いして、<line-break>おわびをしたいのです。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, would you please take Tiana outside? I want to meet the girl in person and apologize.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님… 티아나를 밖으로 데리고 나가 주시겠어요? 직접 만나 사과하고 싶어요.<end>"

## 640223
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それが、王女であり<line-break>第一王位継承権を持つ者の<line-break>務めだと思うのです。<end>"
EN: "I believe that is my duty as a princess and first in line to the throne.<end>"
KO: "그것이 왕녀이자제1왕위 계승권을 가진 자의의무라고 생각합니다.<end>"

## 640224
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…やっぱり、お見通しなのですね。<end>"
EN: "You saw right through me after all.<end>"
KO: "…역시 다 알고 계시는군요.<end>"

## 640225
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうです。<line-break>本当は、一時でいいから<line-break>ここを逃げ出したかったのです。<line-break>でも、その子におわびをしたいのも、<line-break>人々の暮らしを見たいのも本当です！<end>"
EN: "Yes. The truth is, I wanted to escape this place, if only for a little while. But I truly do want to apologize to that girl, and to see how the people live!<end>"
KO: "…맞아요. 사실 잠깐이라도 여기서 도망치고 싶었어요. 하지만 그 아이에게 사과하고 싶은 것도, 사람들의 생활을 보고 싶은 것도 진심이에요!<end>"

## 640226
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お願いです、<value:$28>様。<line-break>どうかティアナを<line-break>街へ連れ出してください。<end>"
EN: "Please, <value:$28>. Take Tiana into town.<end>"
KO: "부탁드립니다, <value:$28> 님. 부디 티아나를 거리로 데려가 주세요.<end>"

## 640227
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "本当ですか！<end>"
EN: "Really?<end>"
KO: "정말입니까!<end>"

## 640228
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もちろん<line-break>危険は覚悟のうえです。<end>"
EN: "Of course. I am prepared for the danger.<end>"
KO: "물론 위험은 각오하고 있습니다.<end>"

## 640229
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あー！<line-break><value:$28>お兄ちゃんだ。<end>"
EN: "Oh! It's big brother <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "아! <value:$28> 오빠다.<end>"

## 640230
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この人、<line-break>お兄ちゃんのカノジョ？<end>"
EN: "Is this your girlfriend?<end>"
KO: "이 사람, 오빠 여자친구야?<end>"

## 640231
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふっ。そうよ。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. That's right.<end>"
KO: "후훗. 그래.<end>"

## 640232
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふーん。<line-break>おにあいのカノジョじゃない。<end>"
EN: "Hmm. She makes a good girlfriend for you.<end>"
KO: "흐응. 잘 어울리는 여자친구잖아.<end>"

## 640233
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええ、知ってるわ。<line-break>古いけど、とってもかわいらしい<line-break>お人形よね？<end>"
EN: "Yes, I know. It is old, but very sweet, is it not?<end>"
KO: "네, 알아요. 낡았지만 아주 귀여운 인형이죠?<end>"

## 640234
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんで知ってるの？<end>"
EN: "How do you know?<end>"
KO: "어떻게 알고 있어?<end>"

## 640235
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "実はね、タルテュバ様は…<end>"
EN: "Well, the truth is, Lord Tartuba...<end>"
KO: "사실은 말이야, 타르튜바 님은…<end>"

## 640236
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そっか、お兄ちゃんが話したんだね。<end>"
EN: "Oh, I get it. Big brother told you.<end>"
KO: "그렇구나, 오빠가 말했구나.<end>"

## 640237
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そっか、<value:$28><line-break>お姉ちゃんが話したんだね。<end>"
EN: "Oh, I get it. Big sister <value:$28> told you.<end>"
KO: "그렇구나, <value:$28>. 언니가 이야기해 줬구나.<end>"

## 640238
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お姉ちゃんも<line-break>タルテュバにいじわるされたら<line-break><value:$28>に助けてもらいなよ。<end>"
EN: "If Tartuba is ever mean to you too, ask <value:$28> to save you.<end>"
KO: "언니도 타르튜바한테 괴롭힘당하면 <value:$28>한테 도와달라고 해.<end>"

## 640239
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えっ、ええ…<end>"
EN: "Oh... yes.<end>"
KO: "어, 어어…<end>"

## 640240
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあね。<line-break>…お兄ちゃん、うまくやるんだよ。<end>"
EN: "Bye! And big brother... don't mess this up.<end>"
KO: "그럼 안녕. …오빠, 잘해 봐.<end>"

## 640241
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあね。<line-break>…今度はカレシをつれてきてよね。<end>"
EN: "Bye! Bring your boyfriend next time, all right?<end>"
KO: "그럼 안녕. …다음엔 남자친구를 데려와 줘.<end>"

## 640242
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くすっ、<line-break>元気でいい子だわ。<line-break>　<line-break>でもどうしてなのです？<line-break>私、あの子に謝りたかったのに。<line-break>本当のことを話すべきだと…<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. What a lively, sweet girl. But why? I wanted to apologize to her. I thought I should tell her the truth...<end>"
KO: "후훗, 활기차고 착한 아이네요. 그런데 왜 그런 건가요? 전 그 아이에게 사과하고 싶었는데. 사실을 말해야 한다고….<end>"

## 640243
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "先日のお人形のことです。<line-break>タルテュバ様がお人形を<line-break>取り上げてしまった女の子に…。<line-break>それに民の暮らしを<line-break>見ておくことも大切でしょう？<end>"
EN: "I mean the girl whose doll Lord Tartuba took the other day. Besides, it is important that I see how the people live, is it not?<end>"
KO: "얼마 전 인형 이야기예요. 타르튜바 님이 인형을 빼앗아 버린 그 여자아이에게…. 그리고 백성들의 생활을 보는 것도 중요하잖아요?<end>"

## 640244
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふーん、それにしては<line-break>おしとやかだね。<end>"
EN: "Hmm. You're awfully ladylike for her friend.<end>"
KO: "흐응, 그래도 꽤 얌전하네.<end>"

## 640245
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あのね、<line-break><value:$28>お兄ちゃんは<line-break>すっごいんだよ。　　　　　　　　　<line-break>あたしのお人形、<line-break>タルテュバに取られちゃったんだけど<line-break>お兄ちゃんが取りかえしてくれたの。<end>"
EN: "You know, big brother <value:$28> is amazing! Tartuba took my doll, but he got it back for me.<end>"
KO: "있지, <value:$28> 오빠는 정말 대단해. 내 인형을 타르튜바가 빼앗아 갔는데 오빠가 되찾아 줬어.<end>"

## 640246
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ひどい傷だ。<line-break>チダフ、しっかりおしよ。<end>"
EN: "That's a terrible wound. Hang in there, Chidaf.<end>"
KO: "…상처가 심해. 치다프, 정신 차려.<end>"

## 640247
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ひどい…。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>誰なの？<line-break>誰がこんなひどいことを！<end>"
EN: "How awful... Who did this? Who could do such a terrible thing?<end>"
KO: "끔찍해…. 누가? 누가 이런 짓을 한 거야!<end>"

## 640248
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "貴族だよ、決まってんだろ！<line-break>スラムの子供の命なんて、<line-break>奴らにはなんの価値もないのさ。<end>"
EN: "A noble, obviously! To them, a child's life in the Slums is worth nothing.<end>"
KO: "귀족이지, 당연하잖아! 빈민가 아이 목숨 따위는 그놈들한테 아무 가치도 없어.<end>"

## 640249
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お医者様はどうしたの？<line-break>早くお医者様を！<end>"
EN: "Where is the doctor? Fetch one at once!<end>"
KO: "의사 선생님은? 빨리 의사를 불러!<end>"

## 640250
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんた、傷を見て<line-break>おかしくなっちまったのかい？<line-break>　<line-break>医者を呼ぶ金が<line-break>どこにあるっていうんだい。<end>"
EN: "Did the sight of that wound knock the sense out of you? Where do you think we'd get the money for a doctor?<end>"
KO: "너, 상처를 보고 이상해진 거야? 의사를 부를 돈이 어디 있다고 그래.<end>"

## 640251
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんな難しいこっちゃないが<line-break>この子が痛みに<line-break>耐えられるかどうか…。<end>"
EN: "It's nothing too difficult. The question is whether the kid can stand the pain.<end>"
KO: "그렇게 어려운 건 아니지만 이 아이가 고통을 견딜 수 있을지….<end>"

## 640252
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "イヤだ！　痛いよ！<line-break>かあさ…ん、助けて…。<end>"
EN: "No! It hurts! Mom... help me...<end>"
KO: "싫어! 아파! 엄마… 도와줘….<end>"

## 640253
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どこ行ったんだ、<line-break>こいつの母ちゃんは？<end>"
EN: "Where's this kid's mother gone?<end>"
KO: "이 녀석 엄마는 어디 간 거야?<end>"

## 640254
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それが、リベルダムへ買い出しに<line-break>出かけたって話で…。<end>"
EN: "We heard she went to Liberdam for supplies...<end>"
KO: "그게, 리벨덤에 장을 보러 나갔다고 해서….<end>"

## 640255
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "答えなさい、ゼネテス！<line-break>ここで何をしているの！？<end>"
EN: "Answer me, Xenetes! What are you doing here?<end>"
KO: "대답해, 제네테스! 여기서 뭐 하고 있는 거야!?<end>"

## 640256
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいえ、その前にお医者様だわ！<line-break>今すぐ城に戻って…<end>"
EN: "No, first we need a doctor! I will return to the castle at once and...<end>"
KO: "아니, 그 전에 의사부터 불러야 해요! 당장 성으로 돌아가서….<end>"

## 640257
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "バカ！　金切り声を出すな。<line-break>チダフが怯えちまう。<end>"
EN: "Idiot! Stop shrieking. You're frightening Chidaf.<end>"
KO: "바보! 꽥꽥 소리 지르지 마. 치다프가 겁먹잖아.<end>"

## 640258
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…この子の手を握ってやってくれ。<line-break>あんたのやさしい声で<line-break>励ましてやるんだ。<end>"
EN: "Hold the kid's hand. Encourage him in that gentle voice of yours.<end>"
KO: "…이 아이의 손을 잡아 줘. 당신의 다정한 목소리로 격려해 줘.<end>"

## 640259
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この子のために…頼む。<end>"
EN: "For the kid's sake... please.<end>"
KO: "이 아이를 위해서… 부탁한다.<end>"

## 640261
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あー！<line-break><value:$28>お姉ちゃんだ。<end>"
EN: "Oh! It's big sister <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "아! <value:$28> 언니다.<end>"

## 640262
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この人、お姉ちゃんのお友だち？<end>"
EN: "Is this lady your friend?<end>"
KO: "이 사람, 언니 친구야?<end>"

## 640263
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうよ。お友だち。<end>"
EN: "That's right. I'm her friend.<end>"
KO: "그래. 친구야.<end>"

## 640264
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あのね、<line-break><value:$28>お姉ちゃんは<line-break>すっごいんだよ。　　　　　　　　　<line-break>あたしのお人形<line-break>タルテュバに取られちゃったんだけど<line-break>取りかえしてくれたの。<end>"
EN: "You know, big sister <value:$28> is amazing! Tartuba took my doll, but she got it back for me.<end>"
KO: "있잖아, <value:$28> 언니는 정말 대단해. 내 인형을 타르튜바한테 빼앗겼는데 되찾아 줬어.<end>"

## 640265
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいか、チダフ。<line-break>ちょっと痛むが、少しの辛抱だ。<line-break>男だったらガマンしろ。<end>"
EN: "Listen, Chidaf. This is going to hurt a little, but it won't last long. Be a man and bear it.<end>"
KO: "잘 들어, 치다프. 조금 아프겠지만 잠깐만 참아. 남자라면 견뎌.<end>"

## 640266
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それにしても大したもんだぜ。<line-break>よくあの痛みに耐えたもんだ。<end>"
EN: "Still, that was impressive. The kid took that pain like a champion.<end>"
KO: "그건 그렇고 대단하군. 그 고통을 잘도 견뎠어.<end>"

## 640267
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "きれいなお姉さんが<line-break>励ましてくれたおかげかな。<line-break>あいつ、ガキのくせに面食いなんだ。<end>"
EN: "Maybe it was having a pretty lady there to cheer him on. That brat already has an eye for pretty faces.<end>"
KO: "예쁜 누나가 격려해 준 덕분인가 봐. 그 녀석, 꼬맹이면서 얼굴 엄청 밝힌다니까.<end>"

## 640268
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…自己満足です。<end>"
EN: "It was only for your own satisfaction.<end>"
KO: "…자기만족입니다.<end>"

## 640269
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたは英雄を<line-break>気取っているだけです。<end>"
EN: "You are only playing the hero.<end>"
KO: "당신은 영웅인 척하고 있을 뿐입니다.<end>"

## 640270
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あそこにはあそこの<line-break>やり方ってもんがある。<line-break>　<line-break>貴族の医者を呼んで<line-break>一流の治療をしてやったところで<line-break>彼らのプライドを傷つけるだけだ。<end>"
EN: "People there have their own way of doing things. Even if you brought in some noble's physician and gave the boy the finest treatment, you'd only wound their pride.<end>"
KO: "거기엔 거기 나름의 방식이 있어. 귀족 의사를 불러 일류 치료를 해 줘 봐야 그들의 자존심만 상하게 할 뿐이야.<end>"

## 640271
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいえ、そんなことを<line-break>言っているのではありません。　<line-break>　<line-break>スラムのあの方たちの暮らし。<line-break>子供の命をなんとも思わない貴族。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "No, that is not what I mean. The way those people in the Slums live. The nobles who place no value on a child's life...<end>"
KO: "아니요, 그런 말을 하는 게 아닙니다. 슬럼에 사는 사람들의 생활, 아이의 목숨을 아무렇지도 않게 여기는 귀족들….<end>"

## 640272
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その構造を変えない限り、<line-break>同じことが繰り返されるのです。<end>"
EN: "Unless that structure changes, the same thing will happen again.<end>"
KO: "그 구조를 바꾸지 않는 한 같은 일이 반복될 겁니다.<end>"

## 640273
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…なるほど。<line-break>さすがは叔母貴の血を引いてる。<line-break>冷静な分析だ。<line-break>さっきまで取り乱していた人間とは<line-break>思えないね。<end>"
EN: "I see. Your aunt's blood runs true. That's a cool-headed analysis from someone who was beside herself a moment ago.<end>"
KO: "…그렇군. 역시 숙모의 피를 이었어. 냉정한 분석이야. 아까까지 당황하던 사람이라고는 생각되지 않네.<end>"

## 640274
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "確かに、あのときは…。<end>"
EN: "I admit, at the time...<end>"
KO: "확실히, 그때는….<end>"

## 640275
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "仕方ないさ。<line-break>王宮とは別世界だからな。<line-break>ルールも価値観もすべて違う。<line-break>貴族に哀れみを受ける道理はないし、<line-break>あんたが夢みていたように<line-break>自由で気楽な暮らしでもない。<end>"
EN: "It's understandable. That place is another world from the palace. The rules and values are completely different. They have no reason to accept a noble's pity, and life there isn't as free and easy as you imagined.<end>"
KO: "어쩔 수 없어. 왕궁과는 완전히 다른 세계니까. 규칙도 가치관도 전부 달라. 귀족에게 동정받을 이유도 없고, 네가 꿈꾸던 것처럼 자유롭고 편한 생활도 아니야.<end>"

## 640276
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まあ、それがわかったら<line-break>二度とスラムをうろついたり<line-break>しないこった。<line-break>これ以上うろちょろすると<line-break>ボロが出るぜ。<end>"
EN: "Now that you understand, don't go wandering around the Slums again. Keep poking your nose in and you'll give yourself away.<end>"
KO: "그래, 알았으면 두 번 다시 슬럼을 돌아다니지 마. 더 어슬렁거리면 정체가 들통난다.<end>"

## 640277
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "とにかく、今日は助かったぜ。<line-break>んじゃな。<end>"
EN: "Anyway, you were a big help today. See you around.<end>"
KO: "어쨌든 오늘은 도움받았어. 그럼 간다.<end>"

## 640278
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それはいけません<end>"
EN: "I can't do that.<end>"
KO: "그러면 안 됩니다<end>"

## 640279
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかりました<end>"
EN: "All right.<end>"
KO: "알겠습니다<end>"

## 640299
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大変だ！<line-break>チダフが、貴族の馬にはねられた！<end>"
EN: "It's terrible! Chidaf was struck by a noble's horse!<end>"
KO: "큰일이야! 치다프가 귀족의 말에 치였어!<end>"

## 640300
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大丈夫かい？<end>"
EN: "Are you all right?<end>"
KO: "괜찮아?<end>"

## 640301
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誰か、ゼネテスさんを呼んできな！<end>"
EN: "Someone go and fetch Xenetes!<end>"
KO: "누가 제네테스 씨 좀 불러와!<end>"

## 640302
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネ…テス…？<end>"
EN: "Xene...tes...?<end>"
KO: "제네… 테스…?<end>"

## 640303
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテスさん！<end>"
EN: "Xenetes!<end>"
KO: "제네테스 씨!<end>"

## 640304
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うい。どうしたい？<end>"
EN: "Yeah? What is it?<end>"
KO: "응. 어떻게 하고 싶어?<end>"

## 640305
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よ、<value:$28>、<line-break>こんなところでデートか。<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>. On a date in a place like this?<end>"
KO: "여, <value:$28>. 이런 데서 데이트냐.<end>"

## 640306
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これから、お前さんを<line-break>デートに誘おうと思ってたのに、<line-break>ティアナに先を越されちまったか。<end>"
EN: "And here I was just about to ask you on a date. Looks like Tiana beat me to it.<end>"
KO: "이제 너한테 데이트 신청하려던 참이었는데 티아나에게 선수를 빼앗겼군.<end>"

## 640308
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うーい。<end>"
EN: "Yeah, yeah.<end>"
KO: "어이.<end>"

## 640309
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなた！<line-break>ここで何をしているのです？<end>"
EN: "You! What are you doing here?<end>"
KO: "당신! 여기서 뭘 하고 있는 겁니까?<end>"

## 640310
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何って、見てのとおりさ。<line-break>飲んだり、食ったり…<line-break>あんたの前では言えないこともな。<end>"
EN: "What does it look like? Drinking, eating... and a few things I couldn't mention in front of you.<end>"
KO: "뭘 하냐니, 보는 그대로지. 마시고 먹고… 네 앞에서는 말 못 할 것도 하고.<end>"

## 640311
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どんな具合だい？<end>"
EN: "How is the child?<end>"
KO: "상태가 어때?<end>"

## 640312
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こりゃ、荒療治が必要だな。<end>"
EN: "This'll need some rough treatment.<end>"
KO: "이건 좀 거친 치료가 필요하겠군.<end>"

## 640332
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え、ええ…。帰りましょう。<end>"
EN: "Yes... Let's go back.<end>"
KO: "네, 네…. 돌아가죠.<end>"

## 640333
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "隠し通路から出てきたのですもの。<line-break>正面からは帰れません。<end>"
EN: "We came out through a secret passage, so we can't very well leave by the front entrance.<end>"
KO: "비밀 통로로 나왔으니 정면으로 돌아갈 순 없습니다.<end>"

## 640334
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今日は…<line-break>よい勉強になりました。<end>"
EN: "Today was... quite educational.<end>"
KO: "오늘은… 좋은 공부가 됐습니다.<end>"

## 640335
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私には手の届かない<line-break>世界なのですね。<end>"
EN: "So that world is beyond my reach.<end>"
KO: "저로서는 닿을 수 없는 세계군요.<end>"

## 640336
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティアナの言うとおりだと思う<end>"
EN: "I think Tiana is right.<end>"
KO: "티아나 말이 맞다고 생각해<end>"

## 640337
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティアナの言うことは何か違うと思う<end>"
EN: "I think there's something off about what Tiana is saying.<end>"
KO: "티아나 말은 뭔가 아닌 것 같아<end>"

## 640338
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "予備メッセージ３<line-break>ティアナ、ボリューム１その２<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "예비 메시지 3 티아나 볼륨 1 그 2<end>"

## 640339
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec064-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "予備メッセージ４<line-break>ティアナ、ボリューム１その２<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "예비 메시지 4 티아나 볼륨 1 그 2<end>"

## 650001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "？？？<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>なんの音かしら？<end>"
EN: "??? What was that sound?<end>"
KO: "??? 무슨 소리지?<end>"

## 650002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "きゃっ！<end>"
EN: "Ah!<end>"
KO: "꺅!<end>"

## 650003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなた、いったいどなたですか？<line-break>こんなところから入ってくるなんて。<end>"
EN: "Who in the world are you? And coming in through a place like that...<end>"
KO: "당신, 대체 누구죠? 이런 곳으로 들어오다니.<end>"

## 650004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティアナ王女に<line-break>会わせてほしいのですか？<end>"
EN: "You want me to take you to Princess Tiana?<end>"
KO: "티아나 공주님을 만나고 싶으신가요?<end>"

## 650005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何かわけありのようですね。<line-break>護衛を呼ぶのは簡単ですが…。<line-break>　<line-break>いいですよ。<line-break>お話を聞きましょう。<end>"
EN: "There seems to be more to this. I could easily call the guards, but... Very well. I'll hear what you have to say.<end>"
KO: "무슨 사정이 있는 모양이군요. 호위를 부르는 건 간단하지만…. 좋아요. 이야기를 들어 보죠.<end>"

## 650006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あのお人形…、<line-break>そういうことでしたの。<end>"
EN: "That doll... So that's what this was about.<end>"
KO: "그 인형… 그런 사정이었군요.<end>"

## 650007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええ、私がティアナです。<line-break>ごめんなさい、名乗らないで。<line-break>　<line-break>少し待っていてくださいね。<end>"
EN: "Yes, I am Tiana. Forgive me for not introducing myself. Please wait here a moment.<end>"
KO: "네, 제가 티아나예요. 미안해요, 이름은 말하지 말아 주세요. 잠깐 기다려 주세요.<end>"

## 650008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くすっ、そういうことだったのですか。<line-break>タルテュバ様からの贈り物にしては、<line-break>その…趣味がいいと思いました。<end>"
EN: "Oh, so that's what happened. For a gift from Lord Tartuba, I thought it was... surprisingly tasteful.<end>"
KO: "후후, 그런 일이었군요. 타르튜바 님의 선물치고는 그… 취향이 좋다고 생각했어요.<end>"

## 650009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、お返しします。<end>"
EN: "Then I'll give it back to you.<end>"
KO: "그럼 돌려드리겠습니다.<end>"

## 650010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そのお人形…、<line-break>とても大切にされていたようですね。<line-break>本当に申しわけないことをしました。<line-break>ティアナが心から謝っていたと<line-break>女の子に伝えてください。<end>"
EN: "That doll... It seems she treasured it dearly. What I did was truly inexcusable. Please tell the girl that Tiana is deeply sorry.<end>"
KO: "그 인형… 정말 소중히 여기고 있었나 보네요. 정말 미안한 일을 했습니다. 티아나가 진심으로 사과했다고 그 아이에게 전해 주세요.<end>"

## 650011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふふ…。<line-break>そんなに変ですか？ <line-break>　<line-break>一国の王女が見ず知らずの侵入者を<line-break>親しげにおもてなしするのが。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee... Is it really so strange? A princess warmly entertaining a trespasser she's never met, I mean.<end>"
KO: "후후후…. 그렇게 이상한가요? 한 나라의 공주가 처음 보는 침입자를 친근하게 대접하는 게.<end>"

## 650012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうかもしれません。<line-break>…でも、あなたが悪い人には<line-break>とても見えないから…。<line-break>では、お名前だけでも<line-break>うかがいましょうか、<line-break>不法侵入者さん？<end>"
EN: "Perhaps it is. But you simply don't look like a bad person. So, Trespasser, may I at least ask your name?<end>"
KO: "그럴지도 모르겠네요. …하지만 당신이 나쁜 사람으로는 도저히 보이지 않아서…. 그럼 이름이라도 들어 볼까요, 불법 침입자님?<end>"

## 650013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0<value:$28>…様？<line-break>レムオン様の弟君で、先ごろ<line-break>ノーブル伯になられた、あの…？<end><if><value:$29><equal>%1<value:$28>…様？<line-break>レムオン様の妹君で、先ごろ<line-break>ノーブル伯になられた、あの…？<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Lord <value:$28>...? You mean Lemghon's younger brother, the one who recently became Count of Noble?<end><if><value:$29><equal>%1Lady <value:$28>...? You mean Lemghon's younger sister, the one who recently became Countess of Noble?<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0<value:$28>… 님? 레무온 님의 남동생이시고 얼마 전 노블 백작이 되신 그분…?<end><if><value:$29><equal>%1<value:$28>… 님? 레무온 님의 여동생이시고 얼마 전 노블 백작이 되신 그분…?<end>"

## 650014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0それではタルテュバ様の<line-break>従弟にあたられるのですね。<line-break>　<line-break>でも、それならどうして<line-break>そんな所からいらしたのです？<end><if><value:$29><equal>%1それではタルテュバ様の<line-break>従妹にあたられるのですね。<line-break>　<line-break>でも、それならどうして<line-break>そんな所からいらしたのです？<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Then you are Lord Tartuba's cousin. But if so, why did you come in through a place like that?<end><if><value:$29><equal>%1Then you are Lord Tartuba's cousin. But if so, why did you come in through a place like that?<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0그렇다면 타르튜바 님의 사촌 남동생이 되시는군요. 그런데 그렇다면 어째서 그런 곳에서 오신 겁니까?<end><if><value:$29><equal>%1그렇다면 타르튜바 님의 사촌 여동생이 되시는군요. 그런데 그렇다면 어째서 그런 곳에서 오신 겁니까?<end>"

## 650015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふ、確かに王女に会いたいと<line-break>言っても、面識のない門番の方は<line-break>連れてきてくれませんよね。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. I suppose a gatekeeper who doesn't know you would hardly bring you to see the princess simply because you asked.<end>"
KO: "후후, 확실히 왕녀님을 만나고 싶다고 해도 안면도 없는 문지기가 데려다주진 않겠죠.<end>"

## 650016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかりました。<line-break>私から、話を通しておきますわ。<end>"
EN: "Very well. I'll make the necessary arrangements myself.<end>"
KO: "알겠습니다. 제가 이야기를 전해 두겠습니다.<end>"

## 650017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今度からは、堂々と正面から<line-break>お入りくださいね。<line-break>　<line-break>侵入者と間違えられて<line-break>騒ぎにでもなったら、あなたのお兄様に<line-break>迷惑がかかるでしょう？<end>"
EN: "From now on, please come openly through the front entrance. If someone mistook you for an intruder and caused an uproar, it would only make trouble for your brother, wouldn't it?<end>"
KO: "다음부터는 당당하게 정문으로 들어오세요. 침입자로 오해받아 소동이 나면 당신 오라버니께 폐가 되잖아요?<end>"

## 650018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…様。<line-break>素敵なお名前ですね。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... What a lovely name.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…님. 멋진 이름이네요.<end>"

## 650019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティアナ様、よろしいでしょうか？<line-break>陛下がおみえです。<end>"
EN: "Lady Tiana, may I? His Majesty is here.<end>"
KO: "티아나 님, 괜찮으시겠습니까? 폐하께서 오셨습니다.<end>"

## 650020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お父様が？　大変！<end>"
EN: "Father? Oh no!<end>"
KO: "아버님이? 큰일이야!<end>"

## 650021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様、<line-break>お人形のこと、お願いいたします。<line-break>私が謝っていたと伝えてください。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, please see to the doll for me. Tell the girl I said I was sorry.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님, 인형 일은 부탁드릴게요. 제가 미안해했다고 전해 주세요.<end>"

## 650022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "追い返すようで<line-break>申しわけありません。<line-break>では、また…。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry to send you away like this. Until we meet again...<end>"
KO: "쫓아내는 것 같아 죄송합니다. 그럼 다음에 또…<end>"

## 650041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今から王宮のティアナ王女を<line-break>訪ねる。お前も来い。<end>"
EN: "I'm going to visit Princess Tiana at the royal palace. You're coming too.<end>"
KO: "지금부터 왕궁의 티아나 공주를 찾아간다. 너도 와.<end>"

## 650042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0ダメだ。お前は俺の弟なのだ。　　　<line-break>言うことを聞いてもらう。<line-break>　<line-break>それに王女に拝謁しておくのは<line-break>悪くはない。そろそろ貴族として<line-break>社交界にも名を売っておかないとな。<end>ダメだ。お前は俺の妹なのだ。　　　<line-break>言うことを聞いてもらう。<line-break>　<line-break>それに王女に拝謁しておくのは<line-break>悪くはない。そろそろ貴族として<line-break>社交界にも名を売っておかないとな。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0No. You're my younger brother, and you'll do as I say. Besides, an audience with the princess will do you no harm. It's time you started making a name for yourself in society as a noble.<end>No. You're my younger sister, and you'll do as I say. Besides, an audience with the princess will do you no harm. It's time you started making a name for yourself in society as a noble.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0안 돼. 넌 내 남동생이다. 내 말을 따라야 해. 그리고 왕녀를 알현해 두는 것도 나쁘지 않다. 이제 귀족으로서 사교계에도 이름을 알려야 하니까.<end>안 돼. 넌 내 여동생이다. 내 말을 따라야 해. 그리고 왕녀를 알현해 두는 것도 나쁘지 않다. 이제 귀족으로서 사교계에도 이름을 알려야 하니까.<end>"

## 650043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "言葉づかいや身だしなみには　　<line-break>細心の注意を払え。<line-break>　<line-break>相手はお前と同い年の小娘だが、<line-break>宮廷暮らしが長い上に<line-break>あのファーロスの雌狐の娘だ。<end>"
EN: "Take the utmost care with your manners and appearance. She may be a mere girl your age, but she's spent years at court―and she's the daughter of that Vixen of Pharos.<end>"
KO: "말투와 몸가짐에는 각별히 주의해라. 상대는 너와 동갑인 어린 여자지만 궁정 생활이 긴 데다 그 파로스 암여우의 딸이다.<end>"

## 650044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "見破られるなよ、　　　　　　　<line-break><value:$28>。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Don't let her see through you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "들키지 마라, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 650045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "レムオン様、<line-break>ようこそおいでくださいました。<end>"
EN: "Lord Lemghon, welcome.<end>"
KO: "레무온 님, 어서 오십시오.<end>"

## 650046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これはティアナ王女、<line-break>今日は一段と美しい。<end>"
EN: "Princess Tiana. You look lovelier than ever today.<end>"
KO: "이런, 티아나 공주님. 오늘은 한층 더 아름다우시군요.<end>"

## 650048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0弟のことをご存じとは<line-break>お耳が早い。さすがは、<line-break>諜略に長けたエリスの娘だな。<end>妹のことをご存じとは<line-break>お耳が早い。さすがは、<line-break>諜略に長けたエリスの娘だな。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0So you've already heard about my younger brother. You are well informed. Just what I'd expect from the daughter of Eris, a woman skilled in intrigue.<end>So you've already heard about my younger sister. You are well informed. Just what I'd expect from the daughter of Eris, a woman skilled in intrigue.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0동생 일을 벌써 알고 있다니 귀가 빠르군. 역시 책략에 능한 에리스의 딸답다.<end>여동생 일을 벌써 알고 있다니 귀가 빠르군. 역시 책략에 능한 에리스의 딸답다.<end>"

## 650049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、王宮中その噂で持ちきりです。<line-break>ファーロスの雌狐の娘で<line-break>なくても知っていますわ。<end>"
EN: "The whole palace is abuzz with the rumor. I would have heard even if I were not the daughter of the Vixen of Pharos.<end>"
KO: "후후, 왕궁 전체가 그 소문으로 떠들썩해요. 파로스의 암여우 딸이 아니어도 다 안답니다.<end>"

## 650050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0まったく、くだらん噂話しか<line-break>能のない奴らが多すぎる。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>紹介する。我が弟、<line-break>ノーブル伯<value:$28>だ。<end>まったく、くだらん噂話しか<line-break>能のない奴らが多すぎる。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>紹介する。我が妹、<line-break>ノーブル伯<value:$28>だ。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Too many people are good for nothing but idle gossip. Let me introduce my younger brother, <value:$28>, Count of Noble.<end>Too many people are good for nothing but idle gossip. Let me introduce my younger sister, <value:$28>, Count of Noble.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0정말이지 시시한 소문이나 떠들 줄 아는 자들이 너무 많군. 소개하지. 내 동생, 노블 백작 <value:$28>이다.<end>정말이지 시시한 소문이나 떠들 줄 아는 자들이 너무 많군. 소개하지. 내 여동생, 노블 백작 <value:$28>이다.<end>"

## 650051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お目にかかれて光栄です、<line-break>ノーブル伯。<end>"
EN: "It is an honor to meet you, Count of Noble.<end>"
KO: "뵙게 되어 영광입니다, 노블 백작님.<end>"

## 650052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺は他にも用事がある。<line-break>お前はあとは好きにしろ。<end>"
EN: "I have other business to attend to. Do as you please from here.<end>"
KO: "나는 다른 볼일도 있다. 너는 이제 마음대로 해.<end>"

## 650053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティアナ、<line-break>少ししか話せなくて残念だが<line-break>これで失礼する。<end>"
EN: "Tiana, I regret that we had so little time to speak, but I must take my leave.<end>"
KO: "티아나, 조금밖에 이야기하지 못해 아쉽지만 이만 실례하지.<end>"

## 650054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "レムオン様、<line-break>またいらっしゃってください。<end>"
EN: "Lord Lemghon, please come again.<end>"
KO: "레무온 님, 또 와 주세요.<end>"

## 650055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>変な気は起こすなよ。　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>王女にはもう、フィアンセがいる。<line-break>しかも、そいつは王妃エリスの実家<line-break>名家ファーロス家の跡取りだ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, do not get any ideas. The princess is already betrothed―and to the heir of the illustrious House Pharos, Queen Eris's own family.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 이상한 마음 품지 마. 왕녀에게는 이미 약혼자가 있어. 게다가 왕비 에리스의 친정인 명문 파로스가의 후계자야.<end>"

## 650056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フィアンセ殿に怒られなければな。<end>"
EN: "Provided your betrothed does not take offense.<end>"
KO: "약혼자에게 혼나지만 않는다면.<end>"

## 650057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フィアンセだなんて！<line-break>存じません、そんな方！<end>"
EN: "Betrothed? I know of no such person!<end>"
KO: "약혼자라니! 그런 분은 모릅니다!<end>"

## 650058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "貴族としての義務を果たさず、<line-break>酒とバクチにおぼれ、<line-break>宮廷にも近づこうとしない…。<line-break>いくら名家の出でも<line-break>あんな方と結婚なんて<line-break>考えられませんわ。<end>"
EN: "Neglecting every noble duty, drowning in drink and gambling, refusing even to go near the court... No matter how illustrious the lineage, I cannot imagine marrying someone like that.<end>"
KO: "귀족으로서 의무도 다하지 않고 술과 도박에 빠져 궁정에도 가까이하려 하지 않는다니…. 아무리 명문가 출신이어도 그런 분과 결혼이라니 생각할 수 없어요.<end>"

## 650059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フィアンセ…ですか？　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>お母様が決めたことです。<line-break>お母様の実家ファーロス家の跡取りで<line-break>従兄に当たる方ですけど…。<end>"
EN: "My betrothed...? Mother arranged it. The heir to House Pharos―her own family―and my cousin as well...<end>"
KO: "약혼자… 말씀이신가요? 어머니께서 정하신 일입니다. 어머니 친정인 파로스 가문의 후계자이고 제 사촌이 되는 분이지만….<end>"

## 650060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "酒とバクチにおぼれ、<line-break>宮廷にも近づこうとしません。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>そんな方と結婚するだなんて<line-break>とても考えられません。<end>"
EN: "Drowning in drink and gambling, never even going near the court... I simply cannot imagine marrying someone like that.<end>"
KO: "술과 도박에 빠져 궁정에도 가까이하지 않습니다. 그런 분과 결혼하다니 도저히 생각할 수 없어요.<end>"

## 650062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、よく来た。　<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, glad you could come.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 잘 왔다.<end>"

## 650063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今からティアナ王女の所へ行く。<line-break>お前も来い。<end>"
EN: "I'm going to see Princess Tiana now. Come with me.<end>"
KO: "지금부터 티아나 왕녀에게 간다. 너도 와라.<end>"

## 650064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…いやに素直だな。<line-break>どこか身体の具合でも悪いのか？<end>"
EN: "You're unusually cooperative. Are you feeling ill?<end>"
KO: "…이상할 만큼 순순하군. 어디 몸이라도 안 좋은 건가?<end>"

## 650065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フッ、さすがは<line-break>丈夫なだけが取り柄の奴だ。<end>"
EN: "Heh. Sturdy as ever. It is the one thing you have going for you.<end>"
KO: "흥, 역시 튼튼한 것만이 장점인 녀석답군.<end>"

## 650066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほめたつもりだが…。ふふっ。<line-break>不器用な兄を許してやってくれ。<end>"
EN: "I meant it as a compliment. Heh. You must forgive your awkward brother.<end>"
KO: "칭찬한 건데… 후훗. 서투른 형을 용서해줘.<end>"

## 650067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これはレムオン様、<line-break>それに<value:$28>様。<line-break>　<line-break>ようこそ<line-break>お越しくださいました。<end>"
EN: "Why, Lemghon―and <value:$28> as well. Welcome.<end>"
KO: "레무온 님, 그리고 <value:$28>님. 어서 오십시오.<end>"

## 650068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティアナ様にもご機嫌うるわしく。<end>"
EN: "And I trust you are well, Princess Tiana.<end>"
KO: "티아나 님도 평안하시고.<end>"

## 650069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さぁ、ご遠慮なさらずに<line-break>奥へお入りくださいませ。<end>"
EN: "Please, don't hesitate. Come in.<end>"
KO: "자, 사양하지 마시고 안으로 들어오세요.<end>"

## 650070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ずいぶん丁重な<line-break>もてなしようではないか？<end>"
EN: "Quite a gracious welcome, isn't it?<end>"
KO: "꽤나 정중한 대접 아닌가?<end>"

## 650071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お母様ですら一目置く<line-break>レムオン様のおいでですもの。<line-break>当然の応対ですわ。<end>"
EN: "Even Mother holds you in high regard, Lemghon. A proper reception is only natural.<end>"
KO: "어머님조차 인정하시는 레무온 님이 오셨는걸요. 당연한 대접이에요.<end>"

## 650072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "笑える冗談だ。　　　　　　　　　<end>"
EN: "That's quite a joke.<end>"
KO: "웃기는 농담이군.<end>"

## 650073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、どういうものかな？<line-break>俺のような者を部屋に入れるとは。<end>"
EN: "Still, is this wise? Letting a man like me into your room?<end>"
KO: "하지만 어떻게 된 일이지? 나 같은 사람을 방에 들이다니.<end>"

## 650074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フィアンセが聞いたら<line-break>怒るのではないか？　　　　　　　<end>"
EN: "Wouldn't your betrothed be angry to hear of it?<end>"
KO: "약혼자가 들으면 화내지 않을까?<end>"

## 650075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "婚約者といっても、<line-break>お母様が勝手に決めたこと。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>幼なじみのレムオン様とのことを<line-break>とやかく言われる筋合いは<line-break>ありません。<end>"
EN: "The engagement was Mother's decision alone. My betrothed has no right to complain about me and Lemghon. We've known each other since childhood.<end>"
KO: "약혼자라 해도 어머니가 마음대로 정한 일입니다. 소꿉친구인 레무온 님과의 일을 이래라저래라 들을 이유는 없어요.<end>"

## 650076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だいたい、酒とバクチにおぼれて<line-break>宮廷に近寄ろうともしない方を<line-break>ティアナは婚約者とは認めません。<end>"
EN: "Besides, Tiana will not accept as a betrothed someone who wallows in drink and gambling and refuses even to come to court.<end>"
KO: "애초에 술과 도박에 빠져 궁정엔 가까이도 하지 않는 분을 티아나는 약혼자로 인정하지 않습니다.<end>"

## 650078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…それはできんな。<end>"
EN: "That I cannot do.<end>"
KO: "…그건 할 수 없군.<end>"

## 650079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まぁ、意地悪！　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>でも、ふふふ…レムオン様らしいわ。<line-break>迷うそぶりくらい見せてくれても<line-break>バチは当たらなくてよ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, how cruel! Still... heh, that is just like you, Lemghon. You might at least have pretended to consider it. It would hardly have hurt you.<end>"
KO: "정말 심술궂으시네요! 하지만 후후… 레무온 님답네요. 망설이는 척 정도는 해 주셔도 벌 받진 않아요.<end>"

## 650080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何も王が男子でなければならぬ<line-break>理由はない。<line-break>　<line-break>ティアナも、エリスのように<line-break>女王として君臨すれば、<line-break>無理に結婚することもなかろう？<end>"
EN: "There is no reason the monarch must be a man. You could reign as queen, as Eris does, and then you would have no need to force yourself to marry, would you?<end>"
KO: "왕이 반드시 남자여야 할 이유는 없다. 티아나도 에리스처럼 여왕으로 군림하면 억지로 결혼할 필요도 없지 않겠나?<end>"

## 650081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "女が出しゃばるのは<line-break>好きではありません。<line-break>　<line-break>特にお母様みたいに<line-break>出しゃばるのは…。<line-break>それに…。<end>"
EN: "I do not care for women who push themselves to the fore. Especially not the way Mother does... And besides...<end>"
KO: "여자가 나서는 건 좋아하지 않습니다. 특히 어머니처럼 나서는 건…. 그리고….<end>"

## 650082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうする、<value:$28>？<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>お前、いつも<line-break>男なんかには負けないと<line-break>言い張っているではないか。<end>"
EN: "What about you, <value:$28>? You're always insisting you won't lose to any man.<end>"
KO: "어쩔 거냐, <value:$28>? 너는 늘 남자 따위에게는 안 진다고 우기잖아.<end>"

## 650083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すみません。　　　　　　<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry.<end>"
KO: "죄송합니다.<end>"

## 650084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様がどうこうと<line-break>申しあげるつもりはなかったのです。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, I did not mean to imply anything about you.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님에 대해 이러쿵저러쿵 말씀드리려던 건 아니었습니다.<end>"

## 650085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "許してやるのか…。　　<end>"
EN: "So you will let it pass...?<end>"
KO: "용서해 주는 건가….<end>"

## 650086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前、もしかして<line-break>俺以外には寛大なのか？<end>"
EN: "Are you generous with everyone but me?<end>"
KO: "너, 혹시 나 말고 다른 사람한테는 관대한 거냐?<end>"

## 650087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私はファーロス家を発展させるための<line-break>道具ではありませんもの。<end>"
EN: "I am not a tool to be used for the advancement of House Pharos.<end>"
KO: "저는 파로스 가문을 발전시키기 위한 도구가 아니니까요.<end>"

## 650088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やれやれ、エリスも哀れだな。　<end>"
EN: "Well, poor Eris.<end>"
KO: "이런, 에리스도 불쌍하군.<end>"

## 650089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ファーロス家発展のために<line-break>知略の限りを尽くしたところが…<end>"
EN: "She has used every ounce of her ingenuity to advance House Pharos...<end>"
KO: "파로스 가문을 발전시키기 위해 온갖 지략을 다했건만….<end>"

## 650090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "娘のわがままのせいで<line-break>その綿密な計画も水の泡か。　　<end>"
EN: "And now her daughter's selfishness has brought that meticulous plan to nothing.<end>"
KO: "딸의 제멋대로인 행동 때문에 그 치밀한 계획도 물거품인가.<end>"

## 650091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ひどいわ、レムオン様！<line-break>わがままだなんて。　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>これでも、国のことを<line-break>いろいろと考えているつもりです。<end>"
EN: "How cruel, Lemghon! Calling me selfish! I do give a great deal of thought to the country, you know.<end>"
KO: "너무해요, 레무온 님! 제멋대로라니. 이래 봬도 나라 일은 여러모로 생각하고 있어요.<end>"

## 650092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これは失礼、ティアナ王女。<line-break>さすがはファーロスの…<end>"
EN: "My apologies, Princess Tiana. Just what one would expect of Pharos's...<end>"
KO: "이거 실례했습니다, 티아나 왕녀님. 역시 파로스의….<end>"

## 650093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええ、<line-break>ファーロスの雌狐の娘です！<end>"
EN: "Yes, I am the daughter of the Vixen of Pharos!<end>"
KO: "네, 파로스의 암여우 딸이에요!<end>"

## 650094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺はいつかエリスの専政を打ち破る。<end>"
EN: "Someday I will overthrow Eris's autocracy.<end>"
KO: "언젠가 에리스의 전제를 무너뜨리겠다.<end>"

## 650095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そして広場の千年樹に記されたとおり<line-break>貴族共和の政治を復活させる。<end>"
EN: "Then I will restore the aristocratic republic, just as it is written on the Thousand-Year Tree in the square.<end>"
KO: "그리고 광장의 천년수에 기록된 대로 귀족 공화 정치를 부활시킨다.<end>"

## 650096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "悲しいですわ。<line-break>ティアナはそのとき…、　　　<line-break>　<line-break>お母様と一緒に<line-break>ファーロスの一族として、<line-break>粛清されてしまうのですね…。<end>"
EN: "How sad. When that day comes... Tiana will be purged alongside Mother as a member of the Pharos family.<end>"
KO: "슬프군요. 티아나는 그때… 어머니와 함께 파로스 일족으로서 숙청당하고 마는 거군요….<end>"

## 650097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "心配するな。幼なじみのよしみで<line-break>特別に大きな墓を建ててやる。<end>"
EN: "Don't worry. In honor of our childhood friendship, I'll have an especially large tomb built for you.<end>"
KO: "걱정 마. 소꿉친구 정으로 특별히 큰 무덤을 만들어주지.<end>"

## 650098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう、レムオン様！<end>"
EN: "Oh, Lemghon!<end>"
KO: "정말, 레무온 님!<end>"

## 650099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…冗談だ。王家と排除するべき敵の　　<line-break>区別は付いている。<line-break>ティアナをどうこうするつもりはない。<end>"
EN: "A joke. I can distinguish the royal house from the enemies who must be eliminated. I have no intention of harming you, Tiana.<end>"
KO: "…농담이다. 왕가와 제거해야 할 적 정도는 구분하고 있어. 티아나에게 무슨 짓을 할 생각은 없다.<end>"

## 650100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふ、<line-break>大事な幼なじみですものね。<end>"
EN: "Heh. I am your dear childhood friend, after all.<end>"
KO: "후후, 소중한 소꿉친구잖아요.<end>"

## 650101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだな。<line-break>大事な幼なじみ…だからな。<end>"
EN: "Yes. You are a dear childhood friend... after all.<end>"
KO: "그래. 소중한 소꿉친구…니까.<end>"

## 650102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また、<line-break>いらしてくださいますよね。<line-break>レムオン様。<end>"
EN: "You will come again, won't you, Lemghon?<end>"
KO: "또 와 주실 거죠, 레무온 님.<end>"

## 650103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、<line-break>くだらぬ噂の種にならぬ程度にな。<end>"
EN: "Yes. As long as it does not start any foolish rumors.<end>"
KO: "그래, 시시한 소문의 씨앗이 되지 않을 정도로만 해라.<end>"

## 650104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様も。<end>"
EN: "And you too, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님도.<end>"

## 650105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "またお会いできる日を<line-break>楽しみにしています。<end>"
EN: "I look forward to seeing you again.<end>"
KO: "다시 뵐 날을 기대하고 있겠습니다.<end>"

## 650106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺とティアナは幼なじみ。　　　　<line-break>ただ、それだけだ。<line-break>　<line-break>何を言い出すかと思えば…。<line-break>ふん、お前も宮廷のくだらぬ噂話に<line-break>毒されたな。<end>"
EN: "Tiana and I have been friends since childhood. That's all there is to it. So that's what you were getting at... Hmph. You too have been poisoned by the court's ridiculous gossip.<end>"
KO: "나와 티아나는 소꿉친구다. 그뿐이야. 무슨 말을 하나 했더니…. 흥, 너도 궁정의 쓸데없는 소문에 물들었군.<end>"

## 650107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…今日は<line-break>無理に付き合わせて悪かった。　<line-break>これでも感謝しているつもりだ。<line-break>お前なら、<line-break>わかってくれるだろうが…。<end>"
EN: "...Sorry I dragged you along today. I do appreciate it, in my own way. I thought you would understand...<end>"
KO: "…오늘은 억지로 끌고 다녀서 미안했다. 이래 봬도 감사하고 있어. 너라면 알아주겠지만….<end>"

## 650108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ、クズどもが<line-break>コソコソ隠れやがって…。<line-break>さては、陰で俺を笑ってるんだな！<end>"
EN: "Damn those scum, skulking around out of sight... I bet they're laughing at me behind my back!<end>"
KO: "큭, 쓰레기들이 숨어서 꼼지락거리기는…. 설마 뒤에서 날 비웃고 있는 거냐!<end>"

## 650109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くそっ！　どいつもこいつも<line-break>俺のことバカにしやがって！<line-break>なめやがって！　なめやがってエ！<end>"
EN: "Damn it! Every last one of them treats me like a fool! How dare they look down on me! How dare they look down on me!<end>"
KO: "젠장! 이놈이고 저놈이고 전부 날 바보 취급해! 얕보지 마! 얕보지 말라고오!<end>"

## 650118
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ゼネテスさん。すまない。<end>"
EN: "Xenetes... I'm sorry.<end>"
KO: "…제네테스 씨. 미안하다.<end>"

## 650119
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "貴族だろうが国王だろうが、<line-break>人を踏みにじっていいやつなんか<line-break>いやしないんだ。<line-break>そのことだけは覚えておけ。<end>"
EN: "Noble or king, no one has the right to trample on others. Don't ever forget that.<end>"
KO: "귀족이든 국왕이든 사람을 짓밟아도 되는 자는 없어. 그것만은 기억해 둬.<end>"

## 650120
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、タルテュバも<line-break>しばらくはおとなしくしてるだろ。<end>"
EN: "Well, Tartuba should stay quiet for a while.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 타르튜바도 당분간은 얌전히 있겠지.<end>"

## 650121
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうよ、本当に。<line-break>助かったよ。<end>"
EN: "Thank you. I mean it. You really helped me out.<end>"
KO: "정말 고맙다. 덕분에 살았어.<end>"

## 650122
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテスと同じくらいかっこよかったよ。<end>"
EN: "You were every bit as cool as Xenetes.<end>"
KO: "제네테스만큼 멋있었어.<end>"

## 650123
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いつもゼネテスさんに<line-break>助けてもらえるわけじゃない。<line-break>それはわかってるんだが…。<end>"
EN: "Xenetes won't always be around to help. I know that, but...<end>"
KO: "언제나 제네테스 씨가 도와줄 수 있는 건 아니야. 그건 알고 있지만….<end>"

## 650124
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec065-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティアナメッセージVOL2終端<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "티아나 메시지 VOL2 끝<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml

EN: ""
KO: "티아나 메시지 VOL3-1 시작<end>"

## 660002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "閣下の婚約者ティアナ王女のもとへ<line-break>足繁くお通いになるようにと。<end>"
EN: "She wishes you to pay frequent visits to your betrothed, Princess Tiana.<end>"
KO: "각하의 약혼자 티아나 왕녀께 자주 찾아가시라는 말씀이었습니다.<end>"

## 660003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺は婚約なんてしていない。<end>"
EN: "I'm not betrothed to anyone.<end>"
KO: "나는 약혼 같은 건 하지 않았다.<end>"

## 660004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私たちは王妃様の伝言を閣下に伝え、<line-break>その内容を実現させるだけです。<end>"
EN: "Our duty is simply to deliver Her Majesty's message and see that you comply with it.<end>"
KO: "저희는 왕비님의 전언을 각하께 전하고 그 내용을 실현할 뿐입니다.<end>"

## 660005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほお、恐いねえ。<end>"
EN: "Ooh, scary.<end>"
KO: "호오, 무섭군.<end>"

## 660006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "任務ですので。<end>"
EN: "It is our duty.<end>"
KO: "임무니까요.<end>"

## 660007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺も金持ちになったら、<line-break>あんたたちほどの者を雇いたいもんだ。<line-break>　<line-break>あんたたちとやり合っちゃ、<line-break>俺も無傷とはいかないだろうからな。<end>"
EN: "If I ever get rich, I'd like to hire people of your caliber. I doubt I'd come away unscathed if I had to fight you.<end>"
KO: "나도 부자가 되면 당신들만 한 사람을 고용하고 싶군. 당신들과 싸우면 나도 멀쩡하진 못할 테니까.<end>"

## 660008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、閣下。<end>"
EN: "Well then, Your Excellency.<end>"
KO: "그럼, 각하.<end>"

## 660009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "行くべきだろうな。　　　　　　　　<end>"
EN: "I suppose I ought to go.<end>"
KO: "가야겠지.<end>"

## 660010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…だから行く。ただそれだけだ。<line-break>ちょっと負け惜しみっぽいけどな。<end>"
EN: "...That's why I'm going. That's all. Though that does sound a bit like I'm trying to save face.<end>"
KO: "…그래서 간다. 그뿐이야. 조금 오기 부리는 것처럼 들리지만.<end>"

## 660011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0…いい女だねえ。<line-break>お前さんもそう思うだろ？　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>ヴァイライラとヴィアリアリ。<line-break>サファイアとルビーと呼ばれる、<line-break>エリスの配下でもトップの実力だ。<end>…いい女だねえ。もちろん、<line-break>お前さんもいい女だけどな。<line-break>　<line-break>ヴァイライラとヴィアリアリ。<line-break>サファイアとルビーと呼ばれる、<line-break>エリスの配下でもトップの実力だ。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Fine women, eh? You think so too, don't you? Vailaila and Vialiali―the Sapphire and the Ruby. They're among the best Eris has.<end>Fine women, eh? You're a fine woman yourself, of course. Vailaila and Vialiali―the Sapphire and the Ruby. They're among the best Eris has.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0…좋은 여자들이군. 너도 그렇게 생각하지? 바일라이라와 비아리아리. 사파이어와 루비라고 불리는, 에리스의 부하들 중에서도 최고 실력자들이야.<end>…좋은 여자들이군. 물론 너도 좋은 여자고. 바일라이라와 비아리아리. 사파이어와 루비라고 불리는, 에리스의 부하들 중에서도 최고 실력자들이야.<end>"

## 660012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "奴らの強さは知っているだろう？<end>"
EN: "You know how strong they are, don't you?<end>"
KO: "놈들의 강함은 알고 있겠지?<end>"

## 660013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "叔母貴…王妃エリスの配下は<line-break>みんな石の名前で呼ばれている。　　<line-break>　<line-break>お前さんのおやじさん…<line-break>フリントさんのようにな。<end>"
EN: "All the people who serve my aunt, Queen Eris, go by the names of stones. Like your father, Flint.<end>"
KO: "숙모… 에리스 왕비의 부하들은 모두 돌 이름으로 불려. 네 아버지… 플린트 씨처럼 말이지.<end>"

## 660014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "奴らは強い。　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>…そして奴らは<line-break>ひとつの信念のためにしか<line-break>その力を使わない。そこが美しい。<end>"
EN: "They're strong. And they use that strength in service of one conviction alone. That's what makes them beautiful.<end>"
KO: "놈들은 강하다. …그리고 놈들은 오직 하나의 신념을 위해서만 그 힘을 쓴다. 그 점이 아름답지.<end>"

## 660015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、その信念てのが、<line-break>金に汚いツェラシェルってところが<line-break>間違ってる気もするがな。<line-break>んじゃ、姫さんの所に顔出すか。<line-break>悪いが、<value:$28>。<line-break>お前さんもついてきてくれ。<end>"
EN: "Though I do wonder if devoting themselves to the money-grubbing Zeraschel might be the wrong conviction. Well, let's go show our faces at the princess's place. Sorry, <value:$28>, but I'd like you to come along.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 그 신념이라는 게 돈에 더러운 체라셸이라는 점부터 잘못된 것 같기도 하지만. 그럼 공주님한테 얼굴이나 비추러 갈까. 미안하지만 <value:$28>, 너도 따라와 줘.<end>"

## 660016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "王女様、総司令閣下がおみえです。<end>"
EN: "Princess, the Commander-in-Chief is here.<end>"
KO: "왕녀님, 총사령관 각하께서 오셨습니다.<end>"

## 660017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "王女と呼ばないで。<end>"
EN: "Don't call me Princess.<end>"
KO: "왕녀라고 부르지 마.<end>"

## 660018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "も、申しわけありません。<line-break>ティアナ様。<end>"
EN: "M-my apologies, Lady Tiana.<end>"
KO: "죄, 죄송합니다. 티아나 님.<end>"

## 660019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "下がって結構です。<end>"
EN: "You may leave us.<end>"
KO: "물러가셔도 됩니다.<end>"

## 660020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんのご用です？<line-break>ファーロス総司令閣下。<end>"
EN: "What do you want, Commander-in-Chief of Pharos?<end>"
KO: "무슨 용건이십니까? 파로스 총사령관 각하.<end>"

## 660021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "姫さんのご機嫌うかがいにね。<end>"
EN: "Just came to see how the princess was doing.<end>"
KO: "공주님 기분도 살필 겸.<end>"

## 660022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まぁ、おやさしいこと。　　　　<end>"
EN: "How very kind of you.<end>"
KO: "어머, 참 친절하시네요.<end>"

## 660023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、あなたのお酒の臭いで<line-break>さっそく気分が悪くなりました。<end>"
EN: "But the smell of drink on you has already made me ill.<end>"
KO: "하지만 당신에게서 술 냄새가 나서 벌써 속이 안 좋아졌어요.<end>"

## 660024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そりゃまずい。<line-break>介抱しようか？<end>"
EN: "That's no good. Want me to look after you?<end>"
KO: "그건 곤란한데. 간호해 줄까?<end>"

## 660025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "結構です。人形泥棒に<line-break>介抱していただく気はありません。<line-break>　<line-break>それに、あなたがいなくなれば<line-break>すぐに治りますから。<end>"
EN: "No, thank you. I have no intention of being tended to by a doll thief. Besides, I'll recover as soon as you're gone.<end>"
KO: "됐습니다. 인형 도둑에게 간호받을 생각은 없습니다. 그리고 당신이 사라지면 금방 나을 테니까요.<end>"

## 660026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "結構です。あなたがいなくなれば<line-break>すぐに治りますから。<end>"
EN: "No, thank you. I'll recover as soon as you're gone.<end>"
KO: "됐습니다. 당신이 없어지면 금방 낫습니다.<end>"

## 660028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんですって？<end>"
EN: "What did you say?<end>"
KO: "뭐라고요?<end>"

## 660029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "例えば、恋の病とかな。<end>"
EN: "Love sickness, for instance.<end>"
KO: "예를 들면, 사랑병이라든가.<end>"

## 660030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だ、誰が！　あなたなんかに！<end>"
EN: "W-who would ever fall for you?!<end>"
KO: "누, 누가! 당신 따위에게!<end>"

## 660031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "失礼で野蛮で、<line-break>酒とバクチにおぼれた人なんて！<end>"
EN: "You're rude and uncivilized, and you wallow in drink and gambling!<end>"
KO: "무례하고 야만적이고, 술과 도박에 빠진 사람이라니!<end>"

## 660032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "少し手柄をあげたくらいで<line-break>調子に乗らないでください。　<end>"
EN: "Don't get carried away just because you've had a little success.<end>"
KO: "조금 공을 세웠다고 우쭐대지 마세요.<end>"

## 660033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ディンガル軍を破ったのだって<line-break>運がよかっただけなのです。<end>"
EN: "You only defeated the Dyneskal Army because you were lucky.<end>"
KO: "딘갈군을 물리친 것도 운이 좋았을 뿐입니다.<end>"

## 660034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだな…。<line-break>ありゃ、ほんとに運がよかった。<line-break>　<line-break>どう考えても<line-break>アンギルダンのとっつぁんが<line-break>勝つ戦だった。<end>"
EN: "That's true... That really was a stroke of luck. By any reckoning, old Angeerdan should have won that battle.<end>"
KO: "그래…. 그건 정말 운이 좋았어. 어떻게 생각해도 앙길단 아저씨가 이길 전투였지.<end>"

## 660035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あのときばかりは、<line-break>運命の女神の気まぐれに感謝したぜ。<end>"
EN: "For once, I was grateful for a whim of the Goddess of Fate.<end>"
KO: "그때만큼은 운명의 여신 변덕에 감사했지.<end>"

## 660036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストール軍総司令のあなたが<line-break>そんな言い方を…！<end>"
EN: "How can you say that when you're commander-in-chief of the Rostorl Army?!<end>"
KO: "로스톨군 총사령관인 당신이 그런 말을…!<end>"

## 660037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "戦場は地獄になる。　　　<end>"
EN: "A battlefield becomes hell.<end>"
KO: "전장은 지옥이 된다.<end>"

## 660038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "地獄が広がらずにすんだ。<line-break>運がよかったんだ。<end>"
EN: "We were lucky that hell spread no farther.<end>"
KO: "지옥이 퍼지지 않고 끝났어. 운이 좋았던 거야.<end>"

## 660039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………。　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>それで昼間から祝杯をあげて<line-break>いらっしゃったのね。<line-break>結構ですこと。<end>"
EN: "...So that's why you were drinking a victory toast in broad daylight. How admirable.<end>"
KO: "………………. 그래서 대낮부터 축배를 들고 계셨군요. 참 좋으시겠어요.<end>"

## 660040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うんにゃ。いくら俺でも<line-break>そこまでは割り切れねぇな。<line-break>　<line-break>ま、弔い酒だ。<end>"
EN: "No. Even I can't shrug it all off that easily. Call it a drink for the dead.<end>"
KO: "아니. 아무리 나라도 그렇게까지 냉정하게 끊어 내진 못하겠네. 뭐, 추모주다.<end>"

## 660041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "弔い酒？<end>"
EN: "A drink for the dead?<end>"
KO: "추모주?<end>"

## 660042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ディンガル兵にしろ、　　　　　<line-break>親兄弟がいる。<line-break>　<line-break>子供がいる奴もいただろう。<line-break>それを…、<line-break>ずいぶん殺しちまった。<end>"
EN: "The Dyneskal soldiers had parents and siblings too. Some of them must've had children. And I... killed a great many of them.<end>"
KO: "딘갈 병사에게도 부모형제가 있어. 아이가 있는 자도 있었겠지. 그런 사람들을… 꽤 많이 죽였어.<end>"

## 660044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストール兵だって、ほとんどは<line-break>無理やり引っぱり出された農民だ。　<line-break>　<line-break>国や貴族の名誉のために死ぬより<line-break>他にやることがあったろうに。<line-break>親父の指揮の誤りで死なせちまった。<end>"
EN: "Most of Rostorl's soldiers were farmers dragged into the army against their will. They must've had better things to do than die for their country or some noble's honor. My old man's mistakes in command got them killed.<end>"
KO: "로스톨 병사들도 대부분 억지로 끌려나온 농민들이야. 나라나 귀족의 명예를 위해 죽는 것 말고도 할 일이 있었을 텐데. 아버지의 잘못된 지휘로 죽게 만들었어.<end>"

## 660045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ウソばっかり。あなたが<line-break>そんなことで悩むものですか。<end>"
EN: "What a pack of lies. As if something like that would trouble you.<end>"
KO: "거짓말뿐이네요. 당신이 그런 일로 고민할 리가 있나요.<end>"

## 660046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ずいぶん俺のことに詳しいねえ。<line-break>気になって調べさせたのかい？<end>"
EN: "You know an awful lot about me. Were you so curious that you had someone investigate?<end>"
KO: "내 일에 꽤 자세하군. 신경 쓰여서 조사시킨 건가?<end>"

## 660047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誰が、そんなことを！　　　<end>"
EN: "Who would do such a thing?!<end>"
KO: "누가 그런 말을!<end>"

## 660048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "と、とにかく、あなたがいると<line-break>気分が悪くなるんです！<end>"
EN: "A-anyway, being around you makes me ill!<end>"
KO: "아, 아무튼 당신이 있으면 기분이 나빠져요!<end>"

## 660049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そりゃどーも。<line-break>んじゃ、行くか、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Much obliged. Come on, <value:$28>. Let's go.<end>"
KO: "그래, 고맙네. 그럼 가자, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 660050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの、<value:$28>将軍<line-break>でしたね。<line-break>　<line-break>見苦しいところをお目にかけて、<line-break>申しわけありません。<line-break>またいらしてください。<end>"
EN: "Um, General <value:$28>, wasn't it? I apologize for subjecting you to such an unseemly display. Please visit again.<end>"
KO: "저기, <value:$28> 장군님이셨죠. 보기 흉한 모습을 보여 드려 죄송합니다. 또 와 주세요.<end>"

## 660051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>だけかい？<line-break>俺は、来ちゃまずいのかな？<end>"
EN: "Only <value:$28>? Would it be so terrible if I came too?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>만이야? 나는 오면 안 되는 건가?<end>"

## 660052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ご勝手に！<end>"
EN: "Do as you please!<end>"
KO: "마음대로 해!<end>"

## 660053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ん？<line-break>あぁ、さっきの人形云々ね。　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>タルテュバの奴が<line-break>スラムの女の子から人形を取り上げて、<line-break>ティアナにプレゼントしたんだ。<end>"
EN: "Huh? Oh, that business with the doll earlier. Tartuba took it from a girl in the Slums and gave it to Tiana as a present.<end>"
KO: "응? 아, 아까 인형 얘기 말이지. 타르튜바가 슬럼의 여자아이에게서 인형을 빼앗아 티아나에게 선물했어.<end>"

## 660054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "で、その女の子に頼まれて、<line-break>ティアナから人形を取り上げたんだが<line-break>それを根に持ってるんだろ？<line-break>俺がなぜ人形を奪ったか、<line-break>ティアナは知らない。<line-break>別に知らなくてもいいことさ。<end>"
EN: "The girl asked me to take it back from Tiana, so I did. And now that's being held against me, right? Tiana doesn't know why I took the doll, and it's not something she needs to know.<end>"
KO: "그래서 그 여자애 부탁으로 티아나에게서 인형을 빼앗았는데 그걸 아직 원망하는 거겠지? 내가 왜 인형을 빼앗았는지 티아나는 몰라. 굳이 알 필요도 없는 일이야.<end>"

## 660055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティアナは誰にでも優しく、　　　　<line-break>誰にでも気を使う。<line-break>　<line-break>だけど人間ってもんは<line-break>そういう風にできちゃいない。<line-break>だから、どっかでムリがくる…。<end>"
EN: "Tiana is kind to everyone. Always looking out for everyone. But people aren't made to keep that up forever. Sooner or later, something has to give.<end>"
KO: "티아나는 누구에게나 상냥하고 누구에게나 신경을 써. 하지만 인간이란 건 그렇게만 살 수는 없어. 그러니 어디선가 무리가 오지….<end>"

## 660056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "彼女には必要なのさ。<line-break>もっともな理由があって<line-break>文句をぶつけられる人間がな。　　　<line-break>これからも、ときどき顔を<line-break>出してやらなきゃならんな。<end>"
EN: "She needs someone she can rail at with good reason. Looks like I'll have to keep dropping by now and then.<end>"
KO: "그녀에겐 필요해. 그럴 만한 이유가 있어서 불평을 쏟아낼 수 있는 사람이 말이야. 앞으로도 가끔 얼굴을 비춰 줘야겠군.<end>"

## 660057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "からかうとおもしろい姫さんだし<line-break>ティアナの方でも<line-break>俺のことが嫌いじゃないらしいし…。<end>"
EN: "The princess is fun to tease, and it seems Tiana doesn't exactly dislike me, either.<end>"
KO: "놀리면 재미있는 공주님이고 티아나 쪽도 날 싫어하는 건 아닌 것 같고….<end>"

## 660058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うぬぼれてるって？　　　　　　　　<end>"
EN: "You think I'm being conceited?<end>"
KO: "자만하고 있다고?<end>"

## 660059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、俺は人生悲観して生きるより<line-break>期待して生きることにしてるんでな。<end>"
EN: "Heh. I'd rather live in hope than spend my life expecting the worst.<end>"
KO: "후후, 난 인생을 비관하며 사느니 기대하며 살기로 했거든.<end>"

## 660060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあな。<line-break>また会おうぜ、<value:$28>。　<end>"
EN: "See you. Till next time, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그럼 잘 가. 또 만나자, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 660061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Hey, <value:$28>. You wouldn't happen to be free right now, would you?<end>"
KO: "어이, <value:$28>. 지금 혹시 한가해?<end>"

## 660062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ヒマ<end>"
