## 660063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "忙しい<end>"
EN: "I'm busy.<end>"
KO: "바빠<end>"

## 660064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょうどいいや。これから<line-break>ティアナに会いに行こうと<line-break>思ってたんだ。ついてきな。<end>"
EN: "Perfect. I was just about to go see Tiana. Come along.<end>"
KO: "잘됐네. 이제 티아나를 만나러 가려던 참이야. 따라와.<end>"

## 660065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "んじゃ、さっそく<line-break>ティアナ王女姫様に<line-break>謁見とまいりましょうか。<end>"
EN: "Well then, let us proceed at once to an audience with Her Highness Princess Tiana.<end>"
KO: "그럼 바로 티아나 왕녀님께 알현하러 가 볼까.<end>"

## 660066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よぉ。じゃまするぜ。<end>"
EN: "Hey. Pardon the intrusion.<end>"
KO: "여어. 실례 좀 할게.<end>"

## 660067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ…、<line-break>ゼネテス…様…。<end>"
EN: "Oh... Lord Xenetes...<end>"
KO: "아… 제네테스… 님….<end>"

## 660068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ご機嫌うるわしゅう。<line-break>しっかし、世辞抜きで<line-break>最近、ホントきれいだな。<end>"
EN: "A very good day to you. And honestly, no flattery―you've been looking truly beautiful lately.<end>"
KO: "안녕하십니까. 그런데 빈말이 아니라 요즘 정말 예뻐졌군.<end>"

## 660069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの…、あ、い、今<line-break>お茶の用意をさせますわ。<end>"
EN: "Um... I-I'll have tea prepared at once.<end>"
KO: "저… 아, 지, 지금 차를 준비시키겠습니다.<end>"

## 660070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その気持ちだけで十分だ。<line-break>あんた、前は一度だって茶なんて<line-break>出しちゃくれなかったからね。<end>"
EN: "It's enough that you offered. You never once served me tea before, you know.<end>"
KO: "그 마음만으로 충분해. 넌 전에는 한 번도 차 같은 걸 내준 적 없었으니까.<end>"

## 660071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、それは…<line-break>あなたのことを誤解していたから…。<end>"
EN: "Th-that was because... I had misunderstood you.<end>"
KO: "그, 그건… 당신을 오해하고 있었으니까….<end>"

## 660072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "んにゃ、おおかた間違っちゃいない。<line-break>あんたの言うとおり、俺は<line-break>酒飲みでバクチ好きの不作法者さ。<end>"
EN: "Nah, you weren't far wrong. Just as you said, I'm a hard-drinking, gambling lout with no manners.<end>"
KO: "아니, 대충 틀리진 않았어. 네 말대로 난 술꾼에 도박 좋아하는 무례한 녀석이지.<end>"

## 660073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "違います！　ゼネテス様は本当は<line-break>やさしくて、思いやりがあって！<line-break>それに、とってもお強くて…。<end>"
EN: "That's not true! Lord Xenetes, you're really kind and considerate! And so very strong...<end>"
KO: "아니에요! 제네테스 님은 사실 상냥하고 배려심도 많고! 게다가 정말 강하시고….<end>"

## 660074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おいおい、どうしたんだ、<line-break>ティアナ王女様？<line-break>もしや、お熱でも？<end>"
EN: "Now, now, what's come over you, Princess Tiana? You haven't got a fever, have you?<end>"
KO: "어이, 왜 그래, 티아나 왕녀님? 혹시 열이라도 나나?<end>"

## 660075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうですね。<line-break>つまらぬ病に<line-break>かかったのかもしれません。<end>"
EN: "Perhaps I have. I may have caught some foolish illness.<end>"
KO: "…그렇군요. 시시한 병에 걸린 건지도 모르겠습니다.<end>"

## 660076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そっか。大事にしろよ。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>しっかし、ちょっと残念だな。<line-break>俺はやっと姫さんが俺のことを<line-break>理解したと思ったのにな。<end>"
EN: "Oh. Take care of yourself, then. Still, that's a little disappointing. I thought you'd finally come to understand me.<end>"
KO: "그렇구나. 소중히 해. 그래도 조금 아쉽네. 난 드디어 공주님이 날 이해해 줬다고 생각했는데.<end>"

## 660077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…少なくとも<line-break>あなたが頭がいいのか悪いのかは<line-break>理解できませんわ。<end>"
EN: "At the very least, I cannot tell whether you are clever or a fool.<end>"
KO: "…적어도 당신이 머리가 좋은지 나쁜지는 이해할 수 없겠군요.<end>"

## 660078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さぁてと、姫さんの機嫌を<line-break>損ねないうちに退散するか。<end>"
EN: "Well, I should make my escape before I put the princess in a foul mood.<end>"
KO: "자, 공주님 심기를 거스르기 전에 물러날까.<end>"

## 660079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ…、<line-break>もう帰ってしまうのですか？<end>"
EN: "Oh... Must you leave already?<end>"
KO: "아… 벌써 돌아가시는 건가요?<end>"

## 660080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また、酒の臭いで気分が悪くなったと<line-break>騒がれちゃたまらないからな。<line-break>行くぞ、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "I can't have you making a fuss about the smell of booze turning your stomach again. Come on, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "또 술 냄새 때문에 기분 나쁘다고 난리 치면 곤란하니까. 가자, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 660081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテス様…。<end>"
EN: "Lord Xenetes...<end>"
KO: "제네테스 님….<end>"

## 660082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ…、ゼネテス様…。<end>"
EN: "Oh... Lord Xenetes...<end>"
KO: "아…, 제네테스 님….<end>"

## 660083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よ、俺の素行の悪さを<line-break>叔母貴に告げ口してない？<end>"
EN: "Hey. You haven't been telling my aunt about my bad behavior, have you?<end>"
KO: "저기, 내 행실이 나쁘다고 고모한테 고자질한 건 아니지?<end>"

## 660084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私、お母様に告げ口したり<line-break>いたしません。<end>"
EN: "I would never tattle to Mother.<end>"
KO: "저, 어머님께 일러바치거나 하지 않아요.<end>"

## 660085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だろうな。<line-break>だが、それも問題かもな。<line-break>あんたは叔母貴に少し冷淡すぎる。<line-break>確かに叔母貴には冷酷なとこもある。<line-break>けど、あんたにとっちゃ、<line-break>血のつながった母親だろ？<end>"
EN: "I didn't think so. But that may be a problem in itself. You're a little too distant with my aunt. She certainly has a cruel side, but she's still your own mother, isn't she?<end>"
KO: "그렇겠지. 하지만 그것도 문제일지 몰라. 넌 아주머니에게 조금 너무 냉담해. 분명 아주머니한테 냉혹한 면도 있어. 그래도 너한텐 피가 이어진 어머니잖아?<end>"

## 660086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ゼネテス様がそうしろと<line-break>おっしゃるのなら、努力はしてみます。<line-break>でも…<end>"
EN: "If that is what you ask of me, Lord Xenetes, I will try. But...<end>"
KO: "…제네테스 님이 그렇게 하라고 하신다면 노력은 해 보겠습니다. 하지만…<end>"

## 660087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…いいさ。俺の方こそ<line-break>家族関係に立ち入るようなことを<line-break>言っちまった。悪かったな。<line-break><value:$28>、<line-break>なに突っ立ってるんだ。帰るぞ。<end>"
EN: "Never mind. I'm the one who stuck his nose into your family affairs. Sorry about that. <value:$28>, what are you standing around for? We're leaving.<end>"
KO: "…됐어. 나도 가족 일에 끼어드는 말을 해 버렸네. 미안하다. <value:$28>, 뭐 하고 서 있어. 돌아가자.<end>"

## 660088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんな。<line-break>まだ、いらっしゃったばかりですわ。<end>"
EN: "But you have only just arrived.<end>"
KO: "그럴 수가. 아직 오신 지 얼마 되지도 않았는데요.<end>"

## 660089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なぁに。<line-break>気が向いたら、<line-break>また顔を出すさ。<end>"
EN: "Don't worry. I'll drop by again when the mood takes me.<end>"
KO: "뭐. 마음 내키면 또 얼굴 비추지.<end>"

## 660090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテス様…。<end>"
EN: "Xenetes...<end>"
KO: "제네테스 님….<end>"

## 660091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ…ゼネテス様…。<line-break>今日はどうして<line-break>いらしゃったのです？<end>"
EN: "Oh, Lord Xenetes... What brings you here today?<end>"
KO: "아… 제네테스 님…. 오늘은 어쩐 일로 오셨어요?<end>"

## 660092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんて答えると思う？<end>"
EN: "What do you think I'll say?<end>"
KO: "뭐라고 대답할 것 같아?<end>"

## 660093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まず、歩いてと<line-break>お答えになるでしょうね。<line-break>とても陳腐ですけれど。<end>"
EN: "First, you will say, “On foot.” It is dreadfully trite.<end>"
KO: "우선 걸어서라고 대답하시겠죠. 너무 진부하지만요.<end>"

## 660095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "来るべきディンガル軍の侵攻に備えて<line-break>何か策はないか聞きにきたと<line-break>おっしゃるんでしょう？<end>"
EN: "You will say that you came to ask whether I have some plan against the coming invasion by the Dyneskal Army.<end>"
KO: "다가올 딘갈군의 침공에 대비해 무슨 방책이 없는지 물으러 왔다고 하실 거죠?<end>"

## 660097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありませんわ。<end>"
EN: "None.<end>"
KO: "없어요.<end>"

## 660098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そりゃ、残念。<line-break>そうしたら次に<line-break>俺はなんて言う？<end>"
EN: "That's a shame. So what do I say next?<end>"
KO: "그거 아쉽네. 그럼 다음엔 내가 뭐라고 하지?<end>"

## 660099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "駆け落ちしてくれ、ティアナ。<line-break>俺と一緒に逃げようと<line-break>おっしゃいます。<end>"
EN: "You will say, “Elope with me, Tiana. Let us run away together.”<end>"
KO: "도망치자, 티아나. 나와 함께 달아나자고 말씀하십니다.<end>"

## 660100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あたり。そしてティアナはこう答える。<line-break>私は王女です。<line-break>ここに残る義務がありますってな。<end>"
EN: "Correct. And Tiana answers, “I am a princess. It is my duty to remain here.”<end>"
KO: "정답. 그리고 티아나는 이렇게 대답하지. '저는 왕녀입니다. 이곳에 남을 의무가 있습니다.'라고.<end>"

## 660101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ？　最近、こういうときは<line-break>逃げるものだと教えてくれた方が<line-break>いますので、どう答えるでしょうか。<line-break>それよりもゼネテス様。<line-break>私なんかと逃げたいなんて<line-break>ずいぶん追いつめられていますのね。<end>"
EN: "Who can say? Someone recently taught me that one should flee at times like this, so what might my answer be? More importantly, Lord Xenetes, you must be terribly desperate if you wish to run away with someone like me.<end>"
KO: "글쎄요? 요즘은 이런 때 도망치는 거라고 가르쳐 준 분이 계셔서 어떻게 대답할까요. 그보다 제네테스 님. 저 같은 사람과 도망치고 싶다니 꽤 궁지에 몰리셨군요.<end>"

## 660102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よいものは繰り返される。<line-break>それゆえに陳腐にもなる。<end>"
EN: "Good things bear repeating. That's how they become trite.<end>"
KO: "좋은 것은 반복된다. 그렇기에 진부해지기도 하지.<end>"

## 660103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふ、苦しいですわね、ゼネテス様。<line-break>どうしてそんなことを<line-break>おっしゃるのかしら？<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. That was rather forced, Lord Xenetes. Why would you say such a thing?<end>"
KO: "후후, 궁색하네요, 제네테스 님. 왜 그런 말씀을 하시는 걸까요?<end>"

## 660104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ただ、あんたの笑顔が見たいんだよ。<line-break>一切の打算、もくろみ、深謀遠慮<line-break>将来の展開と布石なんかを忘れてな。<end>"
EN: "I just want to see you smile. Forget all the calculations and schemes, the far-reaching designs, the plans for what comes next and all the groundwork laid for them.<end>"
KO: "난 그냥 네 웃는 얼굴이 보고 싶을 뿐이야. 이해득실, 계략, 깊은 계산, 미래를 위한 포석 같은 건 전부 잊고 말이지.<end>"

## 660105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…こんな偽りの笑顔を？<end>"
EN: "A false smile like this one?<end>"
KO: "…이런 거짓 웃음을?<end>"

## 660106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…偽り？<line-break>あんたが笑いかけてくれる<line-break>俺にはこれ以上の真実はないんだよ。<end>"
EN: "False? When you smile at me, there is no greater truth.<end>"
KO: "…거짓이라고? 네가 웃어 주는 나에겐 이것보다 더한 진실은 없어.<end>"

## 660107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんたにもこの事実が見えれば、<line-break>その偽りの笑顔とやらは<line-break>真実の笑顔になるんだろうがな。<end>"
EN: "If you could see that truth yourself, I suppose your so-called false smile would become a true one.<end>"
KO: "너도 이 사실을 볼 수 있다면 그 거짓 웃음이라는 것도 진짜 웃음이 되겠지만 말이지.<end>"

## 660108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "きゃっ！<line-break>勝手に部屋に入ってくるなんて！<end>"
EN: "Ah! You cannot simply walk into my room!<end>"
KO: "꺄! 멋대로 방에 들어오다니!<end>"

## 660109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちゃんとノックしたが、<line-break>返事がないから入ったんだ。<line-break>そうだろ、<value:$28>？<end>"
EN: "I did knock. You didn't answer, so I came in. Isn't that right, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "노크는 했는데 대답이 없어서 들어온 거야. 그렇지, <value:$28>?<end>"

## 660110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それとも、入ってきたのが<line-break>タルテュバじゃなくて<line-break>がっかりしてるのかい？<end>"
EN: "Or are you disappointed it was me and not Tartuba?<end>"
KO: "아니면 들어온 사람이 타르튜바가 아니라서 실망한 거야?<end>"

## 660111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わ、私、タルテュバ様とは<line-break>そういう関係では…！<end>"
EN: "L-Lord Tartuba and I do not have that sort of relationship!<end>"
KO: "저, 저는 타르튜바 님과 그런 관계가…!<end>"

## 660112
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "タルテュバはずいぶんあんたに　　<line-break>熱を上げてるって話だが、<line-break>かわいそうに奴の独りよがりか…。<line-break>姫さんの誰にもへだてない笑顔に<line-break>だまされたってわけだな。<end>"
EN: "I'd heard Tartuba was quite smitten with you, but the poor fellow's feelings must be entirely one-sided. Fooled by the smile you bestow on everyone, was he?<end>"
KO: "타르튜바가 자네에게 푹 빠졌다는 얘기던데, 불쌍하게도 그 녀석 혼자 착각한 건가…. 공주님의 누구에게나 똑같은 미소에 속았다는 거군.<end>"

## 660114
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだい、<line-break>いつもの元気はどうした？<end>"
EN: "What's wrong? Where's your usual spirit?<end>"
KO: "왜 그래, 평소의 기운은 어디 갔어?<end>"

## 660115
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいんです。<line-break>私は、あなたみたいに<line-break>立派な人間ではありませんから。<line-break>あなたみたいに力もないし、<line-break>タルテュバ様をご注意申しあげる<line-break>勇気もありません。<end>"
EN: "It does not matter. I am not an admirable person like you. I have none of your power, nor the courage to admonish Lord Tartuba.<end>"
KO: "괜찮아요. 저는 당신처럼 훌륭한 사람이 아니니까요. 당신처럼 힘도 없고 타르튜바 님께 주의를 드릴 용기도 없습니다.<end>"

## 660116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "笑顔を作るか、<line-break>せいぜい歌を歌うことくらいしか<line-break>能がないのです。<end>"
EN: "All I can do is put on a smile, or perhaps sing a song.<end>"
KO: "웃는 얼굴을 만들거나 기껏해야 노래를 부르는 것밖엔 재주가 없습니다.<end>"

## 660117
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それは立派な才能だぜ。<end>"
EN: "That's a fine talent.<end>"
KO: "그건 훌륭한 재능이야.<end>"

## 660118
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちゃかさないでください。<end>"
EN: "Please do not make light of me.<end>"
KO: "놀리지 마세요.<end>"

## 660119
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わからないのかい？<line-break>みんな、あんたの笑顔を見たくて<line-break>あんたの周りに集まって来るんだよ。<line-break>あんたの笑顔に救われたくてね。<end>"
EN: "Don't you understand? Everyone gathers around you because they want to see you smile. They want that smile to save them.<end>"
KO: "모르겠어? 모두 네 웃는 얼굴을 보고 싶어서 네 주위로 모여드는 거야. 네 미소에 구원받고 싶어서 말이지.<end>"

## 660121
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあてと。<line-break>俺もあんたの笑顔を見たこったし、<line-break>そろそろ退散するかな。<end>"
EN: "Well, I've seen your smile too. I suppose it's about time I made my escape.<end>"
KO: "자, 나도 네 웃는 얼굴을 봤으니 슬슬 물러날까.<end>"

## 660122
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう、いいかげんにしてください。<line-break>ノックぐらいされたらどう？<end>"
EN: "That is quite enough. Could you not at least knock?<end>"
KO: "이제 좀 그만하세요. 노크 정도는 하면 어때요?<end>"

## 660123
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、失敬。一刻も早く<line-break>姫さんの顔を見たくてね。<end>"
EN: "Ah, forgive me. I couldn't wait another moment to see your face.<end>"
KO: "아, 실례. 한시라도 빨리 공주님 얼굴을 보고 싶어서 말이야.<end>"

## 660124
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…いつもそんな言葉で<line-break>酒場の女性を<line-break>口説いてらっしゃるのですね。<end>"
EN: "I suppose that is how you woo the women at the tavern.<end>"
KO: "…늘 그런 말로 술집 여자들을 꼬시고 계시는군요.<end>"

## 660125
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へぇ、やきもちかい？<line-break>確かにあの酒場にゃ<line-break>かわいい娘が多いかもな。<end>"
EN: "Oh? Jealous, are you? There may well be plenty of pretty girls at that tavern.<end>"
KO: "헤에, 질투하냐? 확실히 그 술집엔귀여운 여자애가 많긴 하지.<end>"

## 660126
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でしたら、どうぞ<line-break>私のことなど気にせずに、<line-break>酒場へ戻ってください。<line-break>あなたにはあなたなりの<line-break>生き方があるのでしょう？<line-break>私には理解できませんけれど。<end>"
EN: "Then please, pay me no mind and return to the tavern. You have your own way of life, do you not? Though I cannot understand it.<end>"
KO: "그렇다면 저 같은 건 신경 쓰지 말고 술집으로 돌아가세요. 당신에게는 당신 나름의 삶이 있는 거겠죠? 저는 이해하지 못하겠지만요.<end>"

## 660127
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人の生き方は人それぞれだ。<line-break>それが正しい道だと思うなら、<line-break>まっすぐに進むことだ。<line-break>だが、道は１本じゃないぜ。<line-break>姫さんにもそれがわかっただろ？<line-break>町の連中の暮らしを見てな。<end>"
EN: "Everyone has a different way of living. If you believe yours is right, then walk it without wavering. But there's more than one road. You understand that now, don't you, Princess? You've seen how the townsfolk live.<end>"
KO: "사람의 삶은 저마다 달라. 그게 옳은 길이라 생각한다면 곧장 나아가면 돼. 하지만 길은 하나만 있는 게 아니야. 공주님도 알게 됐지? 마을 사람들의 삶을 보고 말이야.<end>"

## 660129
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃ、お言葉に甘えて<line-break>俺は酒場に戻るとするか。<end>"
EN: "Then I'll take you at your word and head back to the tavern.<end>"
KO: "그럼 말씀에 기대서 난 술집으로 돌아갈까.<end>"

## 660130
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、ひとつ言っておく。<line-break>確かにあの酒場に<line-break>かわいい娘が多いかもしれないが、<line-break>あんたほど<line-break>かわいい娘はいやしないよ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, one thing before I go. That tavern may have plenty of pretty girls, but not one of them is as pretty as you.<end>"
KO: "아, 한마디 해 두지. 그 술집에 귀여운 여자애가 많긴 하지만 너만큼 귀여운 애는 없어.<end>"

## 660131
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "きゃっ！<line-break>いきなり入ってこないでください。<end>"
EN: "Ah! Please do not barge in without warning.<end>"
KO: "꺅! 갑자기 들어오지 마세요.<end>"

## 660132
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、悪い。ノックするような<line-break>柄じゃなくてね。<line-break>ご機嫌うるわしゅう、王女様。<end>"
EN: "Sorry. Knocking just isn't in my nature. A very good day to you, Your Highness.<end>"
KO: "아, 미안. 노크 같은 건 체질이 아니라서. 안녕하신가, 공주님.<end>"

## 660133
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "無理なさらなくても結構ですわ。<line-break>あなたのことはよくわかりました。<end>"
EN: "There is no need to strain yourself. I understand you quite well now.<end>"
KO: "무리하지 않으셔도 됩니다. 당신에 대해서는 잘 알았어요.<end>"

## 660134
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へぇ、どうわかったのか聞きたいね。<end>"
EN: "Oh? I'd like to hear what you've learned.<end>"
KO: "흐음, 어떻게 알았는지 듣고 싶군.<end>"

## 660135
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたは貴族としては失格ですが、<line-break>民衆の間では<line-break>とても信頼されているということです。<end>"
EN: "You are a complete failure as a nobleman, yet the common people place great trust in you.<end>"
KO: "당신은 귀족으로서는 실격이지만 민중 사이에서는 매우 신뢰받고 있다는 뜻입니다.<end>"

## 660136
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なぁんだ、そんなことかい。<line-break>あんたはもっと偉いじゃないか。<line-break>あんたは貴族にも連中にもウケがいい。<end>"
EN: "Oh, is that all? You're more impressive than I am. The nobles and the common folk both like you.<end>"
KO: "뭐야, 그런 거였어? 넌 훨씬 대단하잖아. 귀족들에게도 저 사람들에게도 인기가 좋잖아.<end>"

## 660137
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私は<line-break>人の顔色を見ているだけです。<end>"
EN: "I merely watch people's faces and act accordingly.<end>"
KO: "저는 사람 눈치만 보고 있을 뿐입니다.<end>"

## 660138
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なあ、ティアナ。人間てのはさ、<line-break>相手が貴族にしろスラムの連中にしろ<line-break>そうバカにしたもんでもないぜ？<line-break>顔色をうかがうだけで<line-break>人とうまくやっていけるなんて、<line-break>傲慢もいいところだ。<end>"
EN: "Listen, Tiana. People aren't to be underestimated, whether they're nobles or folk from the Slums. Thinking you can get along with everyone simply by watching their moods is arrogance itself.<end>"
KO: "있잖아, 티아나. 인간은 상대가 귀족이든 슬럼 사람이든 그렇게 얕잡아볼 존재가 아니야. 얼굴색만 살피며 사람과 잘 지낼 수 있다고 생각하는 건 오만도 정도가 있지.<end>"

## 660140
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さてと。まーた、<line-break>姫さんのご機嫌を損ねちまった。<line-break>退散するぜ、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Well, I've gone and spoiled the princess's mood again. Time to make our escape, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "자, 또 공주님 기분을 상하게 해 버렸네. 난 물러날게, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 660141
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え…、もうお帰りですの？<end>"
EN: "What...? Are you leaving already?<end>"
KO: "어머…, 벌써 돌아가시나요?<end>"

## 660142
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "生きてたら、<line-break>必ずまた来るさ。<end>"
EN: "If I'm still alive, I'll definitely come back.<end>"
KO: "살아 있다면 반드시 다시 올 거야.<end>"

## 660144
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まあ、ゼネテス様。ご無事でしたの。<line-break>まだ、恨みを持つ女の方に<line-break>刺されていらっしゃらないのですね。<end>"
EN: "Why, Lord Xenetes. So you are still alive. No woman bearing a grudge has stabbed you yet, then.<end>"
KO: "어머, 제네테스 님. 무사하셨군요. 아직 원한을 품은 여자분에게 찔리지는 않으셨네요.<end>"

## 660145
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほう、<line-break>姫さんは心配してくれていたんだ。<line-break>光栄だね。<end>"
EN: "Well now, the princess was worried about me. I'm honored.<end>"
KO: "호오, 공주님께서 걱정해 주셨군. 영광이네.<end>"

## 660146
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええ、あなたみたいな方を刺して<line-break>人生をあやまっては、<line-break>その方があまりにかわいそうですもの。<end>"
EN: "Indeed. I would pity any woman who ruined her life by stabbing a man like you.<end>"
KO: "네, 당신 같은 분을 찔러서 인생을 망치면 그 사람이 너무 불쌍하잖아요.<end>"

## 660147
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか、<line-break>姫さんが心配してくれてるんなら<line-break>また、元気な顔を見せてやらなきゃな。<end>"
EN: "Well, if the princess is worried about me, I'll have to come back and show you I'm still hale and hearty.<end>"
KO: "그런가, 공주님이 걱정해 준다면 또 건강한 모습을 보여 줘야겠군.<end>"

## 660149
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誰かはわからない女の人の方だろ？<line-break>わかってるさ。<line-break>　<line-break>だが、あんたがその誰かのことを<line-break>知らないんなら、俺が姫さんに会うしか<line-break>そいつの無事を知る方法はねえだろ？<end>"
EN: "The woman nobody knows the identity of, right? I understand. But if you don't know who she is, the only way I can find out whether she's safe is by coming to see you, isn't it?<end>"
KO: "누군지 모르는 여자 쪽 말이지? 알아. 하지만 네가 그 사람이 누군지 모른다면 내가 공주님을 만나 보는 수밖에 없잖아. 그래야 그 사람의 무사함을 알 수 있으니까.<end>"

## 660150
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうですね。<line-break>それでは、嫌々ですが<line-break>また、いらしてください。<end>"
EN: "I suppose so. Then, however reluctantly, I ask that you visit again.<end>"
KO: "…그렇군요. 그럼 내키진 않지만 또 와 주세요.<end>"

## 660151
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう、姫さんのために<line-break>来てやるよ。<line-break>じゃあな。<end>"
EN: "Sure. I'll come for your sake, Princess. See you.<end>"
KO: "그래, 공주님을 위해 와 주지. 그럼 간다.<end>"

## 660152
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様なら、<line-break>大歓迎いたしますわ。<line-break>また、いらしてくださいね。<end>"
EN: "As for you, <value:$28>, you would be most welcome. Please come again.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 님이라면 대환영이에요. 또 와 주세요.<end>"

## 660153
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まあ、<value:$28>様。<line-break>ようこそいらっしゃいました。<end>"
EN: "Why, <value:$28>. Welcome.<end>"
KO: "어머, <value:$28>님. 어서 오세요.<end>"

## 660154
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "姫さんのきれいな姿を<line-break>見に来たぜ。<end>"
EN: "I came to admire the beautiful princess.<end>"
KO: "공주님의 아름다운 모습을 보러 왔지.<end>"

## 660155
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私は花ではありません！<line-break>ゼネテス様を容姿で喜ばせるために<line-break>存在しているのではありません。<end>"
EN: "I am not a flower! I do not exist to delight Lord Xenetes with my appearance.<end>"
KO: "저는 꽃이 아닙니다! 제네테스 님을 외모로 기쁘게 하려고 존재하는 게 아니에요.<end>"

## 660156
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "花ねえ。<line-break>意外かもしれないが<line-break>俺は花が好きなんだ。<line-break>花はその美しさで人を喜ばせるために<line-break>存在しているわけじゃあないが、<line-break>人がその姿を愛でるのを許してくれる。<end>"
EN: "A flower, eh? It may surprise you, but I like flowers. They don't exist to delight people with their beauty, yet they allow us to admire them all the same.<end>"
KO: "꽃이라. 의외일지도 모르지만 난 꽃을 좋아해. 꽃은 아름다움으로 사람을 기쁘게 하려고 존재하는 건 아니지만, 사람들이 그 모습을 사랑하는 건 허락해 주지.<end>"

## 660158
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だから、俺は花を見かけると<line-break>散ってしまう前に、花に向かって<line-break>きれいだぜってほめてやりたくなる。<end>"
EN: "That's why, when I see a flower, I want to tell it how beautiful it is before its petals fall.<end>"
KO: "그래서 난 꽃을 보면 져 버리기 전에 꽃을 향해 예쁘다고 칭찬해 주고 싶어져.<end>"

## 660159
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それは、花にではなくて<line-break>女の方にでしょう？<end>"
EN: "You say that to women, not flowers, do you not?<end>"
KO: "그건 꽃이 아니라 여자 쪽이겠죠?<end>"

## 660160
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、相変わらず<line-break>鋭いところついてくるねえ。<line-break>そう、女もほめるかな。<line-break>…っと、姫さんに怒られる前に<line-break>退散するか。<line-break>じゃ、また来るぜ。<end>"
EN: "Heh. Sharp as ever. Yes, I suppose I compliment women too. Well, I'd better make my escape before the princess gets angry. I'll see you again.<end>"
KO: "후후, 여전히 날카로운 데를 찌르는구나. 그래, 여자도 칭찬하지. …어이쿠, 공주님한테 혼나기 전에 물러날까. 그럼 또 올게.<end>"

## 660161
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…あ、<value:$28>様。<line-break>ぜひ、またいらしてください。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>... Please do come again.<end>"
KO: "…아, <value:$28>님. 꼭 다시 와 주세요.<end>"

## 660162
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これはゼネテス・ファーロス総司令。<line-break>失礼ですが、こちらに寄らないで<line-break>いただけますか？<end>"
EN: "Commander-in-Chief Xenetes Pharos. Forgive me, but would you please keep your distance?<end>"
KO: "이분은 제네테스 파로스 총사령관입니다. 실례지만 이쪽으로 가까이 오지 말아 주시겠습니까?<end>"

## 660163
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、<line-break>酒のニオイはお嫌いかな？<end>"
EN: "Heh. Don't care for the smell of wine?<end>"
KO: "후후, 술 냄새는 싫은가?<end>"

## 660164
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええ、酒のニオイも嫌いですけれど、<line-break>それ以上にあなたに染みついた<line-break>血のニオイが嫌ですわ。<line-break>ファーロス家の人間は<line-break>血のニオイがして<line-break>本当に嫌です。<end>"
EN: "I dislike the smell of wine, yes, but I dislike the stench of blood clinging to you even more. The members of House Pharos all reek of blood. I truly cannot stand it.<end>"
KO: "네, 술 냄새도 싫지만 그보다 당신에게 밴 피 냄새가 더 싫어요. 파로스 가문 사람들은 피 냄새가 나서 정말 싫습니다.<end>"

## 660165
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "血のニオイね。<line-break>よかったよ。少なくとも<line-break>血もナミダもない奴じゃあないわけだ。<line-break>俺にしても、<line-break>エリスの叔母貴にしてもな。<end>"
EN: "The smell of blood, eh? That's a relief. At least it proves we've got blood and tears in us after all―Aunt Eris and me both.<end>"
KO: "피 냄새군. 다행이야. 적어도 피도 눈물도 없는 녀석은 아니라는 거지. 나도, 에리스 숙모도 말이야.<end>"

## 660166
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くだらない<line-break>へりくつですわ。<end>"
EN: "What ridiculous sophistry.<end>"
KO: "시시한 억지입니다.<end>"

## 660167
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "血の代わりに葡萄酒のつまった<line-break>お貴族どもをお相手にしておられる<line-break>お姫様には、俺はくさみが強いようだ。<line-break>今日は、退散するわ。<line-break>それじゃあな、お姫さん。<line-break>また来るぜ。<end>"
EN: "To a princess accustomed to dealing with nobles whose veins run with wine instead of blood, I suppose my stench is a little strong. I'll make my escape for today. See you, Princess. I'll be back.<end>"
KO: "피 대신 포도주가 흐르는 귀족들을 상대하시는 공주님께는 내가 냄새가 좀 강한 모양이군. 오늘은 물러나지. 그럼 잘 있어, 공주님. 또 올게.<end>"

## 660168
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お願いですから、今度来るときは<line-break>ロストールが滅ぶ日にしてください。<line-break>それなら、もう会わずにすみますから。<end>"
EN: "Please make your next visit the day Rostorl falls. Then I will never have to see you again.<end>"
KO: "부탁이니까 다음에 올 때는 로스톨이 멸망하는 날로 해 주세요. 그러면 이제 만나지 않아도 되니까요.<end>"

## 660169
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストールが滅ぶ日ね。<line-break>よしわかった。そのときは<line-break>姫さんをこの城から助け出してやるよ。<line-break>な、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "The day Rostorl falls, eh? Very well. When that day comes, I'll rescue the princess from this castle. Right, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "로스톨이 멸망하는 날이네. 좋아, 알았어. 그때는 공주님을 이 성에서 구해 내 줄게. 그렇지, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 660170
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "勝手にしてください。<line-break>その日まで来ていただかなければ<line-break>私は結構ですから。<end>"
EN: "Do as you please. I shall be quite content so long as you do not visit again before that day.<end>"
KO: "마음대로 하세요. 그날까지 와 주시지 않는다면 저는 상관없으니까요.<end>"

## 660171
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そっちは、<line-break>しかとは約束できないな。<line-break>んじゃな。<end>"
EN: "That part I can't promise. See you.<end>"
KO: "그쪽은 확실히 약속 못 하겠네. 그럼 간다.<end>"

## 660172
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう！<end>"
EN: "Oh, honestly!<end>"
KO: "정말!<end>"

## 660173
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "相変わらずだな、姫さんは。<line-break>これからも顔を出してやるか。<end>"
EN: "The princess never changes. I suppose I'll keep stopping by.<end>"
KO: "여전히 똑같군, 공주님은. 앞으로도 얼굴을 비춰 줘야겠어.<end>"

## 660174
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec066-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうかい、残念だな。<line-break>姫さんの所に行こうと思ったんだが<line-break>しょうがない、ひとりで行くか。<end>"
EN: "Oh. That's a shame. I was going to visit the princess, but I suppose I'll have to go alone.<end>"
KO: "그런가, 아쉽군. 공주님에게 가려고 했는데 어쩔 수 없지. 혼자 갈까.<end>"

## 670001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様、<line-break>ようこそ、おいでくださいました。<line-break>　<line-break>今日は、<value:$28>様が<line-break>いらしてくださるような気が<line-break>していました。どうぞこちらへ。<end>"
EN: "Welcome, <value:$28>. I had a feeling you would come today, <value:$28>. Please, come this way.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님, 어서 오세요. 오늘은 <value:$28>님이 와 주실 것 같은 기분이 들었어요. 이쪽으로 오세요.<end>"

## 670002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティアナ、邪魔をする。<end>"
EN: "Tiana. Pardon the intrusion.<end>"
KO: "티아나, 방해 좀 하겠다.<end>"

## 670003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…お母様。<line-break>何かご用ですか？<end>"
EN: "...Mother. Did you need something?<end>"
KO: "…어머니. 무슨 일이신가요?<end>"

## 670004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "実の母親が娘のもとを訪ねるのに<line-break>用などいるのか？<line-break>　<line-break>ただ、そなたの顔を見に<line-break>ここまで来たまでだ。<end>"
EN: "Does a mother need a reason to visit her own daughter? I merely came all this way to see your face.<end>"
KO: "친어머니가 딸을 찾아오는데 무슨 용건이 필요하겠느냐? 그저 네 얼굴을 보러 여기까지 왔을 뿐이다.<end>"

## 670005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それは意外ですわ。<end>"
EN: "How unexpected.<end>"
KO: "그건 뜻밖이네요.<end>"

## 670006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ファーロスの雌狐の動きには<line-break>ひとつの無駄もない。すべてが<line-break>権謀術数につながっている…<end>"
EN: "“The Vixen of Pharos never makes a single wasted move. Everything she does is part of some scheme...”<end>"
KO: "파로스의 여우가 움직이는 데는 한 치의 낭비도 없어. 모든 것이 권모술수로 이어지고 있지….<end>"

## 670007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そのように<line-break>誰もが噂しておりましたので。<end>"
EN: "That is what everyone says.<end>"
KO: "다들 그렇게 소문내고 있었으니까요.<end>"

## 670008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誰もが？<line-break>レムオン坊やであろう？　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>他の者はくだらぬ噂はできても、<line-break>私を批判するだけの気概は<line-break>持ち合わせてはおらぬ。<end>"
EN: "Everyone? You mean young Lemghon, surely. The others may indulge in idle gossip, but none has the courage to criticize me.<end>"
KO: "누구나라고? 레무온 도련님이겠지? 다른 자들은 시시한 소문은 떠들어도 나를 비판할 기개는 없으니.<end>"

## 670009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "少しのどが渇いた。<end>"
EN: "I'm a little thirsty.<end>"
KO: "조금 목이 마르네.<end>"

## 670010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "飲み物を持ってくるように<line-break>言ってくれぬか。<end>"
EN: "Would you have someone bring me a drink?<end>"
KO: "마실 것을 가져오라고 전해 주겠나.<end>"

## 670011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "雌狐の娘ですもの。<line-break>毒を盛るかもしれませんわ、お母様。<end>"
EN: "I am the vixen's daughter, after all. I might poison it, Mother.<end>"
KO: "암여우의 딸이잖아요. 독을 탈지도 몰라요, 어머니.<end>"

## 670012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、そなたに殺されるなら本望よ。<end>"
EN: "Heh. If I am to die by your hand, I could ask for no better end.<end>"
KO: "후후, 그대에게 죽는다면 여한이 없네.<end>"

## 670013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そなた、ティアナの部屋にも<line-break>顔を出しておったのか。<end>"
EN: "So you have been visiting Tiana's Room as well.<end>"
KO: "그대, 티아나의 방에도 얼굴을 비췄던 건가.<end>"

## 670014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これからもティアナのこと、<line-break>守ってやってほしい。<end>"
EN: "I want you to keep protecting Tiana.<end>"
KO: "앞으로도 티아나를 지켜 줬으면 한다.<end>"

## 670015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0レムオン坊やの弟君が<line-break>何をしておいでか？<end>レムオン坊やの妹君が<line-break>何をしておいでか？<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0And what might young Lemghon's younger brother be doing here?<end>And what might young Lemghon's younger sister be doing here?<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0레무온 꼬마의 남동생이뭘 하고 있는지?<end>레무온 꼬마의 여동생이뭘 하고 있는지?<end>"

## 670016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お母様、私のお客様です。<line-break>失礼なことはおっしゃらないで。<end>"
EN: "Mother, this is my guest. Please don't be rude.<end>"
KO: "어머니, 제 손님이에요. 실례되는 말씀은 하지 마세요.<end>"

## 670021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そなた、ティアナと仲がよいのか？<end>"
EN: "Are you close to Tiana?<end>"
KO: "그대, 티아나와 사이가 좋은가?<end>"

## 670022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これからもティアナのこと、　<line-break>守ってやってほしい。<line-break>　<line-break>そなたの兄が目の敵にしておる<line-break>ファーロスの雌狐の頼みだが、<line-break>聞いてくれぬか？<end>"
EN: "I want you to keep protecting Tiana. I know it is a request from the Vixen of Pharos, whom your brother despises, but will you grant it?<end>"
KO: "앞으로도 티아나를 지켜 주었으면 한다. 그대의 형이 원수처럼 여기는 파로스의 암여우 부탁이지만, 들어 주겠나?<end>"

## 670034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そなた、<value:$28>だな？<end>"
EN: "You are <value:$28>, are you not?<end>"
KO: "그대가 <value:$28>인가?<end>"

## 670035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテスがそなたのことを<line-break>話しておった。よい冒険者だと。<line-break>　<line-break>ティアナのことを<line-break>守ってやってほしい。<end>"
EN: "Xenetes told me about you. He said you were a fine adventurer. I want you to protect Tiana.<end>"
KO: "제네테스가 그대 이야기를 하더군. 좋은 모험자라고. 티아나를 지켜 주었으면 한다.<end>"

## 670036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…よい目をしておる。<line-break>目の輝きは魂の輝きを示す。<line-break>　<line-break>そなたの目の輝きは<line-break>無限の可能性を感じさせる。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "...You have good eyes. Their light reflects the light of the soul, and yours speaks of limitless possibility.<end>"
KO: "…좋은 눈을 하고 있군. 눈빛은 영혼의 빛을 나타낸다. 그대의 눈빛에서는 무한한 가능성이 느껴진다.<end>"

## 670037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティアナのことを<line-break>守ってやってほしい。<end>"
EN: "I want you to protect Tiana.<end>"
KO: "티아나를 지켜 줬으면 한다.<end>"

## 670038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "母親である私は国のため、家のため、<line-break>このようなザマだ。<line-break>　<line-break>あれにもつらい思いをさせていよう。<line-break>ティアナの心を<line-break>少しでも助けてやってくれ。<end>"
EN: "For the sake of my country and my house, this is what I have become, even as her mother. Tiana must have suffered for it. Please do what you can to ease her heart.<end>"
KO: "어머니인 나는 나라와 가문을 위해 이런 꼴이다. 그 아이도 힘들겠지. 티아나의 마음을 조금이라도 도와 줘.<end>"

## 670039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、私も親バカなことよ。<line-break>よろしく頼むぞ、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Heh. I suppose I am a doting mother after all. I leave her in your care, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "후후, 나도 팔불출이군. 잘 부탁한다, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 670040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティアナ、邪魔をしたな。<end>"
EN: "Tiana, forgive the intrusion.<end>"
KO: "티아나, 방해했군.<end>"

## 670041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "飲み物は、毒が入っておらぬなら、<line-break>供の者にでもやるとよい。<end>"
EN: "If the drink is not poisoned, you may give it to one of my attendants.<end>"
KO: "마실 것에 독이 들지 않았다면 수행원에게라도 주면 되겠지.<end>"

## 670042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お母様は、私を外に追い出して、<line-break>あなたに何をお話しだったのです？<end>"
EN: "What did Mother talk to you about after sending me away?<end>"
KO: "어머니께서는 저를 밖으로 내보내고 당신께 무슨 말씀을 하셨나요?<end>"

## 670043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そう。<end>"
EN: "...I see.<end>"
KO: "…그래.<end>"

## 670044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お母様はとてもお美しい。<end>"
EN: "Mother is very beautiful.<end>"
KO: "어머님은 정말 아름다우시다.<end>"

## 670045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ですが、お父様は…<line-break>あまりパッとしません。<line-break>　<line-break>…釣り合いませんよね。<end>"
EN: "But Father is... rather unremarkable. They aren't well matched, are they?<end>"
KO: "하지만 아버님은… 별로 눈에 띄지 않으세요. …어울리지 않죠.<end>"

## 670046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "たとえ、お父様の他に誰かがいても、<line-break>不思議はありませんよね？<end>"
EN: "It would hardly be surprising if there were someone other than Father, would it?<end>"
KO: "아버지 말고 다른 누군가가 있어도 이상할 건 없겠죠?<end>"

## 670047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "男の人は、美人には弱いのですね…。<end>"
EN: "Men are helpless before a beautiful woman, aren't they...?<end>"
KO: "남자는 미인에게 약하군요….<end>"

## 670048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…今日は…気分がすぐれません。<line-break>お引き取りいただけませんか？<end>"
EN: "...I'm not feeling well today. Would you please leave?<end>"
KO: "…오늘은… 기분이 좋지 않네요. 돌아가 주시겠어요?<end>"

## 670049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、どうした？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, what's wrong?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 무슨 일이야?<end>"

## 670050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "陛下の他に男が？<line-break>あのエリスにか？　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>くだらん噂話はやめろと言ったはずだ。<line-break>エリスが色恋におぼれるような女なら<line-break>俺も苦労はしない。<end>"
EN: "Another man besides His Majesty? Eris, of all people? I thought I told you to stop repeating such ridiculous gossip. If Eris were the kind of woman to lose herself in romance, I wouldn't have half the trouble I do.<end>"
KO: "폐하 말고 다른 남자가? 그 에리스에게? 시답잖은 소문은 그만두랬을 텐데. 에리스가 사랑에 빠질 여자라면 나도 고생 안 하지.<end>"

## 670051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、そんな危険な噂話をするほど<line-break>度胸のある人間が王宮にいるとはな。<line-break>その話、どこから仕入れた？<end>"
EN: "Still, I'm surprised anyone in the palace has the nerve to gossip about something so dangerous. Where did you hear it?<end>"
KO: "그런데 그런 위험한 소문을 떠들 만큼 배짱 있는 인간이 왕궁에 있다니. 그 이야기는 어디서 들었지?<end>"

## 670052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティアナか？<end>"
EN: "Tiana?<end>"
KO: "티아나인가?<end>"

## 670053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フッ、お前は王宮の事情に<line-break>うといから、からかわれたのだ。<end>"
EN: "Heh. You were being teased because you know so little about palace affairs.<end>"
KO: "흥, 넌 왕궁 사정에 어두우니까 놀림받은 거다.<end>"

## 670054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテスさん！<end>"
EN: "Xenetes!<end>"
KO: "제네테스 씨!<end>"

## 670055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うい。どうしたい？<end>"
EN: "Yeah? What's up?<end>"
KO: "응. 어떻게 하고 싶어?<end>"

## 670056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よ、<value:$28>、<line-break>デート？<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>. On a date?<end>"
KO: "여어, <value:$28>, 데이트?<end>"

## 670057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんた…、<value:$2B>の　<line-break><value:$28>さんだろ？<line-break>　<line-break>あんたみたいな有名人に<line-break>こんなところで会えるとはねぇ。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Wait... You're <value:$28>, the <value:$2B>, aren't you? Never thought I'd run into someone so famous in a place like this.<end>"
KO: "당신… <value:$2B>의 <value:$28> 씨지? 당신 같은 유명인을 이런 곳에서 만나다니.<end>"

## 670058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺はゼネテス。よろしく。<end>"
EN: "I'm Xenetes. Good to meet you.<end>"
KO: "나는 제네테스. 잘 부탁한다.<end>"

## 670059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…あんた、いい目してるね。　　　<end>"
EN: "You've got good eyes.<end>"
KO: "…당신, 눈빛이 좋군.<end>"

## 670060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺の叔母貴がよく言ってるよ。<line-break>目の輝きは魂の輝きを示すってね。<end>"
EN: "My aunt always says the light in your eyes reflects the light in your soul.<end>"
KO: "우리 숙모가 자주 하는 말이 있어. 눈빛은 영혼의 빛을 보여 준다고.<end>"

## 670062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>ね。<line-break>俺はゼネテス。よろしく。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, is it? I'm Xenetes. Good to meet you.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>이지. 난 제네테스다. 잘 부탁해.<end>"

## 670063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテスさん！<line-break>そんなところで女の子口説いてる<line-break>場合じゃないぜ。早く！<end>"
EN: "Xenetes! This is no time to be chatting up girls. Hurry!<end>"
KO: "제네테스 씨! 이런 데서 여자나 꼬실 때가 아니야. 빨리!<end>"

## 670064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うーい。<end>"
EN: "Yeah, yeah.<end>"
KO: "어이.<end>"

## 670065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなた…いったいここで<line-break>何をしているのです？<end>"
EN: "What in the world are you doing here?<end>"
KO: "당신… 대체 여기서 뭘 하고 있는 겁니까?<end>"

## 670066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いろんなことさ。<line-break>メシ食ったり、寝たり、いろいろな。<line-break>　<line-break>悪いが、話はあとだ。<end>"
EN: "All sorts of things. Eating, sleeping, you name it. Sorry, but we'll have to talk later.<end>"
KO: "여러 가지지. 밥 먹고 자고, 이것저것. 미안하지만 얘기는 나중에 하자.<end>"

## 670067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どんな具合だい？<end>"
EN: "How are things looking?<end>"
KO: "상태가 어때?<end>"

## 670068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "骨は折れちゃいない。<line-break>問題はショックで<line-break>体温が下がってきていることだな。<end>"
EN: "No broken bones. The problem is the shock: body temperature is starting to drop.<end>"
KO: "뼈가 부러진 건 아니야. 문제는 충격 때문에체온이 내려가고 있다는 거지.<end>"

## 670069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテス！<line-break>このような所で何をしているのです。<line-break>まじめに答えてください！<end>"
EN: "Xenetes! What are you doing in a place like this? Give me a serious answer!<end>"
KO: "제네테스! 이런 곳에서 뭘 하고 있는 겁니까. 진지하게 대답하세요!<end>"

## 670070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…今まで歌ってたの、あんただな。<end>"
EN: "That was you singing just now, wasn't it?<end>"
KO: "…지금까지 노래하던 게 너였군.<end>"

## 670071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え、ええ。<end>"
EN: "Y-yes.<end>"
KO: "아, 네.<end>"

## 670072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この子は恐がっている。<line-break>優しい歌を歌ってくれ。<end>"
EN: "This child is frightened. Sing something gentle.<end>"
KO: "이 아이가 무서워하고 있어. 다정한 노래를 불러 줘.<end>"

## 670074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この子のために歌ってくれ。<line-break>頼む。<end>"
EN: "Sing for the child. Please.<end>"
KO: "이 아이를 위해 노래해 줘. 부탁한다.<end>"

## 670075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よかったな。大事にならなくて。<end>"
EN: "Good thing it wasn't anything serious.<end>"
KO: "다행이군. 큰일로 번지지 않아서.<end>"

## 670076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…自己満足ですわ。<end>"
EN: "It was nothing but self-satisfaction.<end>"
KO: "…자기만족이에요.<end>"

## 670077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "て、言うと？<end>"
EN: "What do you mean?<end>"
KO: "그 말은?<end>"

## 670078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの方たちの暮らし。<line-break>子供の命をなんとも思わない貴族。　　<line-break>　<line-break>それを解決しない限り、<line-break>あの男の子…チダフちゃんみたいな子が<line-break>また出てくるのです！<end>"
EN: "The way those people live. The nobles who care nothing for a child's life. Until those problems are solved, there will be more children like that boy... like little Chidaf!<end>"
KO: "그 사람들의 삶. 아이의 목숨을 아무렇지도 않게 여기는 귀족. 그것을 해결하지 않으면 그 남자아이… 치다프 같은 아이가 또 생길 겁니다!<end>"

## 670079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほぉ、お前さん、人の名前<line-break>覚えるのが得意だね。<end>"
EN: "Well, aren't you good at remembering names.<end>"
KO: "호오, 자네 사람 이름 외우는 데 소질이 있군.<end>"

## 670080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちゃかさないでください！<end>"
EN: "Please don't make light of this!<end>"
KO: "놀리지 마세요!<end>"

## 670081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まじめに聞いてるさ。それで？<end>"
EN: "I am taking this seriously. Go on.<end>"
KO: "진지하게 듣고 있어. 그래서?<end>"

## 670082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ケガ人を助けて、感謝されて、あなたは<line-break>それで、気分がいいかもしれません。<line-break>　<line-break>でも、本当にあの方たちのことを<line-break>思うなら、もっと他に<line-break>するべきことがあるはずです。<end>"
EN: "You help someone who's hurt, get thanked, and perhaps that makes you feel good. But if you truly care about those people, surely there are other things you should be doing.<end>"
KO: "부상자를 돕고 감사받으니 당신은 기분이 좋을지 모르죠. 하지만 정말 그분들을 생각한다면 다른 해야 할 일이 있을 겁니다.<end>"

## 670083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前さんはどう思う？<end>"
EN: "What do you think?<end>"
KO: "자네는 어떻게 생각해?<end>"

## 670084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティアナの言うとおりだと思う<end>"
EN: "I think Tiana is right.<end>"
KO: "티아나 말이 맞다고 생각해<end>"

## 670085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティアナの言うことは何か違うと思う<end>"
EN: "I think there's something off about what Tiana is saying.<end>"
KO: "티아나 말은 뭔가 아닌 것 같아<end>"

## 670086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なるほどね。　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>俺たちが、今ここでやったこと…<line-break>確かになんの解決にも<line-break>なっちゃあいないだろうが、<end>"
EN: "I see. What we did here may not have solved anything, but...<end>"
KO: "그렇군. 우리가 지금 여기서 한 일… 확실히 아무 해결도 되지 않았겠지만,<end>"

## 670087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "意味はあったと思いたいねぇ。<end>"
EN: "I'd like to think it meant something.<end>"
KO: "의미는 있었다고 생각하고 싶네.<end>"

## 670088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "んじゃな。<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "See you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그럼 이만. <value:$28>.<end>"

## 670089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "優しい歌をありがとうよ。<end>"
EN: "Thanks for the gentle song.<end>"
KO: "다정한 노래 고맙구나.<end>"

## 670090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…帰りましょう。<end>"
EN: "...Let's go back.<end>"
KO: "…돌아가죠.<end>"

## 670091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "隠し通路から出てきたのですもの。<line-break>正面からは帰れません。<end>"
EN: "We came out through a secret passage. We can't go back through the front entrance.<end>"
KO: "비밀 통로로 나왔으니 정면으로 돌아갈 순 없습니다.<end>"

## 670092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私の部屋はこちらでは<line-break>なかったはずです。<end>"
EN: "I don't believe my room was this way.<end>"
KO: "제 방은 이쪽이 아니었던 것 같은데요.<end>"

## 670093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今日は…<line-break>よい勉強になりました。<end>"
EN: "Today was... quite educational.<end>"
KO: "오늘은… 좋은 공부가 됐습니다.<end>"

## 670094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですよね。<end>"
EN: "That's true, isn't it?<end>"
KO: "그렇죠.<end>"

## 670095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私には…無力な鳥には<line-break>かごの外に居場所なんて…ない。<end>"
EN: "For me... for a helpless bird, there's no place outside the cage.<end>"
KO: "내겐… 힘없는 새에게는 새장 밖에 있을 곳 따위… 없어.<end>"

## 670096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お手を、お姫さん<end>"
EN: "Your hand, Princess.<end>"
KO: "손을, 공주님<end>"

## 670098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…あ。<end>"
EN: "...Oh.<end>"
KO: "…아.<end>"

## 670099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なぜ、この間、<line-break>私ではなく、アトレイア様を<line-break>かばわれたのですか？<end>"
EN: "Why did you shield Lady Atleia instead of me the other day?<end>"
KO: "왜 지난번에는 저 대신 아트레이아 님을 감싸신 건가요?<end>"

## 670100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "彼女が先王の娘だから？<line-break>彼女は正真正銘、<line-break>先王の娘だから？<end>"
EN: "Because she's the former king's daughter? Because she's his true daughter?<end>"
KO: "그녀가 선왕의 딸이라서요? 그녀가 틀림없는 선왕의 딸이라서요?<end>"

## 670101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec067-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…フフ、ごめんなさい。<line-break>軽蔑しましたか？<line-break>もう言いません…。<end>"

KO: "…후후, 미안해요. 경멸했나요? 이제 말하지 않을게요….<end>"

## 680000
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec068-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%5わしが若いころは、キャプテン・カンダカンと<line-break>暴れ回ったものよ。じゃが、カンダカンが<line-break>死んで、昔の勢いはなくなってしもうた。<end>カンダカンは海洋冒険者共同体として<line-break>ワッシャー海賊を結成したんじゃよ。<line-break>じゃから、わしらの本業は宝探しなんじゃ<end>冒険で得た金で船を買ったカンダカンは、<line-break>自分を慕う冒険者やアウトローを引き連れ<line-break>この地に砦を築いたのじゃ。<end>最初は１４人しかいなかった住人も　<line-break>カンダカンがスカウトしてきたり、<line-break>あんたみたいに未開の森をこえてきた<line-break>強者が住み着いたりして、<line-break>今じゃ３００人をこえちまった。<end>わしは、ある隠れ里の出身でな、<line-break>カンダカンに会うまではバロルに仕え<line-break>スパイをしておったのじゃ。<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%5In my younger days, Captain Kandakan and I raised hell together. But since Kandakan died, we've lost our old fire.<end>Kandakan founded the Washer Pirates as a fellowship of seafaring adventurers. Treasure hunting is our true trade.<end>Kandakan bought a ship with the money from those adventures, gathered adventurers and outlaws who looked up to Kandakan, and built this fort.<end>There were only fourteen people here at first. Then Kandakan began recruiting, and hardy souls like you crossed the Uncharted Forest and settled here. Now there are more than three hundred of us.<end>I come from a hidden village. Before I met Kandakan, I served Balor as a spy.<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%5내가 젊었을 때는 캡틴 칸다칸과 함께 날뛰곤 했지. 하지만 칸다칸이 죽고 옛날 기세는 사라져 버렸어.<end>칸다칸은 해양 모험자 공동체로 와셔 해적을 만들었지. 그러니 우리 본업은 보물찾기라네.<end>모험으로 번 돈으로 배를 산 칸다칸은 자신을 따르는 모험자와 무법자들을 데리고 이곳에 요새를 세웠지.<end>처음엔 14명뿐이던 주민도 칸다칸이 스카우트해 오거나, 자네처럼 미개척 숲을 넘어온 강자가 정착하면서 지금은 300명을 넘었어.<end>나는 어느 숨겨진 마을 출신인데, 칸다칸을 만나기 전에는 발로르를 섬기며 스파이를 했었지.<end>"

## 680001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec068-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こっから先は進入禁止なんだよ。<line-break>部外者は、あんまりうろちょろしないで<line-break>欲しいんだけどね。<end>"
EN: "You're not allowed past here. I'd rather outsiders didn't wander around too much.<end>"
KO: "여기서부터는 출입 금지야. 관계없는 사람은 너무 돌아다니지 않았으면 하는데.<end>"

## 680002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec068-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%4キャプテンが亡くなって、一番つらいのは<line-break>ヒルダリアだよ。だけど、あの娘は<line-break>私らの生活を守るために一生懸命さ。<line-break>泣きたいくらいつらいこともあるだろうに、<line-break>キャプテンの葬式以来、涙を流すことも<line-break>なくなってしまってね。<end>カンダカンはワンマンでねえ。<line-break>何でもかんでも自分でやるもんだから、<line-break>他の人間は命令を聞くだけでよかったのさ。<line-break>おかげでヒルダリアを補佐できるような<line-break>人がいないってわけさ。<end>この砦には貧富の差はないよ。<line-break>なんせ辺境の地だから、みんなで助け合わないと<line-break>生きていけないからね。<end>わしゃあワッシャーを、自由で差別ものうて<line-break>人が人らしゅう暮らせる国にしたいんよ。<line-break>じゃけえ、あんたもがんばりんさいよ。<line-break>カンダカンの真似だよ。<line-break>変な話し方だよねえ。<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%4No one has suffered more from the Captain's death than Hildaria. Even so, she's doing everything she can to protect our way of life. There must be times when it hurts enough to make her cry, but she hasn't shed a tear since the Captain's funeral.<end>Kandakan ran everything alone, handling every last thing while everyone else simply followed orders. That's left us with no one capable of supporting Hildaria.<end>There are no rich or poor in this fort. We're out on the frontier; if we don't help one another, we won't survive.<end>I want Washer to be a free country without discrimination, where people can live with dignity. So you do your best too, you hear? That was my Kandakan impression. Strange way of talking, isn't it?<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%4캡틴이 돌아가시고 가장 힘든 건 힐다리아야. 하지만 그 애는 우리 생활을 지키려고 최선을 다하고 있어. 울고 싶을 만큼 힘들 때도 있을 텐데 캡틴의 장례식 이후로 눈물도 흘리지 않게 됐지.<end>칸다칸은 독단적인 사람이었어. 뭐든 혼자 다 하다 보니 다른 사람은 명령만 따르면 됐지. 그래서 힐다리아를 보좌할 사람이 없는 거야.<end>이 요새에는 빈부 차이가 없어. 워낙 변방이라 서로 돕지 않으면 살아갈 수 없거든.<end>난 와셔를 자유롭고 차별도 없고 사람이 사람답게 사는 나라로 만들고 싶어. 그러니 자네도 힘내게나. 칸다칸 흉내야. 말투가 좀 이상하지?<end>"

## 680003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec068-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんた、まだ俺たちの仲間じゃないよな。<line-break>ここから先は、よそ者に行かせるわけには<line-break>いかねえんだ。どっかよそに行ってくれ。<end>"
EN: "You're not one of us yet, are you? We can't let outsiders go any farther. Go somewhere else.<end>"
KO: "당신, 아직 우리 동료는 아니지. 여기서부터는 외부인을 들여보낼 수 없어. 다른 데로 가 줘.<end>"

## 680004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec068-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%3すべての人に食を与えよ！<line-break>それが師匠の遺言だった。<line-break>ゆえに私は、すべての人に食を与えるのだ！！<end>食べたい料理があれば遠慮なく言ってくれ。<line-break>食べたいときに食べたいものを！<line-break>それがカンダカンとの約束だ。<end>そろそろ調味料がなくなるな。<line-break>ヒルダリアに調達するよう頼んで<line-break>おかなくては…。<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%3“Feed every person!” Those were my master's dying words. And so I shall feed every person!<end>If there's any dish you'd like, don't hesitate to ask. Whatever you want, whenever you want it! That was my promise to Kandakan.<end>We're nearly out of seasonings. I must ask Hildaria to procure more...<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%3모든 이에게 먹을 것을 주어라! 그것이 스승님의 유언이었다. 그러므로 나는 모든 이에게 먹을 것을 주겠다!!<end>먹고 싶은 요리가 있으면 사양 말고 말해. 먹고 싶을 때 먹고 싶은 것을! 그것이 칸다칸과의 약속이다.<end>슬슬 조미료가 떨어지겠군. 힐다리아에게 조달해 달라고 부탁해 둬야겠어….<end>"

## 680005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec068-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%2サラドの料理はうまい。<line-break>腹一杯食べて力いっぱい働く所存。<end>ここはみんなが使う場所。<line-break>壊れたところはすぐに直す。<line-break>これが俺の仕事。<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%2Sarado's cooking is good. I intend to eat my fill and work with all my strength.<end>Everyone uses this place. If something breaks, I fix it at once. That's my job.<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%2사라드의 요리는 맛있다. 배불리 먹고 힘껏 일할 생각이다.<end>여긴 모두가 쓰는 곳. 망가진 곳은 바로 고친다. 그게 내 일이다.<end>"

## 680006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec068-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%3め～し！　め～し！　め～し！<end>みんなが来ないと飯にならないよ～。<line-break>早く来いよ～。<end>サラドの料理はうますぎるよ～。<line-break>あんなに食べたのに、<line-break>すぐ食べたくなっちゃう～。<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%3Fooood! Fooood! Fooood!<end>We can't eat until everyone gets here! Hurry uuuup!<end>Sarado's cooking is too gooood! I ate all that, and I already want more!<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%3밥~! 밥~! 밥~!<end>다들 안 오면 밥 못 먹잖아~. 빨리 와~.<end>사라드 요리는 너무 맛있어~. 그렇게 많이 먹었는데도 금방 또 먹고 싶어진다니까~.<end>"

## 680007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec068-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%3サラドをスカウトしてきた先代の舌に<line-break>間違いはなかった～よ～。<line-break>先代は偉大ね～。<end>ヒルダリアの姉御はおっかね～の～。<line-break>お仕置きされるのイヤな～の～。<end>海賊するのは恐い～の～。<line-break>昔みたいに宝探しをしたい～の～。<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%3The old Captain knew good food when it came along. Recruiting Sarado proved it! What a great Captain!<end>Boss Hildaria is scaaary! I don't want her to punish meee!<end>Being a pirate is scaaary! I want to hunt for treasure like we used tooo!<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%3사라드를 스카우트해 온 전대의 안목은 틀리지 않았~어~. 전대는 위대해~.<end>힐다리아 누님은 무서워~. 벌 받는 건 싫어~.<end>해적질은 무서워~. 옛날처럼 보물찾기 하고 싶어~.<end>"

## 680008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec068-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%4普通の船乗りは危険を恐れるが、<line-break>俺たちは海の冒険者だ。<line-break>冒険に危険はつきもの、恐れはしない。<end>フゴーは先代に珍品を売ってもらえなかった<line-break>腹いせに、我々の船が各地の港に<line-break>入港できないようにした。<line-break>だから、必要な物資はフゴーの交易船から<line-break>いただくことにしているのだ。<end>ヒルダリアもがんばっているが、<line-break>先代には到底およばない。<line-break>やはり冒険の素人にキャプテンは無理だろう。<end>先代と一緒に西の海を冒険していたころは、<line-break>毎日が充実していたものだ。<line-break>あんたなら、俺の気持ちもわかるだろ？<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%4Ordinary sailors fear danger, but we are adventurers of the sea. Danger is part of any adventure. We do not fear it.<end>Hugo retaliated when the old Captain wouldn't sell him any rare goods, arranging for our ships to be barred from ports everywhere. That is why we take the supplies we need from Hugo's merchant vessels.<end>Hildaria is doing her best, but she cannot begin to compare with the old Captain. A novice adventurer simply cannot serve as Captain.<end>When I was exploring the western seas with the old Captain, every day felt worthwhile. You know what I mean, don't you?<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%4보통 선원은 위험을 두려워하지만 우리는 바다의 모험자다. 모험에 위험은 따라오는 법, 두렵지 않아.<end>휴고는 선대에게 진품을 팔지 않았다는 앙갚음으로 우리 배가 각지 항구에 입항하지 못하게 했다. 그래서 필요한 물자는 휴고의 무역선에서 받아 가기로 했지.<end>힐다리아도 노력하고 있지만 선대에는 도저히 미치지 못해. 역시 모험 초보에게 선장은 무리겠지.<end>선대와 함께 서쪽 바다를 모험하던 시절은 매일이 충실했지. 너라면 내 마음도 알겠지?<end>"

## 680009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec068-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%4わしは泳げぬから、船に乗るのは無理じゃ。<line-break>だからこうして船大工をしておるのじゃ。<end>わしが船の整備を怠けると、<line-break>仲間の命に関わるからのう。<line-break>いつも真剣勝負なのじゃ。<end>あのような巨大な物が海に浮く…。<line-break>船とは本当に不思議な物じゃ。<end>グレイシール号も傷みが激しくなってきた。<line-break>そろそろ新しい船を造ったほうが<line-break>いいかもしれぬな。<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%4I cannot swim, so going to sea aboard a ship is out of the question. That is why I work as a shipwright.<end>If I neglect the ships, my comrades' lives are at stake. I must give the work my full attention every time.<end>To think something so enormous can float upon the sea... Ships truly are wondrous things.<end>The Gray Seal is showing a great deal of wear. It may be time to build a new ship.<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%4난 헤엄을 못 쳐서 배를 타는 건 무리라네. 그래서 이렇게 배를 만드는 일을 하는 게지.<end>내가 배 정비를 게을리하면 동료들의 목숨이 달린 문제니까. 언제나 진검승부라네.<end>저렇게 거대한 것이 바다에 뜨다니…. 배란 정말 신기한 물건이야.<end>그레이실호도 많이 낡았군. 슬슬 새 배를 만드는 편이 좋을지도 모르겠어.<end>"

## 680010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec068-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう！　あんたよそ者だろ。<line-break>ここから先はオイラたちの領域だ！<line-break>悪いが通すわけにはいかないね。<end>"
EN: "Hey! You're an outsider, aren't you? From here on, you're in our territory! Sorry, but I can't let you through.<end>"
KO: "오! 너 외지인이지. 여기서부터는 우리 영역이야! 미안하지만 지나가게 해 줄 순 없어.<end>"

## 680011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec068-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%3ヒルダリアには悪いが、<line-break>彼女は冒険者の心を理解してない。<line-break>俺たちは海賊じゃない！　冒険者なんだ！<end>生きるためにフゴーの船を襲うのは納得できる。<line-break>だが海賊を中心に活動して、冒険をしないのは<line-break>絶対に納得できぬのだ！<end>キャプテンは偉大な人だった。<line-break>冒険者としても、指導者としても<line-break>尊敬しているよ。<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%3No offense to Hildaria, but she doesn't understand how adventurers feel. We're not pirates! We're adventurers!<end>I can accept raiding Hugo's ships to survive. But I will never accept making piracy our main pursuit and abandoning adventure altogether!<end>The Captain was a great person and earned my respect as both an adventurer and a leader.<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%3힐다리아에겐 미안하지만, 그녀는 모험자의 마음을 이해하지 못해. 우리는 해적이 아니야! 모험자라고!<end>살기 위해 휴고의 배를 습격하는 건 이해할 수 있어. 하지만 해적질을 주된 활동으로 삼고 모험을 하지 않는 건 절대 납득할 수 없어!<end>캡틴은 위대한 사람이었어. 모험자로서도 지도자로서도 존경하고 있지.<end>"

## 680012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec068-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんじゃい、もう出てってしまうんかい。<line-break>もうちょい、ゆっくりしていけばええのにのう。<line-break>ほんじゃあ、船を出す準備をするかいのう。<end>"
EN: "What, leaving already? You could stay and take it easy a while longer. Well, I suppose I'd better get the boat ready.<end>"
KO: "뭐여, 벌써 나가려는 거여. 좀 더 느긋하게 있다 가도 되는데 말이여. 그럼 배를 띄울 준비를 할까나.<end>"

## 680013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec068-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "砦から出る<end>"
EN: "Leave the fort<end>"
KO: "요새에서 나간다<end>"

## 680014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec068-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "砦から出ない<end>"
EN: "Stay at the fort<end>"
KO: "요새에서 나가지 않는다<end>"
