source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec068-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ワッシャーメッセージここまで<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "왓샤 메시지 여기까지<end>"

## 690000
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アーギルシャイアメッセージ開始<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "아르길샤이어 메시지 시작<end>"

## 690001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここがディンガル帝国の首都<line-break>エンシャントだ。<line-break>　<line-break>町の中でまで群れては歩かん。<line-break>町を出るまでは別行動が冒険者同士の<line-break>暗黙のルールだ。憶えておけ。<end>"
EN: "This is Ancient, capital of the Dyneskal Empire. Adventurers don't travel in packs inside the city. Until we leave town, we go our separate ways. It's an unwritten rule among adventurers. Remember that.<end>"
KO: "여기가 딩갈 제국의 수도 엔샨트다. 도시 안에서까지 무리 지어 다니진 않는다. 도시를 나설 때까지 따로 움직이는 게 모험자들 사이의 암묵적인 규칙이다. 기억해 둬.<end>"

## 690002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "冒険者として<line-break>旅をした経験は？<end>"
EN: "Have you ever traveled as an adventurer?<end>"
KO: "모험자로 여행해 본 경험은?<end>"

## 690003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…だろうな。<line-break>今からギルドで冒険者登録をする。<line-break>ギルドは、そこだ。<end>"
EN: "I thought not. Now it's time to register as an adventurer at the Guild. The Guild's right over there.<end>"
KO: "…그렇겠지. 지금부터 길드에서 모험가 등록을 한다. 길드는 저기다.<end>"

## 690004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…来たか。<line-break>カウンターにいるおやじに<line-break>話しかけろ。…登録してくれる。<end>"
EN: "...So you're here. Talk to the man at the counter. He'll register you.<end>"
KO: "…왔군. 카운터에 있는 아저씨에게 말을 걸어. …등록해 줄 거다.<end>"

## 690005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カウンターにいるおやじに<line-break>話しかけろ。登録してくれる。<end>"
EN: "Talk to the old man at the counter. He'll register you.<end>"
KO: "카운터에 있는 아저씨에게 말 걸어. 등록해 줄 거야.<end>"

## 690006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "冒険者登録かい？<line-break>あんたみたいに若い人は大歓迎だ。<line-break>　<line-break>ちょっと調べさせてもらうよ。<line-break>信用できる人物かどうか<line-break>確かめなくちゃならないんでな。<end>"
EN: "Here to register as an adventurer? Young people like you are always welcome. Let me just check you out first. We need to make sure you're someone we can trust.<end>"
KO: "모험자 등록인가? 너처럼 젊은 사람은 대환영이지. 잠깐 조사 좀 할게. 믿을 수 있는 사람인지 확인해야 하거든.<end>"

## 690007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "幻術計振れておらず<line-break>犯罪登録当てはまらず。<line-break>　<line-break>おめでとう。<line-break>あんたも冒険者として<line-break>登録されたよ。<end>"
EN: "The illusion gauge didn't react, and there's no match in the criminal registry. Congratulations. You're now registered as an adventurer.<end>"
KO: "환술계 수치도 없고 범죄 등록에도 해당하지 않는군. 축하해. 너도 모험자로 등록됐어.<end>"

## 690008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これからは、バリバリ仕事して<line-break>名をあげていってくれよ。<line-break>がんばりな！<end>"
EN: "Now get out there, take on plenty of work, and make a name for yourself. Good luck!<end>"
KO: "이제부터 팍팍 일해서 이름을 날려 줘. 힘내!<end>"

## 690009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "仕事をこなしながら、<line-break>強くなることだ。<line-break>弱いやつは邪魔なだけだからな。<line-break>仕事の選択は、お前に任せる。<line-break>では、町を出るときに合流しよう。<end>"
EN: "Take on jobs and grow stronger. Weaklings only get in the way. What work you choose is up to you. We'll meet up when you leave town.<end>"
KO: "일을 해내면서 강해져라. 약한 녀석은 방해만 되니까. 어떤 일을 고를지는 네게 맡긴다. 그럼 마을을 나갈 때 합류하지.<end>"

## 690010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>、<line-break>気をつけろ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, watch out.<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28>, 조심해.<end>"

## 690011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの女…強い…。<end>"
EN: "That woman... she's strong.<end>"
KO: "그 여자… 강해….<end>"

## 690012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…引き上げることにしましょう…。<line-break>…ミイスは焼かれ、神器もとうとう<line-break>アーギルシャイアに落ちましたか…。<end>"
EN: "We should withdraw. So Miis has been burned, and the divine artifact has finally fallen into Argyleshire's hands...<end>"
KO: "…철수하도록 하지요…. 미이스는 불탔고 신기도 결국 아르길샤이어 손에 떨어졌군요….<end>"

## 690013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アーギルシャイアだと？<line-break>貴様！<line-break>アーギルシャイアの仲間か…！<end>"
EN: "Argyleshire? You! Are you one of her allies?!<end>"
KO: "아르길샤이어라고? 네놈! 아르길샤이어의 동료인가…!<end>"

## 690014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、転送中に来ては危険です。<line-break>下がって…。<end>"
EN: "Oh, it's dangerous to come near during teleportation. Stand back...<end>"
KO: "아, 전송 중에 오면 위험합니다. 물러나세요….<end>"

## 690015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ！<line-break>瞬間移動…だと！？<end>"
EN: "Gah! Teleportation?!<end>"
KO: "큭! 순간이동…이라고!?<end>"

## 690016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…瞬間移動だ。<line-break>空間をねじ曲げて一瞬のうちに<line-break>何かを移動させる術。<line-break>空間をねじ曲げることができるのは<line-break>高レベルの魔道士だけだ。<line-break>…魔人クラスのな。<end>"
EN: "Teleportation. A spell that bends space to move something in an instant. Only high-level sorcerers can bend space. Demon-class sorcerers, that is.<end>"
KO: "…순간이동이다. 공간을 비틀어 순식간에 무언가를 이동시키는 술법. 공간을 비틀 수 있는 건 고위 마도사뿐이야. …마인급이지.<end>"

## 690017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "奴も魔人かもしれん。<line-break>つまり、<line-break>アーギルシャイアの仲間…。<end>"
EN: "Maybe we're dealing with another demon. In other words, one of Argyleshire's allies...<end>"
KO: "그 녀석도 마인일지 모른다. 즉 아르길샤이어의 동료….<end>"

## 690018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…いや、奴の目的地から<line-break>そう遠くに放り出されてはいない。<line-break>　<line-break>…瞬間移動は<line-break>そう使える術ではない。<line-break>奴も、この近くにいる。<end>"
EN: "No. We haven't been thrown far from the destination. Teleportation isn't a spell that can be used so freely. The caster must be nearby too.<end>"
KO: "…아니, 녀석의 목적지에서 그리 멀리 떨어진 곳에 내던져진 건 아니다. …순간이동은 그렇게 자주 쓸 수 있는 술법이 아니야. 녀석도 이 근처에 있다.<end>"

## 690019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうだな。<line-break>この森から捜そう。<end>"
EN: "Right. Let's start with this forest.<end>"
KO: "…그래. 이 숲부터 찾아보자.<end>"

## 690020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おや、めずらしい。<line-break>お客様ですか？<end>"
EN: "Well, this is unusual. A visitor?<end>"
KO: "어라, 드문 일이네요. 손님인가요?<end>"

## 690021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…くっ、やはり<line-break>アーギルシャイアの仲間か！<end>"
EN: "Urgh... I knew it! You're with Argyleshire!<end>"
KO: "…큭, 역시 아르길샤이어의 동료인가!<end>"

## 690022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "違いますよ。<line-break>アーギルシャイアの仲間にご用なら<line-break>呼びましょう。<end>"
EN: "No, I'm not. But if you have business with Argyleshire's companions, I can call one of them.<end>"
KO: "아닙니다. 아르길샤이어의 동료에게 볼일이 있다면 불러 드리죠.<end>"

## 690023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "呼んだかー？　オルファウス。<end>"
EN: "You called, Orphaus?<end>"
KO: "불렀나~? 오르파우스.<end>"

## 690024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…なんだ<line-break>このブサイクな猫は。<end>"
EN: "What is this ugly cat?<end>"
KO: "…뭐야, 이 못생긴 고양이는.<end>"

## 690025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "猫じゃない！<line-break>破壊神の円卓騎士のひとり、<line-break>ネモ様だ！<line-break>今でこそ、<line-break>こんな姿に変えられているが<line-break>本来は立派な魔人なんだ！<end>"
EN: "I'm not a cat! I am Lord Nemo, one of the God of Destruction's Knights of the Round Table! I've been changed into this shape for the moment, but in my true form, I'm a magnificent demon!<end>"
KO: "고양이가 아니야! 파괴신의 원탁기사 중 하나인 네모 님이다! 지금은 이런 모습으로 변했지만 원래는 훌륭한 마인이야!<end>"

## 690026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そこの方がアーギルシャイアのことで<line-break>お話があるようですよ、ネモ。<end>"
EN: "It seems our visitor wishes to speak about Argyleshire, Nemo.<end>"
KO: "저분이 아르길샤이어에 대해 할 말이 있는 모양이에요, 네모.<end>"

## 690027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺は奴の同僚だが、保護者じゃない。<line-break>苦情なんか聞いてられるか。<end>"
EN: "I'm her colleague, not her keeper. Why should I listen to complaints about her?<end>"
KO: "난 녀석 동료지만 보호자는 아니야. 불평 같은 걸 들어줄까 보냐.<end>"

## 690028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それでも、ここまで来たということは　<line-break>偶然ではない、ということです。<line-break>巡り合わせが働いているはずです。<line-break>中に入ってください。<line-break>お茶にしながら、お話を伺いましょう。<end>"
EN: "Even so, the fact that you came this far cannot be mere coincidence. Some turn of fate must be at work. Please come inside. We can have tea while I hear what you have to say.<end>"
KO: "그래도 여기까지 왔다는 건 우연이 아니라는 뜻입니다. 인연이 작용하고 있는 겁니다. 들어오세요. 차를 마시며 이야기를 들어 보죠.<end>"

## 690029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…女、<line-break>俺に命令するな。<end>"
EN: "Woman... don't order me around.<end>"
KO: "…여자, 나한테 명령하지 마.<end>"

## 690030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "女…って私は男ですよ。<line-break>アーギルシャイアの仲間でもないです。<line-break>そして、命令でなくて提案です。<line-break>それとも、さっきの一撃で<line-break>動けませんか？<line-break>加減はしたんですよ。<end>"
EN: "Woman? I'm a man. Nor am I one of Argyleshire's companions. And that was a suggestion, not an order. Or did that last blow leave you unable to move? I did hold back.<end>"
KO: "여자…라니 저는 남자입니다. 아르길샤이어의 동료도 아니고요. 그리고 명령이 아니라 제안입니다. 아니면 아까 한 방에 움직이지 못하시겠습니까? 힘 조절은 했는데요.<end>"

## 690031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…くっ。<line-break>…すぐ行く。<end>"
EN: "Urgh... I'll be right there.<end>"
KO: "…큭. …곧 간다.<end>"

## 690032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それは結構です。<line-break>待っていますね。<end>"
EN: "Excellent. I'll be waiting.<end>"
KO: "그거 좋네요. 기다리고 있을게요.<end>"

## 690033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうですか、あなたは<line-break>ミイスの生き残りなんですね。<line-break>それで、アーギルシャイアを追った。<line-break>それならば、あなたがたは<line-break>聖杯を追うとよいでしょう。<end>"
EN: "I see. You're a survivor of Miis. That's why you pursued Argyleshire. In that case, all of you should seek the Grail.<end>"
KO: "…그렇군요, 당신은 미이스의 생존자군요. 그래서 아르길샤이어를 쫓았고. 그렇다면 여러분은 성배를 쫓는 게 좋겠습니다.<end>"

## 690034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…聖杯？<end>"
EN: "The Grail?<end>"
KO: "…성배?<end>"

## 690035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "闇の神器、禁断の聖杯です。<line-break>アーギルシャイアは究極生物を作り上げ<line-break>この世界を破壊しようとしています。<line-break>その究極生物を作る方法を知るために<line-break>禁断の聖杯によって得られる<line-break>膨大な知識を利用するつもりなのです。<end>"
EN: "The Dark Artifact known as the Forbidden Grail. Argyleshire intends to create the Ultimate Life-Form and use it to destroy this world. She means to draw upon the vast knowledge granted by the Forbidden Grail to learn how such a creature can be made.<end>"
KO: "어둠의 신기, 금단의 성배입니다. 아르길샤이어는 궁극생물을 만들어 이 세계를 파괴하려 하고 있습니다. 그 궁극생물을 만드는 방법을 알기 위해 금단의 성배로 얻을 수 있는 방대한 지식을 이용하려는 겁니다.<end>"

## 690036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "彼女が追っている聖杯を追えば　　　　<line-break>彼女といつか関わることに<line-break>なるでしょう。<end>"
EN: "If you pursue the Grail she seeks, your paths will eventually cross.<end>"
KO: "그녀가 쫓고 있는 성배를 쫓으면 언젠가 그녀와 관계하게 되겠지요.<end>"

## 690038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゴブゴブ団を名乗るゴブリンどもが<line-break>今、聖杯を持っている。<line-break>　<line-break>聖杯の力で人間の言葉を<line-break>しゃべれるようになっている。<line-break>すぐわかるはずだ。<end>"
EN: "A band of Goblins calling themselves the Gob-Gob Gang has the Grail now. Its power has given them the ability to speak the human tongue. You should recognize them at once.<end>"
KO: "고브고브단이라는 고블린들이 지금 성배를 갖고 있다. 성배의 힘으로 인간 말을 할 수 있게 됐으니 금방 알아볼 거야.<end>"

## 690039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くすっ。<line-break>ネモはもとの姿に戻るために<line-break>彼らを操り、聖杯を盗ませたのですが、<line-break>知恵を付けた彼らに見放されて、　　　<line-break>今もこの格好なんです。<end>"
EN: "Heh. Nemo manipulated them into stealing the Grail so he could regain his true form. But once it gave them some intelligence, they abandoned him. That is why he still looks like this.<end>"
KO: "후훗. 네모는 원래 모습으로 돌아가기 위해 그들을 조종해 성배를 훔치게 했지만, 지혜를 얻은 그들에게 버림받아서 지금도 이 모습이랍니다.<end>"

## 690040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うるさい！　とにかく、<line-break>このままでは奴らが危ない。　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>それに最悪、アーギルシャイアが<line-break>奴らがいる町ごと焼き払うことだって<line-break>あり得るんだ。ミイスのときみたいに！<end>"
EN: "Shut up! The point is, they're in danger if this goes on. In the worst case, Argyleshire could burn down the entire town they're in, just like what happened to Miis!<end>"
KO: "시끄러워! 아무튼 이대로면 녀석들이 위험해. 최악의 경우 아르길샤이어가 녀석들이 있는 마을째 불태울 수도 있어. 미이스 때처럼!<end>"

## 690041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたなら、ご存じでしょう？<line-break>アーギルシャイアは気まぐれで残酷…。<line-break>　<line-break>おもしろ半分に殺戮を楽しみ、<line-break>町を焼くことなどなんでもありません。<line-break>神器を奪うためならなおさらです。<end>"
EN: "You know what Argyleshire is like. Capricious and cruel. She delights in slaughter for sport and thinks nothing of burning a town to the ground, especially if it means seizing an artifact.<end>"
KO: "당신이라면 아시겠죠? 아르길샤이어는 변덕스럽고 잔혹해요…. 재미 삼아 살육을 즐기고 마을을 불태우는 것쯤 아무렇지도 않아요. 신기를 빼앗기 위해서라면 더더욱 그렇죠.<end>"

## 690042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そういうわけで、新たな犠牲をだす前に<line-break>聖杯を取ってきてほしいのです。<line-break>お願いできるでしょうか？<end>"
EN: "That is why I want you to retrieve the Grail before anyone else is sacrificed. Will you do it?<end>"
KO: "그런 이유로 새로운 희생자가 나오기 전에 성배를 가져와 주셨으면 합니다. 부탁드려도 될까요?<end>"

## 690043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "聖杯はここに持ってきてくれれば　　<line-break>私が預かります。<line-break>　<line-break>もっとも、私が信用できないなら、<line-break>あなたが責任持って<line-break>預かっていてくれてもかまいません。<end>"
EN: "If you bring the Grail here, I will keep it safe. Of course, if you do not trust me, you may take responsibility for guarding it yourselves.<end>"
KO: "성배는 여기 가져오시면 제가 맡겠습니다. 다만 저를 믿을 수 없다면 당신이 책임지고 보관해도 괜찮습니다.<end>"

## 690044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そのあたりの判断は<line-break>あなた方にお任せします。　　　　　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "I leave that decision to you.<end>"
KO: "그 부분의 판단은 여러분께 맡기겠습니다.<end>"

## 690045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あと、合成怪物を作る素材を<line-break>アーギルシャイアは求めています。　<line-break>　<line-break>最近、妖術宰相ゾフォルが<line-break>古代の闇の怪物、ティラの娘たちを<line-break>復活させています。<end>"
EN: "Argyleshire is also seeking materials from which to create synthetic monsters. Recently, Zofor, the Sorcerous Chancellor, has been reviving the Daughters of Tira, ancient monsters of darkness.<end>"
KO: "그리고 아르길샤이어는 합성괴물을 만들 재료를 찾고 있습니다. 최근 요술재상 조포르가 고대 어둠의 괴물, 티라의 딸들을 부활시키고 있어요.<end>"

## 690046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もしかすると、素材を求めて　　　　<line-break>それらの怪物たちを狩りに<line-break>彼女が現れるかもしれません。<end>"
EN: "Argyleshire may appear to hunt those monsters for the materials she needs.<end>"
KO: "어쩌면 소재를 구하려고 그 괴물들을 사냥하러 그녀가 나타날지도 모릅니다.<end>"

## 690047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それにしても、<line-break>無限のソウルを持つ者ですかぁ…。<line-break>　<line-break>竜王が目覚め、闇の門が開き、<line-break>そして無限のソウルの<line-break>持ち主が現れる…。<end>"
EN: "Still, the Bearer of the Infinite Soul... The Dragon King awakens, the Dark Gate opens, and the Bearer of the Infinite Soul appears...<end>"
KO: "그나저나 무한의 소울을 지닌 자라…. 용왕이 깨어나고 어둠의 문이 열리며, 무한의 소울을 지닌 자가 나타난다….<end>"

## 690048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "運命の女神も悪戯好きですねえ。<line-break>くすくす…。<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>さん、でしたね。<line-break>来てください。<line-break>説明したいことがあります。<end>"
EN: "The Goddess of Fate does enjoy her little games. Heh heh... Your name was <value:$28>, wasn't it? Please come. There's something I'd like to explain.<end>"
KO: "운명의 여신도 장난을 좋아하네요. 후후…. <value:$28>님이셨죠. 따라오세요. 설명드릴 게 있어요.<end>"

## 690049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…行け。<line-break>殺す気はないらしい。<line-break>　<line-break>殺す気なら<line-break>とうにやっている。<line-break>あいつは…強い。<end>"
EN: "Go. It seems killing isn't the plan. If it were, it would've happened by now. That one... is strong.<end>"
KO: "…가. 죽일 생각은 없는 모양이다. 죽일 생각이었다면 진작 그랬겠지. 녀석은… 강해.<end>"

## 690050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "軽傷とはいえ、セラを<line-break>ケガさせてしまいましたんでね。<line-break>　<line-break>つぐないに、微力ながら<line-break>お手伝いできること<line-break>ということで…。<end>"
EN: "Sera's injuries may have been minor, but I was responsible for them. So, by way of atonement, I thought I might offer what little help I can.<end>"
KO: "가벼운 상처라지만 세라를 다치게 했으니 말이죠. 사죄하는 뜻에서 미력하나마 도울 수 있는 일을….<end>"

## 690051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この子でも役立ててもらおうかな<line-break>と思うんです。<line-break>　<line-break>この転送機は世界中の<line-break>たいていの所から<line-break>仲間を呼び出せます。<end>"
EN: "I thought perhaps you could put this little one to use. This teleportation device can summon your companions from almost anywhere in the world.<end>"
KO: "이 아이도 도움이 되게 해 볼까 합니다. 이 전송기는 세계 대부분의 곳에서 동료를 불러낼 수 있어요.<end>"

## 690053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "逆に、別れたい仲間を、望む所に<line-break>送り出してやることもできます。<end>"
EN: "It works the other way, too: it can send companions you want to part ways with wherever they wish to go.<end>"
KO: "반대로 헤어지고 싶은 동료를 원하는 곳으로 보내 줄 수도 있습니다.<end>"

## 690054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたが将来、旅の途中で<line-break>さまざまな人と出会い、<line-break>仲間を増やしたとき、<line-break>たぶん、この子が<line-break>役に立ちますよ。<end>"
EN: "Someday, when you've met all sorts of people on your travels and gathered more companions, this little one will probably come in handy.<end>"
KO: "당신이 앞으로 여행 중 여러 사람을 만나 동료를 늘리게 되면 아마 이 아이가 도움이 될 겁니다.<end>"

## 690056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くすくす。<line-break>驚かせてしまってすみません。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>今後は呼び出す前に<line-break>相手の方に都合を確認します。<line-break>もう、こんなことはありませんから。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. I'm sorry I startled you. From now on, I'll make sure it's convenient before summoning anyone. This won't happen again.<end>"
KO: "후후. 놀라게 해서 죄송합니다. 앞으로는 불러내기 전에 상대방 사정을 확인하겠습니다. 다시는 이런 일 없을 겁니다.<end>"

## 690057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "一緒に旅する仲間を変更したいときは<line-break>この猫屋敷に来てください。　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>ああ、すいません。<line-break>私ばかり話してしまって…。<end>"
EN: "Whenever you want to change your traveling companions, come to Cat Manor. Ah, forgive me. I've been doing all the talking.<end>"
KO: "함께 여행할 동료를 바꾸고 싶을 때는 이 고양이 저택으로 와 주세요. 아, 죄송합니다. 저만 계속 이야기했네요….<end>"

## 690058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "無限のソウルとは<end>"
EN: "What is the Infinite Soul?<end>"
KO: "무한의 소울이란<end>"

## 690059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "転送機について<end>"
EN: "Tell me about the teleportation device.<end>"
KO: "전송기에 대해<end>"

## 690060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "闇の神器について<end>"
EN: "Tell me about the Dark Artifact.<end>"
KO: "어둠의 신기에 대하여<end>"

## 690061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネモについて<end>"
EN: "Tell me about Nemo.<end>"
KO: "네모에 대해서<end>"

## 690062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アーギルシャイアについて<end>"
EN: "Tell me about Argyleshire.<end>"
KO: "아르길샤이어에 대해서<end>"

## 690063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "質問はない<end>"
EN: "I don't have any questions.<end>"
KO: "질문은 없다<end>"

## 690064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、それは秘密です。　　<line-break>ま、おいおい、わかりますよ。<line-break>　<line-break>とりあえずはギルドで<line-break>仕事をしていってください。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Heh heh. That's a secret. You'll understand in time. For now, take on some work at the Guild.<end>"
KO: "후후, 그건 비밀입니다. 뭐, 차차 알게 될 겁니다. 일단 길드에서 일을 해 주세요.<end>"

## 690065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "他人のために働くことで<line-break>魂を成長させていけば、<line-break>いずれ、わかってきますよ。<end>"
EN: "As you work for the sake of others and allow your soul to grow, you'll gradually come to understand.<end>"
KO: "다른 사람을 위해 일하며 영혼을 성장시키다 보면 언젠가 알게 될 겁니다.<end>"

## 690066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "将来、旅の途中で出会った仲間を<line-break>呼び出すための装置です。　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>一緒に旅する仲間を変更したくなったら<line-break>この猫屋敷に来てください。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "It's a device for summoning companions you meet on your future travels. Whenever you want to change your traveling companions, come to Cat Manor.<end>"
KO: "앞으로 여행 중 만난 동료를 불러내기 위한 장치입니다. 함께 여행할 동료를 바꾸고 싶어지면 이 고양이 저택으로 와 주세요.<end>"

## 690067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "一緒に旅をしたい仲間を呼び出したり、<line-break>別行動を取ることを決めた仲間を<line-break>送り出したりできますよ。<end>"
EN: "You can summon companions you wish to travel with, or send off those who have decided to go their separate ways.<end>"
KO: "함께 여행하고 싶은 동료를 불러오거나 따로 행동하기로 한 동료를 돌려보낼 수 있어요.<end>"

## 690068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "破壊神ウルグの復活に用いられた<line-break>強力な魔道器です。<line-break>　<line-break>１２種類あるといいます。<line-break>禁断の聖杯は<line-break>その中のひとつですね。<end>"
EN: "They are powerful magical artifacts used to resurrect Ulugh, the God of Destruction. There are said to be twelve of them, and the Forbidden Grail is one.<end>"
KO: "파괴신 울그의 부활에 쓰인 강력한 마도기입니다. 12종류가 있다고 하지요. 금단의 성배도 그중 하나입니다.<end>"

## 690069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "禁断の聖杯については<line-break>今、ゴブゴブ団という<line-break>３匹のゴブリンが持っています。<line-break>とりあえずは、<line-break>エンシャントを探してみると<line-break>彼らに会えると思いますよ。<end>"
EN: "The Forbidden Grail is now in the hands of three Goblins called the Gob-Gob Gang. For now, try searching Ancient. I believe you'll find them there.<end>"
KO: "금단의 성배라면 지금 고브고브단이라는 고블린 셋이 가지고 있습니다. 일단 엔샨트를 찾아보면 그들을 만날 수 있을 거예요.<end>"

## 690070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここにいるブサイクな猫です。　　　　<line-break>口は悪いですけど、無害ですよ。<line-break>　<line-break>もともとは老人星の精霊で<line-break>天空の賢者とも呼ばれていましたが<line-break>地上に堕ちて、魔人になりました。<end>"
EN: "The ugly cat right here. Foul-mouthed, but harmless. Originally a spirit of the Old Man Star, the cat was also known as the Sage of the Heavens, but fell to earth and became a demon.<end>"
KO: "여기 있는 못생긴 고양이입니다. 입은 험하지만 해는 없어요. 원래는 노인성의 정령으로 천공의 현자라고도 불렸지만 지상에 떨어져 마인이 되었습니다.<end>"

## 690071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "破壊神の強力な魔人、<line-break>１２人の円卓騎士のひとりで<line-break>名前を知るものという異名を持ちます。<end>"
EN: "The cat is a powerful demon of the God of Destruction, one of the twelve Knights of the Round Table, with the epithet “The One Who Knows the Name.”<end>"
KO: "파괴신의 강력한 마인, 12인의 원탁기사 중 하나로 '이름을 아는 자'라는 이명을 지니고 있습니다.<end>"

## 690072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "円卓騎士には他に<line-break>アーギルシャイアなどがいますね。　　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "The other Knights of the Round Table include Argyleshire.<end>"
KO: "원탁기사에는 그 밖에도 아르길샤이어 등이 있습니다.<end>"

## 690073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "破壊神ウルグの円卓騎士のひとりです。<line-break>妖艶な美女の姿をしています。<line-break>　<line-break>きわめて、気まぐれで残酷です。<line-break>すでに神器を手に入れるため<line-break>ミイスの村を焼き払っています。<end>"
EN: "This is one of the Knights of the Round Table who serve Ulugh, the God of Destruction, and takes the form of an alluringly beautiful woman. This Knight is exceedingly capricious and cruel, and has already burned Miis Village to the ground in pursuit of a divine artifact.<end>"
KO: "파괴신 울그의 원탁기사 중 하나입니다. 요염한 미녀의 모습을 하고 있습니다. 매우 변덕스럽고 잔혹합니다. 이미 신기를 손에 넣기 위해 미이스 마을을 불태웠습니다.<end>"

## 690074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "究極生物を作ろうとして、<line-break>その製法を禁断の聖杯から得ようと<line-break>ねらっています。<line-break>また、その究極生物の核には<line-break>グフトクを使おうと考えています。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "This Knight seeks to create the Ultimate Life-Form and obtain the means to do so from the Forbidden Grail. Guftok is intended to serve as the creature's core.<end>"
KO: "궁극 생물을 만들려고 그 제조법을 금단의 성배에서 얻으려 노리고 있습니다. 또 그 궁극 생물의 핵에는 구프트크를 쓰려 하고 있습니다.<end>"

## 690075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "セラとは何か因縁があるようですが、<line-break>セラはそのことについて<line-break>一切、語ろうとしません。<line-break>円卓騎士には他に<line-break>うちのネモなどがいますね。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "There seems to be some history between this Knight and Sera, but Sera refuses to say anything about it. There are other Knights of the Round Table as well, including our own Nemo.<end>"
KO: "세라와 무슨 인연이 있는 듯하지만 세라는 그 일에 대해 전혀 말하지 않습니다. 원탁기사 중에는 우리 네모도 있지요.<end>"

## 690076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですか。<end>"
EN: "I see.<end>"
KO: "그렇습니까.<end>"

## 690077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、こんな感じですかね。<line-break>わかっていただけましたか？<end>"
EN: "Well, that about covers it. Does that make sense?<end>"
KO: "뭐, 이런 느낌입니다. 이해하셨나요?<end>"

## 690078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "聞きたいことがあれば<line-break>答えますよ。<end>"
EN: "If there's anything you'd like to ask, I'll be happy to answer.<end>"
KO: "궁금한 게 있으면 대답해 드리죠.<end>"

## 690079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "世界を巡って、人に出会い、　　　<line-break>仲間を作ってください。<line-break>　<line-break>その中で世界は<line-break>様々な顔を見せてくれるでしょう。<line-break>自由な旅を！<end>"
EN: "Travel the world, meet people, and find companions. Along the way, the world will show you many different faces. Travel as you please!<end>"
KO: "세계를 돌아다니며 사람을 만나고 동료를 만드세요. 그 속에서 세계는 여러 얼굴을 보여 줄 겁니다. 자유로운 여행을!<end>"

## 690080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すごいゴブ！ <line-break>やっぱりこの聖杯は我らを<line-break>幸せの地へ連れていってくれるゴブ！<end>"
EN: "Amazing, gob! I knew this Grail would lead us to the land of happiness, gob!<end>"
KO: "대단하다 고브! 역시 이 성배는 우리를행복의 땅으로 데려가 줄 거다 고브!<end>"

## 690081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "団長、幸せの地もいいゴブが<line-break>自分は腹が減ったゴブ。<end>"
EN: "Chief, the land of happiness sounds nice, gob, but I'm hungry, gob.<end>"
KO: "단장, 행복의 땅도 좋지만 나는 배고프다 고브.<end>"

## 690082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オルナット、お前はいつも食い気ゴブ！<line-break>ちゃんと団長の話を聞くゴブ！<end>"
EN: "Ornut, all you ever think about is food, gob! Listen to the chief, gob!<end>"
KO: "오르나트, 너는 늘 먹을 생각뿐 고브! 단장 말 좀 제대로 들어 고브!<end>"

## 690083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうゴブよね、団長！ <line-break>我々、ゴブゴブ団は<line-break>この聖杯の力できっと幸せになるゴブ！<end>"
EN: "That's right, isn't it, Chief? With the power of this Grail, the Gob-Gob Gang is sure to find happiness, gob!<end>"
KO: "그렇고브, 단장! 우리 고브고브단은 이 성배의 힘으로 반드시 행복해질 고브!<end>"

## 690084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "む、腹が減ったゴブな。<line-break>よし、ゴブゴブ団、食事ゴブ！<end>"
EN: "Hmm, I am hungry, gob. Right! Gob-Gob Gang, time to eat, gob!<end>"
KO: "음, 배고프다 고브. 좋아, 고브고브단, 식사다 고브!<end>"

## 690085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "団長までそれゴブか！<line-break>ひどいゴブ！<end>"
EN: "Not you too, Chief, gob! That's awful!<end>"
KO: "단장까지 그러는 거냐고브! 너무하다고브!<end>"

## 690086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 690087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 690088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 690089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうだった？<line-break>私の方は、噂だけ聞いたっていう<line-break>話ばっかりで、居場所は全然…。<end>"

KO: "어땠어? 난 소문만 들었다는 이야기뿐이라 있는 곳은 전혀…<end>"

## 690090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Hmm? That's the savage woman's sidekick, gob. Which means...?<end>"
KO: "음? 저건 난폭녀의 부하 고브. 그렇다면…?<end>"

## 690091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Oh, <value:$28>. There you are.<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28>, 여기 있었구나.<end>"

## 690092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "む、やはり例によって、凶暴女が<line-break>いいかげん飽き飽きの展開で登場ゴブ！<line-break>　<line-break>ゴブゴブ団、戦略的転進ゴブ！<line-break>マルーン、オルナット、行くでゴブ！<end>"
EN: "Hmm. Right on cue, the savage woman appears for the same tiresome scene yet again, gob! Gob-Gob Gang, make a strategic withdrawal! Maroon, Ornut, let's move, gob!<end>"
KO: "으, 역시나 흉포한 여자가 지긋지긋한 전개로 등장했다 고브! 고브고브단, 전략적 후퇴다 고브! 마룬, 오르나트, 가자 고브!<end>"

## 690093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "店主さん、申しわけないが<line-break>さっきの注文は取り消しゴブ。　　<line-break>　<line-break>では、さらばゴブ。<line-break>できれば我々のことは<line-break>もう捜さないでほしいゴブ。<end>"
EN: "Sorry, but we'd like to cancel our earlier order, gob. Farewell, then. And if possible, please don't come looking for us again, gob.<end>"
KO: "주인장, 미안하지만 아까 주문은 취소다 고브. 그럼 이만 고브. 가능하면 우리를 더는 찾지 말아 줬으면 한다 고브.<end>"

## 690094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、それと少年、<line-break>人生のパートナーは<line-break>ちゃんと選んだ方がいいゴブよ。<line-break>ガサツ女がパートナーだと<line-break>きっと後々苦労するゴブよ。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Oh, and one more thing, lad. Choose your partner in life carefully, gob. A crude woman will only bring you trouble down the road.<end>"
KO: "아, 그리고 소년. 인생의 동반자는 제대로 고르는 게 좋아 고브. 거친 여자를 동반자로 고르면 나중에 분명 고생해 고브.<end>"

## 690095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう！　何なのよ！？<line-break>許さない！<line-break>こら、待ちなさいよ！<end>"
EN: "Oh, come on! What was that about!? I won't let you get away with this! Hey, wait!<end>"
KO: "정말! 뭐야!? 용서 못 해! 야, 기다려!<end>"

## 690096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あーっ！　あんたたち！<line-break>いいかげん、聖杯を返しなさいよ！<end>"
EN: "Ah! You lot! Give back the Grail already!<end>"
KO: "아! 너희들! 이제 그만 성배를 돌려줘!<end>"

## 690098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なぜゴブ！　なぜ、ガサツ女は<line-break>現れないゴブ！！<end>"
EN: "Why, gob!? Why hasn't the crude woman appeared!?<end>"
KO: "왜 고브! 왜 거친 여자는 나타나지 않는 고브!!<end>"

## 690099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうゴブ！　そうゴブ！<line-break>パターンは繰り返さないとダメゴブ！<end>"
EN: "Exactly, gob! Exactly! You have to repeat the pattern, gob!<end>"
KO: "그렇다 고브! 그렇다 고브! 패턴은 반복해야 한다 고브!<end>"

## 690100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お約束だからこそ、基本だからこそ、<line-break>おろそかにしてはならんでゴブ！<line-break>　<line-break>そういうことでは…<line-break>そういう姿勢では…<line-break>立派なアレになれないゴブよ！！<end>"
EN: "Precisely because it's the routine―because it's fundamental―we must never neglect it, gob! If that's how you... if that's your attitude... you'll never become a respectable... something, gob!!<end>"
KO: "정석이기 때문에, 기본이기 때문에 소홀히 해서는 안 된다 고브! 그런 식으로는… 그런 자세로는… 훌륭한 그것이 될 수 없다 고브!!<end>"

## 690101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "親分、アレって何でゴブ？<end>"
EN: "Boss, what's the “something,” gob?<end>"
KO: "두목, 저게 뭐야 고브?<end>"

## 690102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…アレは…アレ…ゴブ。<end>"
EN: "The... something is... something, gob.<end>"
KO: "…그건… 그거… 고브.<end>"

## 690105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だ、だまされてはダメゴブ！！<line-break>これはあのガサツ女のおまけの罠ゴブ！<line-break>　<line-break>む。危ないところだったでゴブ！<line-break>本当に静かになってしまったゴブ！<line-break>ゴブゴブ団、撤退ゴブ！<end>"
EN: "D-don't be fooled, gob!! This is a trap laid by the savage woman's sidekick! Hmm. That was close, gob! It really did go completely quiet! Gob-Gob Gang, retreat, gob!<end>"
KO: "소, 속으면 안 된다 고브!! 이건 저 거친 여자가 남긴 추가 함정이다 고브! 음, 위험할 뻔했다 고브! 정말 조용해져 버렸다 고브! 고브고브단, 철수다 고브!<end>"

## 690106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何？<line-break>しゃべるゴブリン？　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>んー、そんな噂は聞いたが<line-break>実際この目で見たことはないな。<end>"
EN: "What? A talking Goblin? Hmm. I've heard rumors, but I've never seen one with my own eyes.<end>"
KO: "뭐? 말하는 고블린? 음, 그런 소문은 들었지만 실제로 본 적은 없군.<end>"

## 690107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な、な、な、な、何ゴブ！？<line-break>この聖杯は我らゴブゴブ団の物ゴブ。<line-break>　<line-break>これは我らが手に入れた物ゴブ！<line-break>これがあれば人間に襲われないゴブ！<line-break>これが我らを幸せにしてくれるゴブ！<end>"
EN: "W-w-w-w-what, gob!? This Grail belongs to us, the Gob-Gob Gang, gob! We got it, gob! With this, humans won't attack us! This will make us happy, gob!<end>"
KO: "뭐, 뭐, 뭐, 뭐라는 고브!? 이 성배는 우리 고브고브단 물건인 고브. 우리가 손에 넣은 물건인 고브! 이게 있으면 인간들에게 습격당하지 않는 고브! 이게 우리를 행복하게 해 주는 고브!<end>"

## 690108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お、お前、これをねらってるゴブな！<line-break>渡さないゴブ！　渡せないゴブ！<line-break>我らは絶対に幸せになるのでゴブ！<end>"
EN: "Y-you're after the Grail, gob! You can't have it! We can't give it up! We are going to find happiness, no matter what, gob!<end>"
KO: "너, 너 이걸 노리는 고브구나! 안 줄 고브! 줄 수 없는 고브! 우리는 반드시 행복해질 거고브!<end>"

## 690109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "団長、何を騒いでいるゴブ？<end>"
EN: "Chief, what's all the commotion, gob?<end>"
KO: "단장, 뭘 그렇게 떠드는 거야 고브?<end>"

## 690110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こいつが我らから禁断の聖杯を強奪し<line-break>我らを不幸のどん底に突き落としてやる<line-break>イーヒヒヒ、と言ってるゴブ！<end>"
EN: "This one says, “I'll seize the Forbidden Grail from the lot of you and cast you into the depths of misery! Hee hee hee!” Gob!<end>"
KO: "이 녀석이 우리에게서 금단의 성배를 강탈하고 우리를 불행의 밑바닥에 떨어뜨리겠다고 이히히히 하면서 말하는 고브!<end>"

## 690111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ひどいゴブ！　ひどいゴブ！<line-break>どうしてそんなひどいことを言うゴブ！<end>"
EN: "That's horrible, gob! Horrible! Why would you say such a horrible thing, gob?<end>"
KO: "너무해 고브! 너무해 고브! 왜 그런 심한 말을 하는 거야 고브!<end>"

## 690112
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "聖杯は渡さないゴブ！<line-break>ゴブゴブ団、撤退ゴブ～！<end>"
EN: "You'll never get the Grail, gob! Gob-Gob Gang, retreat, gooob!<end>"
KO: "성배는 못 준다 고브! 고브고브단, 철수 고브~!<end>"

## 690113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、おねーさん、勘定はテーブルの上に<line-break>置いておいたゴブ。<line-break>　<line-break>それと追加注文は全部キャンセルゴブ。<line-break>キャンセルが通らなかったら<line-break>その分はこいつから取ってほしいゴブ！<end>"
EN: "Oh, miss! I left the money for the bill on the table, gob. And cancel all the extra orders, gob. If you can't cancel them, make this one pay for them, gob!<end>"
KO: "아, 누나. 계산은 테이블 위에 놓아 둔 고브. 그리고 추가 주문은 전부 취소 고브. 취소가 안 되면 그 몫은 이 녀석에게 받아 줘 고브!<end>"

## 690114
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "団長！　あいつゴブ！<end>"
EN: "Captain! It's that one, gob!<end>"
KO: "단장! 저 녀석 고브!<end>"

## 690115
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "む、しつこい奴ゴブ！<line-break>エンシャントからこんな所まで、<line-break>この聖杯を追ってきたゴブか？<line-break>む、無益な戦いはさけたかったゴブが、<line-break>仕方ないゴブ。<line-break>ゴブゴブ団、突撃ゴブ！<end>"
EN: "Hmph. What a persistent pest, gob! Did you come all the way from Ancient in pursuit of this Grail, gob? I wanted to avoid a pointless fight, but so be it, gob. Gob-Gob Gang, charge, gob!<end>"
KO: "음, 끈질긴 녀석이다 고브! 엔샨트에서 여기까지 이 성배를 쫓아온 거냐 고브? 무익한 싸움은 피하고 싶었지만 어쩔 수 없다 고브. 고브고브단, 돌격이다 고브!<end>"

## 690116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "む、勝敗は兵家の常。<line-break>ゴブゴブ団、退却ゴブ！<end>"
EN: "Hmph. Victory and defeat are the fortunes of war. Gob-Gob Gang, retreat, gob!<end>"
KO: "흥, 승패는 병가의 상사. 고브고브단, 후퇴 고브!<end>"

## 690117
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これに懲りて<line-break>もう、我らの幸せの邪魔はするなゴブ！<line-break>　<line-break>さあ、聖杯とともに<line-break>ゴブゴブ団、幸せに向けて<line-break>進軍を続行するゴブ～！<end>"
EN: "Let that teach you not to stand in the way of our happiness again, gob! Now, with the Grail, the Gob-Gob Gang will continue its march toward happiness, gooob!<end>"
KO: "이번 일로 정신 차리고 이제 우리 행복을 방해하지 마라 고브! 자, 성배와 함께 고브고브단은 행복을 향해 진군을 계속하는 고브~!<end>"

## 690118
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "む。また、お前ゴブか！<line-break>しつこいゴブ。　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>我らの幸せのため、<line-break>お前たちに聖杯は渡せないゴブ。<line-break>ゴブゴブ団、突撃ゴブ！<end>"
EN: "Hmph. You again, gob? You just don't give up, gob. For the sake of our happiness, we can't let you have the Grail, gob. Gob-Gob Gang, charge, gob!<end>"
KO: "음. 또 너 고브냐! 끈질기다 고브. 우리 행복을 위해 너희에게 성배는 못 준다 고브. 고브고브단, 돌격 고브!<end>"

## 690119
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "マルーンがやられたゴブ～。<line-break>ゴブゴブ団、撤退ゴブ～。<end>"
EN: "Maroon's down, gob! Gob-Gob Gang, retreat, gob!<end>"
KO: "마룬이 당했다 고브~. 고브고브단, 후퇴다 고브~.<end>"

## 690120
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "団長！　別に、自分だけが<line-break>やられたわけではないゴブよ～。<end>"
EN: "Boss! I'm not the only one who got beaten, gob!<end>"
KO: "단장! 단장만 당한 건 아니잖아 고브~.<end>"

## 690121
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゴブゴブ団、大勝利ゴブ！ <line-break>こんな弱っちい奴、ほっといて<line-break>ゴブゴブ団、進軍続行ゴブ～！<end>"
EN: "A glorious victory for the Gob-Gob Gang, gob! Forget a weakling like that. Gob-Gob Gang, onward, gob!<end>"
KO: "고브고브단 대승리 고브! 이런 약해 빠진 녀석은 내버려 두고 고브고브단 진군 계속 고브~!<end>"

## 690122
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どちらへ行かれるのです？<line-break>そちらにいるのは、闇の怪物…。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>ティラの娘と呼ばれ、神々の争いで<line-break>闇の先兵として破壊をほしいままにした<line-break>強力な、いにしえの怪物です。<end>"
EN: "Where are you going? What awaits you there is a monster of darkness... It is a powerful monster from ancient times, known as the Daughter of Tira. During the conflict among the gods, it served as the vanguard of darkness and wreaked destruction at will.<end>"
KO: "어디로 가시는 겁니까? 저쪽에 있는 건 어둠의 괴물…. 티라의 딸이라 불리며 신들의 전쟁에서 어둠의 첨병으로 파괴를 일삼았던 강력한 고대의 괴물입니다.<end>"

## 690123
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…あなたがただけでは、<line-break>すこし、危険では、ありませんか？　　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Would it not be rather dangerous for you to face it on your own?<end>"
KO: "…당신들만으로는 조금 위험하지 않겠습니까?<end>"

## 690124
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "無礼をお許しください。ただ、<line-break>私の実力と、私に害意がないことを<line-break>理解していただきたかったのです。　　<line-break>よみがえりし闇の怪物を倒す。<line-break>私とあなたがたの目的は同じ。<line-break>私とあなたがたは協力できるはずです。<end>"
EN: "Forgive my discourtesy. I merely wished to demonstrate my abilities and assure you that I mean no harm. Our purpose is the same: to defeat the resurrected monster of darkness. We should be able to work together.<end>"
KO: "무례를 용서해 주십시오. 다만 제 실력과 제가 해칠 뜻이 없다는 것을 이해해 주셨으면 했습니다. 되살아난 어둠의 괴물을 쓰러뜨린다. 저와 여러분의 목적은 같습니다. 우리는 협력할 수 있을 겁니다.<end>"

## 690125
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ともに怪物を倒しましょう。<line-break>いかがですか？<end>"
EN: "Let us defeat the monster together. What do you say?<end>"
KO: "함께 괴물을 쓰러뜨립시다. 어떠신가요?<end>"

## 690126
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "結構です。<line-break>まいりましょう。<end>"
EN: "Very well. Let us be on our way.<end>"
KO: "좋습니다. 가죠.<end>"

## 690127
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "相手は強力な怪物です。<line-break>油断なきよう…。<end>"
EN: "You're facing a powerful monster. Do not let your guard down.<end>"

## 690128
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 690129
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 690132

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 690133
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こっちには来なかった。<line-break>…背後に瞬間移動を<line-break>使える者がいるということだな…。<line-break>これで確かになった。<line-break>瞬間移動が使える者など<line-break>そうはいない。<end>"

## 690134
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "貴様、名を名乗れ。<end>"

## 690135

KO: "이 동굴 밖까지 같이 가 주겠다. 이름을 대라.<end>"

## 690136

## 690137
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 690138
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…フッ<line-break>相手を見て突っかかるのだな。　<line-break>　<line-break>無謀な挑戦は、自分だけではなく<line-break>周りの人間にも迷惑がかかる。<line-break>…覚えておけ。<end>"

## 690139
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 690140

## 690141

## 690142
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Quite the guardian act. But you seem to be confusing your own dream with that man's.<end>"

## 690144
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 690145
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…フッ、どうした？<line-break>ひよっこをひよっこ扱いしただけだ。　<line-break>　<line-break>自分の能力と立場を見極め、<line-break>相手との相対的な関係を見抜かなければ<line-break>いずれ、ひどい目に遭うことになるぞ。<end>"
EN: "Hmph. What's wrong? I merely treated a novice like a novice. Unless you learn to judge your own abilities and position in relation to your opponent, you'll come to grief one day.<end>"
KO: "…흥, 왜 그래? 풋내기를 풋내기 취급했을 뿐이다. 자기 능력과 처지를 파악하고 상대와의 상대적 관계를 읽지 못하면 언젠가 호되게 당할 거다.<end>"

## 690146
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ふん。<end>"
EN: "Hmph.<end>"
KO: "…흥.<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 690151
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 690152
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フッ…、自分が何者なのか<line-break>わかっている人間などいない。<line-break>　<line-break>だが、お前が<line-break>何者かは教えてやる。<line-break>役立たずだ。<end>"

## 690153

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "放せ！　放せしてよ！<line-break><value:$28>！<end>"

## 690155
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 690156
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フッ、他人の迷惑もかえりみず<line-break>お節介と勇み足、<line-break>…結構なことだ。<end>"

## 690157

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 690158
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "単純な構造の頭だから<line-break>今まで後悔をしたことがないだろうが、<line-break>　<line-break>忘れられない後悔をしでかす前に、<line-break>そのおっちょこちょいなところは<line-break>治しておくことだな。<end>"
EN: "With a mind that simple, I doubt you've ever regretted anything. But you'd best cure that heedless streak before it leaves you with a regret you'll never forget.<end>"
KO: "단순한 머리 구조라 지금까지 후회라는 걸 해 본 적 없겠지만, 잊을 수 없는 후회를 만들기 전에 그 덜렁대는 성격은 고쳐 두는 게 좋겠군.<end>"

## 690159
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なーんか、当たってる気がするから<line-break>なおのことムカツクねえ…。<end>"
EN: "What really gets me is, I think you might have a point...<end>"
KO: "왠지 맞는 말 같아서 더 열받네…<end>"

## 690160
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかったよ。<line-break>あんたがそう言うんならさ…。<line-break>　<line-break>ふーんだ。<line-break>なんだい。<line-break>ちょっといい男だと思ってさ！<end>"

## 690161
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なぁーにぃ！？　この下等生物は！？<line-break>生意気よ！　生意気よ！　生意気よお！<end>"

KO: "뭐라고!? 이 하등생물은 뭐야!? 건방져! 건방져! 건방지다고!<end>"

## 690162
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…フッ、無知な<line-break>わがままエルフか？<end>"
EN: "Hmph. An ignorant, spoiled Elf, are you?<end>"
KO: "…훗, 무지하고 제멋대로인 엘프인가?<end>"

## 690163
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "無知なわがままエルフぅ？　よくって？<line-break>アタクシは１６００年も生きてるの…！<line-break>　<line-break>１００年も生きていない下等生物に<line-break>無知呼ばわりされるようなことなんて<line-break>絶対、絶対、絶対なくってよぉおお！<end>"
EN: "An ignorant, spoiled Elf? Now listen here! I have lived for sixteen hundred years! I will absolutely, absolutely, absolutely not be called ignorant by some lower life-form not even a hundred years old!<end>"
KO: "무지하고 제멋대로인 엘프으? 잘 들어! 나는 1600년이나 살았어…! 100년도 못 산 하등생물에게 무지하다는 소리를 들을 일은 절대, 절대, 절대 없어어어!<end>"

## 690164
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そ、そね。　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>コホン。<line-break>　<line-break>高貴で優雅ななエルフであるアタクシが<line-break>こぉんな下等生物に本気で怒るなんて…<line-break>ほんの少し、はしたないことだったわ。<end>"
EN: "Y-yes, quite. Ahem. For a noble and graceful Elf such as myself to become truly angry with such a lowly life-form... was perhaps just the tiniest bit unseemly.<end>"
KO: "…그, 그러네. 흠흠. 고귀하고 우아한 엘프인 내가 이런 하등생물에게 진심으로 화내다니… 조금은 품위 없는 일이었어.<end>"

## 690165
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…では、行くぞ。<end>"
EN: "Come. We're leaving.<end>"
KO: "…그럼, 간다.<end>"

## 690166
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くすっ、<value:$28>、<line-break>また会えてうれしいわ。　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>でも、とっても残念。<line-break>約束が果たせそうもないわ…。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. I'm so glad to see you again, <value:$28>. But what a terrible shame. It seems I won't be able to keep my promise...<end>"
KO: "후후, <value:$28>. 다시 만나서 기뻐. 하지만 정말 아쉽네. 약속을 지키기 어려울 것 같아….<end>"

## 690167
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私、あなたと一緒にいる人が邪魔なの。<line-break>だから、あなただけを、じっくり<line-break>殺してあげられないの…。<end>"
EN: "The person with you is in my way. So I won't be able to take my time killing you, and you alone...<end>"
KO: "난 네 곁에 있는 사람이 방해돼. 그래서 너 하나만 천천히 죽여 줄 수가 없어….<end>"

## 690168
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "貴様が…、やはり…！<end>"
EN: "You... I knew it!<end>"
KO: "네놈이…, 역시…!<end>"

## 690169
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "本当に残念だわ。<line-break>でも、これがふたりの運命だったのよ。<line-break>　<line-break>あなたの苦痛の記憶はあきらめるわ。<line-break>…じゃ、これ以上ここにいると<line-break>別れがつらいから、失礼するわね。<end>"
EN: "It truly is a shame. But this was the fate of the two of us. I'll have to give up on the memory of your suffering. Well, if I stay any longer, parting will only grow harder. So I'll take my leave.<end>"
KO: "정말 아쉽네. 하지만 이게 두 사람의 운명이었던 거야. 당신의 고통스러운 기억은 포기할게. …그럼 더 있으면 헤어지기 힘들 테니 먼저 갈게.<end>"

## 690170
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待て！<end>"
EN: "Wait!<end>"
KO: "기다려!<end>"

## 690171
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しつこいわ、セラ。<line-break>もういいかげん、大人になって…。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>私も、もう大人なの。<line-break>あなたの保護はいらないわ。<line-break>…新しい保護者もできたしね。くすっ。<end>"
EN: "You're so persistent, Sera. It's time you grew up. I'm an adult now, too. I don't need your protection. Besides, I have a new guardian now. Hee hee.<end>"
KO: "끈질기네, 세라. 이제 좀 어른이 돼…. 나도 이제 어른이야. 네 보호는 필요 없어. …새 보호자도 생겼고. 후훗.<end>"

## 690179
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか、猫屋敷…という所で　　　　　<line-break>呼び出すのだな。<line-break>　<line-break>ぼんくらどもをパーティーから外して、<line-break>さっさと俺を加えることだな。<line-break>俺より優れた者などいない。<end>"
EN: "I see. So Cat Manor is where I am to be summoned. Remove those fools from the party and add me at once. No one surpasses me.<end>"
KO: "그런가, 고양이 저택이라는 곳에서 부르는 거군. 멍청이들을 파티에서 빼고 얼른 나를 넣도록 해. 나보다 뛰어난 자는 없으니까.<end>"

## 690180
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…俺が、ただひとり認めた<line-break>あの男をのぞいてはな。<end>"
EN: "Except for that man―the only one I have ever acknowledged.<end>"
KO: "…내가 유일하게 인정한 그 남자를 제외하면 말이지.<end>"

## 690181
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、腹を立てなさんなって。<line-break>世の中にはああいう奴もいる。　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>腕も結構、立つみたいだしな。<line-break>ま、不器用でかわいらしいじゃないか？<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Now, don't get so angry. There are people like that in the world. He seems quite capable, too. And isn't that awkwardness of his rather endearing?<end>"
KO: "뭐, 화내지 마. 세상에는 저런 녀석도 있어. 실력도 꽤 있는 것 같고. 서툴러서 귀엽잖아?<end>"

## 690182
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あれはあれで結構つらい生き方だぜ？<line-break>ま、理解してやれ、とまでは言わないが<line-break>少なくとも拒絶はしないでやるんだな。<end>"
EN: "It's a hard way to live, being like that. I won't ask you to understand him, but at least don't reject him outright.<end>"
KO: "그건 그것대로 꽤 힘든 삶이야. 뭐, 이해해 주라고까지는 안 하겠지만 적어도 거부하지는 말아 줘.<end>"

## 690183
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なぁにぃ？　あの不作法な男は？<line-break>自分がド下等生物だってことを　　　　<line-break>わきまえているのかしら？<line-break>なんでも強引に、勝手に決めちゃって。<line-break>秘密の臭いだけプンプンさせちゃって。<line-break>ちょっと強いからっていい気になって！<end>"
EN: "What is with that ill-mannered man? Does he even realize he's the lowest of lower life-forms? Always forcing his way and deciding everything on his own! Reeking of secrets! Getting conceited just because he's a little strong!<end>"
KO: "뭐야아? 저 무례한 남자는? 자기가 최하등 생물이라는 걸 알고나 있는 거야? 뭐든 억지로 자기 멋대로 정해 버리고. 비밀 냄새만 잔뜩 풍기고. 좀 강하다고 잘난 척은!<end>"

## 690184
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "絶対、絶対、絶対、絶対、絶対、絶対、<line-break>気に入らないわぁー！<end>"
EN: "I absolutely, absolutely, absolutely, absolutely, absolutely, absolutely cannot stand him!<end>"
KO: "절대, 절대, 절대, 절대, 절대, 절대 마음에 안 들어어어!<end>"

## 690185
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フェティをなだめる<end>"
EN: "Calm Fety down<end>"
KO: "페티를 달랜다<end>"

## 690186
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フェティをしかる<end>"
EN: "Scold Fety<end>"
KO: "페티를 꾸짖는다<end>"

## 690187
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "甘いわ！　あなた、同じ人間族だから<line-break>あの無礼者をかばってるんじゃなくて？<end>"
EN: "You're too soft! Aren't you defending that insolent wretch just because you're both human?<end>"
KO: "무르네! 당신, 같은 인간족이라고 그 무례한 자를 감싸는 거 아냐?<end>"

## 690188
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうね。確かに<line-break>あなたは種族で差別はしないわ。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>それで、高貴なエルフと<line-break>他のド下等生物と同じ扱いにするのには<line-break>ほんと、アタクシも閉口しているもの。<end>"
EN: "Well... yes. You certainly don't discriminate by race. In fact, I'm thoroughly exasperated by the way you treat noble Elves exactly the same as all those other utterly inferior creatures.<end>"
KO: "…그렇네. 확실히 당신은 종족으로 차별하진 않아. 그래서 고귀한 엘프와 다른 하등생물을 똑같이 취급하는 건 정말 나도 질색이지만.<end>"

## 690189
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…わかったわ。あなたがそう言うなら、<line-break>５６７０００００００歩譲って<line-break>一応アタクシも納得してさしあげるわ。<end>"
EN: "Very well. If you insist, I'll concede 5,670,000,000 steps and deign to accept your argument―for the moment.<end>"
KO: "…알았어. 네가 그렇게 말한다면 56억 걸음 양보해서 일단 나도 납득해 주겠어.<end>"

## 690190
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28><line-break>どうして、アタクシをしかるわけ？<line-break>この場合、おかしいのはあの男よー！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, why are you scolding me? He's the one in the wrong here!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 왜 나를 혼내는 거야? 이 경우 이상한 건 그 남자잖아ー!<end>"

## 690191
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちゃんとした事情も語らないくせに<line-break>こっちが何か尋ねようとすると怒る。　<line-break>　<line-break>強引に、勝手にすべてのことを決定して<line-break>アタクシたちのことをよく知らないのに<line-break>侮辱するようなことばかり言うのよー？<end>"
EN: "He won't even give us a proper explanation, yet he gets angry whenever we try to ask him anything. He forces through every decision entirely on his own, and even though he barely knows us, all he does is insult us!<end>"
KO: "제대로 된 사정도 말하지 않으면서 이쪽이 뭔가 물어보면 화를 내. 억지로 제멋대로 모든 걸 결정하고 우리를 잘 알지도 못하면서 모욕적인 말만 한다고오!<end>"

## 690192
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたのアタクシへの非難は不当だわ。<line-break>絶対、今すぐ即時撤回してちょうだい。<line-break>そして、あなたの非を認めてー！<end>"
EN: "Your accusations against me are completely unjustified. I demand that you retract them immediately―right this instant! And admit that you were wrong!<end>"
KO: "당신이 나를 비난한 건 부당해. 당장 즉시 전부 철회해 줘. 그리고 당신 잘못을 인정해애!<end>"

## 690193
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "非を認めて撤回する<end>"
EN: "Admit fault and withdraw<end>"
KO: "잘못을 인정하고 철회한다<end>"

## 690194
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "撤回しない<end>"
EN: "Do not withdraw<end>"
KO: "철회하지 않는다<end>"

## 690195
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それならいいわ。許してさしあげてよ。<line-break>今後、言動には気をつけていただくわ。<end>"
EN: "Then that's all right. Please let it go. I'll see that more care is taken with words and actions from now on.<end>"
KO: "그렇다면 됐어요. 용서해 주세요. 앞으로 언행에는 주의하도록 하겠습니다.<end>"

## 690196
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "信じられない、信じられないわ！<line-break>自分の非を認められないの？<line-break>つまらないメンツが大事だから？　　　<line-break>そんな人と旅を続けられなくてよ！<line-break>お別れよ、さよなら。２度と会わない！<end>"
EN: "I can't believe it. I simply can't! Can you really not admit when you're in the wrong? Is your petty pride that important? I can't go on traveling with someone like you! This is goodbye. Farewell. I'm never seeing you again!<end>"
KO: "믿을 수 없어, 정말 믿을 수가 없어! 자기 잘못을 인정하지 못하는 거야? 하찮은 체면이 그렇게 중요해? 그런 사람이랑 계속 여행 못 해! 끝이야, 잘 있어. 두 번 다시 안 만나!<end>"

## 690197
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フェティが<line-break>パーティーから離脱しました。<end>"
EN: "Fety has left the party.<end>"
KO: "페티가 파티에서 이탈했습니다.<end>"

## 690198
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…あいつ、ぶん殴ってやる！<end>"
EN: "I'm gonna punch that jerk!<end>"
KO: "…저 녀석, 한 대 갈겨 주겠어!<end>"

## 690199
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…わかったよ。お前がいいんなら、<line-break>俺もセラのことがまんしてやるよ。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>あいつが、なんだか秘密ぶった<line-break>ちょっと腕が立つことを自慢している<line-break>鼻持ちならない奴でも、かまわないさ。<end>"
EN: "All right. If Sera's fine by you, I'll put up with him too. I don't care if he's an insufferable show-off who acts all mysterious and brags about being halfway decent in a fight.<end>"
KO: "…알겠어. 네가 괜찮다면 나도 세라를 참아 줄게. 그 녀석이 뭔가 비밀스러운 척하고, 조금 싸움을 잘한다고 자랑하는 재수 없는 놈이어도 상관없어.<end>"

## 690200
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あいつが、顔がよくて、クールそうで　<line-break>そのくせ、どこか感情的に不安定で<line-break>そこが、女の母性本能くすぐって、<line-break>いつでもどこでもモテモテ大王で<line-break>奴がそれでのぼせまくっていて<line-break>言い寄る女をゴミのように扱って、<end>"
EN: "I don't care if Sera's handsome and seems cool, yet is somehow emotionally unstable in a way that stirs women's maternal instincts and makes him wildly popular with women wherever he goes, or if all that attention has gone to his head and he treats women who come on to him like garbage.<end>"
KO: "그 녀석은 얼굴도 잘생겼고 쿨해 보이는데, 또 어딘가 감정적으로 불안정해서 그게 여자들의 모성본능을 자극하고, 언제 어디서나 인기 만점이라 그걸로 잔뜩 우쭐해서 들이대는 여자들을 쓰레기처럼 다루고,<end>"

## 690201
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そのせいで何百人もの女が泣いていて<line-break>その女たちに惚れている何千人もの男が<line-break>その影で泣いていて…<line-break>そいつらが思いあまって犯罪に走ってて<line-break>それが、この世界をすさませていても<line-break>俺はかまわないさ！　かまわないとも！<end>"
EN: "I don't care if hundreds of women are crying over him, while thousands of men in love with those women are crying in the shadows... or if those men get so desperate they turn to crime and make the whole world a harsher place! I don't care! I really don't!<end>"
KO: "그 때문에 수백 명의 여자가 울고, 그 여자들을 사랑한 수천 명의 남자가 뒤에서 울고…. 그들이 절망 끝에 범죄를 저질러 이 세계가 황폐해진다 해도 난 상관없어! 정말 상관없다고!<end>"

## 690202
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、かまわないさ！<line-break>そうさ、俺はかまわないさ！<line-break>俺はどうせチェリーくんさああ！！　　<end>"
EN: "Yeah, I don't care! That's right, I don't care! I'm just a viiiirgin anyway!!<end>"
KO: "아아, 상관없어! 그래, 난 상관없다고! 난 어차피 모태솔로라고오오!!<end>"

## 690203
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…なんだか、かたくなな奴だなあ。<line-break>あのセラって奴…。　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>ハハ、あいつ見てると<line-break>リディオンのこと思い出すぜ。<line-break>アミラル一の剣士、リディオンをさ。<end>"
EN: "Sera sure is stubborn... Ha. Watching him reminds me of Lidion, the best sword fighter in Amiral.<end>"
KO: "…왠지 완고한 녀석이네. 그 세라라는 놈…. 하하, 저 녀석 보고 있으면 리디온이 생각나. 아미랄 최고의 검사, 리디온 말이야.<end>"

## 690204
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "リディオンも銀狼を退治したほど<line-break>腕は立ったが、かたくなな奴だからな。<line-break>誰とも仲よくしようとしない。<line-break>何かと、俺のことライバル視して<line-break>突っかかってきてた。<line-break>…奴がああなったのもわけがあるんだ。<end>"
EN: "Lidion was skilled enough to defeat the Silver Wolf, but just as hardheaded. Never tried to make friends with anyone. Always treated me like a rival and picked fights with me over one thing or another. But there was a reason Lidion ended up that way.<end>"
KO: "리디온도 은랑을 쓰러뜨릴 정도로 실력은 있었지만 워낙 고집 센 놈이라 아무와도 친해지려 하지 않았지. 툭하면 날 라이벌로 보고 덤벼들었어. …녀석이 그렇게 된 데도 이유가 있어.<end>"

## 690205
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あいつ、小さいころ、ロベルトさんの<line-break>娘と冒険気分で、町の外に出てたのさ。<line-break>俺たちもよくやったろ？<line-break>そのとき、モンスターに襲われてさ…。<line-break>幸い、旅の冒険者が駆けつけて…<line-break>リディオンだけは助かったんだが、な。<end>"
EN: "When Lidion was little, Lidion and Roberto's daughter went outside town, pretending they were off on an adventure. We used to do that too, remember? Then a monster attacked them... Luckily, a traveling adventurer came running... but only Lidion survived.<end>"
KO: "그 녀석 어릴 때 로베르토 씨 딸과 모험 기분을 내며 마을 밖으로 나갔었어. 우리도 자주 그랬잖아? 그때 몬스터에게 습격당했고…. 다행히 지나가던 모험자가 달려와서… 리디온만은 살아남았지만.<end>"

## 690206
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロベルトさんの娘はダメだった。<line-break>…その娘を守れなかったって泣いてた<line-break>リディオンの顔が今も忘れられないぜ。<line-break>それからだな、<line-break>リディオンがみんなに心を閉ざして、<line-break>剣だけに打ち込むようになったのは…。<end>"
EN: "Roberto's daughter didn't make it. I'll never forget the tears on Lidion's face over not being able to protect her. That was when Lidion shut everyone out and devoted everything to the sword.<end>"
KO: "로베르토 씨 딸은 구하지 못했어. …그 아이를 지키지 못했다며 울던 리디온 얼굴을 지금도 잊을 수가 없다. 그때부터였지. 리디온이 모두에게 마음을 닫고 검에만 매달리게 된 건….<end>"

## 690207
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あのセラって奴も<line-break>そんな感じがする。<line-break>奴も、つらいんじゃないかな。　　　　<line-break>んじゃな、<value:$28>。<line-break>　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Something about Sera feels the same. Maybe he's hurting too. See you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그 세라라는 녀석도 그런 느낌이야. 녀석도 힘든 게 아닐까. 그럼 또 보자, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 690208
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "姉ちゃん、姉ちゃん。<line-break>あのセラって奴さ、<line-break>姉ちゃん、どお？　シュミ？<end>"
EN: "Sis! Hey, sis! What do you think of Sera? Your type?<end>"
KO: "누나, 누나. 저 세라라는 녀석 말이야. 누나 취향이야?<end>"

## 690209
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "취미일지도<end>"

## 690210
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "別に…<end>"
EN: "Not really...<end>"
KO: "별로…<end>"

## 690211
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だよなあ。姉ちゃん、エストとか<line-break>守ってあげたい系に弱いかんな。<line-break>筋肉が反応しちゃうんだな。<end>"
EN: "Yeah, I thought so. You've always had a weakness for people like Est, Sis―the kind you want to protect. Gets those muscles of yours going, doesn't it?<end>"
KO: "그렇지. 누나는 에스트처럼 지켜 주고 싶은 타입에 약하니까. 근육이 반응해 버리는 거지.<end>"

## 690212
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "아야! 누나, 자꾸 때리지 좀 마! 머리 나빠지잖아! 정말!<end>"

## 690213
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "チャカは少し頭が悪くなった。<line-break>ＩＮＴが１下がった。<line-break>　<line-break>チャカは少し打たれ強くなった。<line-break>ＶＩＴが１上がった。<end>"

KO: "차카는 조금 머리가 나빠졌다. INT가 1 내려갔다. 차카는 조금 더 맷집이 좋아졌다. VIT가 1 올랐다.<end>"

## 690214
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だよなあ。　<line-break>姉ちゃんって、ああいうもてそうなの、<line-break>興味なさそうだからなあ。よかった。<end>"
EN: "Yeah, I thought so. You've never seemed interested in the popular type, Sis. Good.<end>"
KO: "그렇지. 누나는 저렇게 인기 많을 것 같은 타입엔 관심 없어 보이니까. 다행이네.<end>"

## 690215
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃ、姉ちゃん、いつもどおり<line-break>町出るときに待ち合わせってことで。<end>"
EN: "All right, Sis. We'll meet up when we leave town, same as always.<end>"
KO: "그럼 누나, 평소처럼 마을을 나갈 때 만나기로 하자.<end>"

## 690216
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…しっかし、クールでかっこいいねえ。<line-break>そのくせに子供みたいにわがままで、<line-break>どこか影があって…セクシーだねえ。<line-break>結構、実力もあるしさ。<line-break>なんていうか、<line-break>ちょっと惹かれちゃうねえ。　　　　<end>"
EN: "Still... so cool and good-looking, yet willful as a child, with a hint of darkness... Sexy, too. And pretty capable. I don't know. Somehow, I'm kind of drawn to all that.<end>"
KO: "…그래도 쿨하고 멋있네. 그러면서도 어린애처럼 제멋대로고, 어딘가 그늘이 있어서… 섹시하기도 하고. 실력도 꽤 있고 말이야. 뭐랄까, 조금 끌리네.<end>"

## 690217
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すごい自信よね。　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>オッシ先生が言ってたわ。<line-break>実力があっても、かえってそれで<line-break>目をふさがれて、自滅するって。<end>"
EN: "That's quite some confidence. Master Ossi said that even real ability can blind you and lead you to your own ruin.<end>"
KO: "대단한 자신감이네. 오시 선생님이 그러셨어. 실력이 있어도 오히려 그 때문에 눈이 가려져 자멸한다고.<end>"

## 690218
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんか、かっこいいよね。セラってさ。<line-break>ちょっと、気になるかな。<line-break>　<line-break>…ってボクが言ったら<line-break><value:$28>は気になる？<end>"
EN: "Sera's kind of cool, don't you think? I might be a little interested. If I said that, would it bother you, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "뭔가 멋있지, 세라는. 조금 신경 쓰인달까. …내가 그렇게 말하면 <value:$28>은 신경 쓰여?<end>"

## 690219
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、ウソウソ、冗談だよ！<line-break>ね、ね、こっち向いてよ！<end>"
EN: "Oh, no, I was kidding! Come on, look at me!<end>"
KO: "아, 거짓말 거짓말, 농담이야! 자, 자, 이쪽 좀 봐!<end>"

## 690220
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あー、ボクのことからかってたんだ！ <line-break>ひどいよ、<value:$28>。<line-break>ボク…本気で嫌われたと思ったんだよ！<end>"
EN: "Oh, you were teasing me! That's mean, <value:$28>. I really thought you hated me!<end>"
KO: "아, 나 놀린 거였구나! 너무해, <value:$28>. 나… 진짜 미움받은 줄 알았어!<end>"

## 690221
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ボク、やっぱり役立たずなのかな。<end>"
EN: "Maybe I really am useless.<end>"
KO: "…나, 역시 쓸모없는 걸까.<end>"

## 690222
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "セラは…純粋だね。<line-break>何も知らないってことは幸せなんだ。<line-break>それに、そう…、いいことだよ。<end>"
EN: "Sera is... innocent. There's happiness in knowing nothing. And... yes. That's a good thing.<end>"
KO: "세라는… 순수하네. 아무것도 모른다는 건 행복한 일이야. 그리고 그래… 좋은 일이기도 하지.<end>"

## 690223
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人は何かを追い求めるとき　　　　　　<line-break>他のものを見失う。<line-break>…あの男はそういう男だ。<line-break>他の物が見える男より<line-break>他の物が見えなくなる男の方が<line-break>求めるべき物だけを見ることができる。<end>"
EN: "When people pursue something, they lose sight of everything else. That's the kind of man he is. A man who can no longer see anything else can focus solely on what he ought to seek, unlike one who still sees other things.<end>"
KO: "사람은 무언가를 좇을 때 다른 것을 놓친다. …그 남자는 그런 사람이야. 다른 것까지 보이는 남자보다 다른 것이 보이지 않게 되는 남자가 자신이 추구해야 할 것만 바라볼 수 있지.<end>"

## 690225
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どちらへ行かれるのです？<line-break>そちらにいるのは、闇の怪物…　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>ティラの娘と呼ばれ、神々の争いで<line-break>闇の先兵として破壊の限りをつくした<line-break>強力な、いにしえの怪物です。<end>"
EN: "Where are you headed? Over there is a monster of darkness, an ancient and powerful creature known as the Daughter of Tira. It served as darkness's vanguard in the conflict among the gods and wrought utter devastation.<end>"
KO: "어디로 가시는 겁니까? 그쪽에 있는 건 어둠의 괴물…. 티라의 딸이라 불리며 신들의 전쟁에서 어둠의 첨병으로 온갖 파괴를 일삼았던 강력한 고대의 괴물입니다.<end>"

## 690226
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…あなたがただけでは、<line-break>少し、危険では、ありませんか？　　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Would it not be a little dangerous for you to go there by yourselves?<end>"
KO: "…여러분만으로는 조금 위험하지 않을까요?<end>"

## 690227
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "無礼をお許しください。ただ、<line-break>私の実力と、私に害意がないことを<line-break>理解していただきたかったのです。　　<line-break>よみがえりし闇の怪物を倒す…。<line-break>私とあなたがたの目的は同じ。<line-break>私とあなたがたは協力できるはずです。<end>"
EN: "Forgive my discourtesy. I merely wanted you to understand what I am capable of, and that I have no intention of harming you. We share the same goal: to defeat the monster of darkness that has risen again. We should be able to work together.<end>"
KO: "무례를 용서해 주십시오. 다만 제 실력과 제가 해칠 뜻이 없다는 것을 이해해 주셨으면 했습니다. 되살아난 어둠의 괴물을 쓰러뜨린다…. 저와 여러분의 목적은 같습니다. 우리는 협력할 수 있을 겁니다.<end>"

## 690228
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ともに怪物を倒しましょう。<line-break>いかがですか？<end>"
EN: "Let us defeat the monster together. What do you say?<end>"
KO: "함께 괴물을 쓰러뜨립시다. 어떠신가요?<end>"

## 690229
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待て、<value:$28>…<line-break>あの刀は…。<end>"
EN: "Wait, <value:$28>... That sword...<end>"
KO: "잠깐, <value:$28>… 저 검은….<end>"

## 690230
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さ、まいりましょう。<end>"
EN: "Come, let us be on our way.<end>"
KO: "자, 가시죠.<end>"

## 690231
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あれは…？<line-break>いや、まさか…。<end>"
EN: "Is that...? No, it couldn't be...<end>"
KO: "저건…? 아니, 설마….<end>"

## 690232
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "相手は強力な怪物です。<line-break>油断なきよう…。<end>"
EN: "Your opponent is a powerful monster. Do not let your guard down.<end>"
KO: "상대는 강력한 괴물입니다. 방심하지 마십시오….<end>"

## 690233
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "念には念…ということです。<line-break>それでは。<line-break>また会うこともあるでしょう。<end>"
EN: "One can never be too careful. Farewell. Perhaps we shall meet again.<end>"
KO: "돌다리도 두드려 보라는 거죠. 그럼. 또 만날 일도 있겠죠.<end>"

## 690234
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…待て。<line-break>貴様、名を名乗れ。<end>"
EN: "Wait. State your name.<end>"
KO: "…기다려. 네놈, 이름을 대라.<end>"

## 690235
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私の名…ですか？<line-break>私は…、　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>…私は、そう、仮面のしもべ…、<line-break>あのお方は私をサイフォスと<line-break>お呼びくださいます。<end>"
EN: "My name...? I am... I am, yes, a servant of the mask. The exalted one is gracious enough to call me Xiphos.<end>"
KO: "제 이름…입니까? 저는… 저는… 그래요, 가면의 종…. 그분은 저를 사이포스라 불러 주십니다.<end>"

## 690236
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "サイフォス？<line-break>それが本当に貴様の名なのか？<end>"
EN: "Xiphos? Is that truly your name?<end>"
KO: "사이포스? 그게 정말 네 이름이냐?<end>"

## 690237
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…では。<end>"
EN: "Well, then...<end>"
KO: "…그럼.<end>"

## 690238
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待て！<end>"
EN: "Wait!<end>"
KO: "기다려!<end>"

## 690239
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…貴様には関わりのないことだ。<line-break>人のことにかまっていられるほどの<line-break>実力もない者は、黙っているんだな。<end>"
EN: "This is no concern of yours. If you lack the strength to spare for other people's affairs, keep your mouth shut.<end>"
KO: "…네가 상관할 일이 아니다. 남의 일에 참견할 만큼 실력도 없는 자는 입 다물고 있어라.<end>"

## 690240
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "行くぞ。<end>"
EN: "Let's go.<end>"
KO: "간다.<end>"

## 690242
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうだな、<line-break>お前も、同じ、か。<line-break>…行くぞ。<end>"
EN: "Yes... I suppose you're the same. Let's go.<end>"
KO: "…그래, 너도 같은가. …가자.<end>"

## 690243
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "結構です。<line-break>まいりましょう。<end>"
EN: "Very well. Let us be on our way.<end>"
KO: "좋습니다. 가죠.<end>"

## 690244
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "相手は強力な怪物です。<line-break>油断なきよう…。<end>"

KO: "상대는 강력한 괴물입니다. 방심하지 마십시오….<end>"

## 690245
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "念には念を…ということです。<line-break>それでは。<line-break>また会うこともあるでしょう。<end>"

## 690246
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私の名…ですか？<line-break>私は…、　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>…私は、そう、仮面のしもべ…、<line-break>あのお方は私をサイフォスと<line-break>お呼びくださいます。<end>"
EN: "My name...? I am... I am, yes, a servant of the mask. The exalted one is gracious enough to call me Xiphos.<end>"
