## 690247
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…では。<end>"
EN: "Well, then...<end>"
KO: "…그럼.<end>"

## 690248
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おや、またお会いできましたね。　　　<line-break>…<value:$28>さん。<line-break>　<line-break>今回も我々の目的は同じ…<line-break>そう見ましたが、いかがでしょう？<line-break>また協力させていただきたいのですが。<end>"
EN: "Well, we meet again... <value:$28>. It seems our goals are aligned once more. Would you agree? I'd like us to work together again.<end>"
KO: "이런, 또 만났군요. …<value:$28> 씨. 이번에도 우리의 목적은 같은 것 같은데, 어떻습니까? 또 협력하고 싶습니다만.<end>"

## 690249
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…あなただけは、なぜか<line-break>不審をぬぐえないようですね。<end>"
EN: "And yet, for some reason, you alone can't seem to shake your suspicions.<end>"
KO: "…당신만은 어째서인지 의심을 거둘 수 없는 모양이군요.<end>"

## 690250
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…なぜだか、<line-break>思い出せないのか。<end>"
EN: "For some reason... the memory won't come back?<end>"
KO: "…왜인지 기억이 안 나는 건가.<end>"

## 690251
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ。それに警戒されるべき理由など<line-break>存在するのでしょうか？<line-break>　<line-break>とにかく、まいりましょう。<line-break>あなたのような戦士とご一緒できて<line-break>光栄ですよ、<value:$28>さん。<end>"
EN: "Who can say? Is there truly any reason to be wary? In any case, let us be on our way. It is an honor to travel with a warrior such as you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "글쎄요. 경계받을 이유가 존재하기는 할까요? 어쨌든 갑시다. 당신 같은 전사와 함께할 수 있어 영광입니다, <value:$28> 씨.<end>"

## 690252
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "確かに世界は広い。じゃが、<line-break>この広い世界にお前の居場所はない。<line-break>　<line-break>人間どもにとって<line-break>お前は恐怖以外の何ものでもない。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "The world is vast, that much is true. But in all this vast world, there is no place for you. To humans, you are nothing but an object of fear.<end>"
KO: "확실히 세계는 넓다. 하지만 이 넓은 세계에 네가 있을 곳은 없다. 인간들에게 너는 공포 그 자체일 뿐이다.<end>"

## 690253
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前が、住むべきわずかな場所と、　<line-break>生きていくのに必要なだけの<line-break>肉を求めたとしても、<line-break>奴らの頭の中では、<line-break>お前は凶悪な侵略者でしかない。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "You may seek only some small place to live and enough meat to survive, but in their minds, you will be nothing more than a vicious invader.<end>"
KO: "네가 살 작은 장소와 살아가는 데 필요한 만큼의 고기를 원할 뿐이어도, 녀석들 머릿속에서 넌 흉악한 침략자일 뿐이다.<end>"

## 690254
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "奴らはお前の力に嫉妬し、恐怖し、　<line-break>憎悪し、そしてお前を殺しに来る。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Your power fills them with envy, fear, and hatred, and they come to kill you.<end>"
KO: "놈들은 네 힘을 질투하고 두려워하며, 증오하고, 결국 널 없애러 올 거다.<end>"

## 690255
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前が身を守れば、お前は奴らの<line-break>敵だったことが証明される。<line-break>　<line-break>かくて、正義の名の下に<line-break>奴らの刃がお前を襲う。<line-break>…つぎつぎとな。<end>"
EN: "If you defend yourself, they will take it as proof that you were their enemy. And so, in the name of justice, their blades will fall upon you... one after another.<end>"
KO: "네가 자신을 지키면 네가 놈들의 적이었다는 게 증명된다. 그리하여 정의의 이름 아래 놈들의 칼날이 너를 덮친다. …차례차례 말이지.<end>"

## 690256
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうじゃ。なれば、<line-break>地上から奴らを消せばいい。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>奴らがお前にすることを<line-break>ただ、やり返してやればいい。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Yes. Then you need only wipe them from the face of the earth. Simply do to them what they would do to you.<end>"
KO: "그래. 그렇다면 지상에서 놈들을 없애면 된다. 놈들이 네게 하는 짓을 그대로 되갚아 주면 돼.<end>"

## 690257
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "奴らが正しいならば、<line-break>お前にはそれ以上の正当性がある。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "If they are in the right, then your cause is more just still.<end>"
KO: "그들이 옳다면 너에게는 그보다 더 큰 정당성이 있다.<end>"

## 690258
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そら、お前を殺そうとして<line-break>凶悪な冒険者がやってきおった。<line-break>まず、奴らを血祭りにあげよ。<end>"
EN: "See? Vicious adventurers have come to kill you. Start by slaughtering them.<end>"
KO: "자, 널 죽이려고 흉악한 모험가들이 왔다. 우선 녀석들을 피바다로 만들어라.<end>"

## 690259
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "念には念…ということです。　　　　　<line-break>おかしいですか？<line-break>　<line-break>もしかしたら、これが、そちらの方の<line-break>不審を招いているのかもしれませんね。<line-break>フフ、我ながら、律儀なもので…。<end>"
EN: "One can never be too careful. Does that seem strange? Perhaps this is what's making that person suspicious. Heh. How very conscientious of me...<end>"
KO: "확실히 해 두려는 것뿐입니다. 이상합니까? 어쩌면 이것이 그분의 의심을 부르고 있는지도 모르겠군요. 후후, 나도 참 성실해….<end>"

## 690260
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…違う！　サイフォスとやら、<line-break>思い出さないのか！<end>"
EN: "No! Xiphos, or whatever you're called―don't you remember?<end>"
KO: "…아니야! 사이포스라는 자, 기억나지 않는 거냐!<end>"

## 690261
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ですから、いったい何をです？<end>"
EN: "As I keep asking, remember what?<end>"
KO: "그러니까 대체 뭘 말입니까?<end>"

## 690262
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それでは。<value:$28>さん、<line-break>また会える日を楽しみに。<end>"
EN: "Until next time, <value:$28>. I look forward to the day we meet again.<end>"
KO: "그럼 이만. <value:$28>님, 다시 만날 날을 기대하겠습니다.<end>"

## 690263
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何も、思い出さないのか…？<end>"
EN: "You remember nothing at all...?<end>"
KO: "아무것도 기억나지 않는 건가…?<end>"

## 690264
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…寄るな！<end>"
EN: "Stay back!<end>"
KO: "…다가오지 마!<end>"

## 690265
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…なんでもない。<line-break>ゆくぞ。<end>"
EN: "It's nothing. Let's go.<end>"
KO: "…아무것도 아니다. 가자.<end>"

## 690266
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、まいりましょう。<line-break><value:$28>さん。<line-break>　<line-break>あなたのような<line-break>資質の優れた方と<line-break>ご一緒できて光栄です。<end>"
EN: "Then let us be on our way, <value:$28>. It is an honor to travel in the company of someone of your exceptional caliber.<end>"
KO: "그럼 가시죠, <value:$28>님. 당신처럼 자질이 뛰어난 분과 함께할 수 있어 영광입니다.<end>"

## 690267
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "念には念…ということです。<line-break>フフ、我ながら、律儀なものです…。<line-break>　<line-break>今日は、あなたのような、すばらしい<line-break>資質をお持ちの方とご一緒できて<line-break>非情に刺激になりました。<end>"
EN: "One can never be too careful, as they say. Heh. Quite conscientious of me, if I do say so myself. Spending the day with someone of your remarkable gifts has been most stimulating.<end>"
KO: "조심해서 나쁠 건 없다는 겁니다. 후후, 나도 참 성실하군요…. 오늘은 당신처럼 훌륭한 자질을 가진 분과 함께할 수 있어 무척 자극이 되었습니다.<end>"

## 690268
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また、お会いできる日を　　　　　　<line-break>楽しみにしています。<line-break>では、さらば！<end>"
EN: "I look forward to the day we meet again. Until then, farewell!<end>"
KO: "다시 만날 날을 기대하고 있겠습니다. 그럼, 안녕히!<end>"

## 690269
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しゃべるゴブリン？<line-break>っへーっ！　すごいねぇ。<line-break>きっとゴブリンの英雄なんだろうな。<end>"
EN: "A talking Goblin? Wow! That's amazing. Must be some great Goblin hero.<end>"
KO: "말하는 고블린? 헤에! 대단하네. 틀림없이 고블린의 영웅인가 봐.<end>"

## 690270
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "聖杯って何のことだろう？<end>"
EN: "I wonder what the Grail is.<end>"
KO: "성배가 대체 뭘까?<end>"

## 690271
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え？　ゴブリンが…しゃべるんですか？<line-break>そんな不思議なことって…。<end>"
EN: "What? A Goblin... can talk? Can something that strange really happen...?<end>"
KO: "네? 고블린이… 말을 한다고요? 그런 신기한 일이….<end>"

## 690272
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うっわーっ！！<line-break>ホントにゴブリンがしゃべってたよ！<line-break>でも、あんまりカッコよくないね。<end>"
EN: "Wow! That Goblin really did talk! Not very impressive-looking, though.<end>"
KO: "우와아!! 정말 고블린이 말했어! 그런데 별로 멋있진 않네.<end>"

## 690273
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うそ…。ゴブリンが…しゃべってた…？<line-break>こんな不思議なことって…。<line-break>　<line-break>ああ、テラネのお母さん、<line-break>都会は不思議なことばっかりです…。<end>"
EN: "No way... A Goblin... really talked? Can something that strange really happen...? Oh, Mother back in Terane, the city is full of strange things...<end>"
KO: "거짓말…. 고블린이… 말을 했어…? 이런 신기한 일이…. 아아, 테라네의 엄마, 도시는 신기한 일투성이예요….<end>"

## 690274
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こりゃあ、おどろいた。<line-break>実際、自分の目で見てみると<line-break>噂で聞くより奇妙なもんだな。<end>"
EN: "Well, that's a surprise. Seeing it with my own eyes, it's even stranger than the rumors made it sound.<end>"
KO: "이야, 놀랍네. 실제로 내 눈으로 보니 소문으로 듣던 것보다 더 기묘하군.<end>"

## 690275
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そこまでは付き合ってやろう。<line-break>行くぞ。ぐずぐずするな。<end>"
EN: "I'll go along with you that far. Come on. Don't dawdle.<end>"
KO: "거기까진 같이 가 주지. 간다. 꾸물대지 마.<end>"

## 690276
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec069-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その男との話は終わったのか？<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>俺は今、お前と話すことはない。<line-break>用が済んだのならすぐ出るぞ。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Are you finished talking to that man? I have nothing to say to you right now. If you're done here, we're leaving at once.<end>"
KO: "그 남자와 이야기는 끝났나? 난 지금 너와 할 말이 없어. 볼일이 끝났으면 바로 나가자.<end>"

## 700001

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よ。もしかして　　　　<line-break>ご同業？<line-break>闇の怪物を退治しに来たのかい？<end>"
EN: "Hey. You in the same line of work? Come to take down the dark monster?<end>"
KO: "여어. 혹시 동업자? 어둠의 괴물을 퇴치하러 왔나?<end>"

## 700002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やっぱ、そうか。<line-break>だが、奴の相手は、少しばかし<line-break>きついかもしんないぜ？<end>"

KO: "역시 그런가. 하지만 놈을 상대하는 건 조금 벅찰지도 몰라.<end>"

## 700003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "で、どうだい？<line-break>俺でよければ<line-break>手伝わしてくれないか？<end>"
EN: "So, what do you say? If you'll have me, mind letting me help?<end>"
KO: "그래, 어때? 나라도 괜찮다면 도와주게 해주지 않을래?<end>"

## 700004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "別に報酬はいらない。俺は好きで<line-break>こうしてうろついてるだけだからな。<line-break>ま、悪い話じゃないと思うぜ？<end>"
EN: "I don't need paying. I roam around like this because I enjoy it. Not a bad offer, is it?<end>"
KO: "보수는 필요 없어. 난 좋아서 이렇게 돌아다니는 것뿐이니까. 뭐, 나쁜 이야기는 아니라고 생각해.<end>"

## 700007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "素直だねえ。<line-break>素直なのはいいことだ。<line-break>　<line-break>んじゃ、自己紹介だ。<line-break>俺はゼネテス。<line-break>お前さんは？<end>"
EN: "Straightforward, aren't you? That's a good quality. Well, introductions, then. I'm Xenetes. And you are?<end>"

## 700008

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、ね。<line-break>よろしく、<value:$28>。<end>"

KO: "<value:$28>, 그렇지. 잘 부탁해, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 700009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "他人の手は借りないってわけだ。<line-break>勇敢だねえ。<end>"

KO: "남의 도움은 받지 않겠다는 거군. 용감하네.<end>"

## 700010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 700011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、ね。<line-break>カッコいいじゃない。　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>俺はゼネテスっていうんだ。<line-break>それじゃ、ま、がんばってな。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, huh? Nice name. I'm Xenetes. Well, good luck out there.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>이라고? 멋진 이름이네. 난 제네테스라고 해. 그럼 뭐, 힘내라고.<end>"

## 700012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう、<value:$28>。<line-break>久しぶり。　　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>しっかし、あのときはまいったぜ。<line-break>いきなり金持って姿消すんだからな。　<line-break>俺は酒場で待ちぼうけだ。<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>. Long time no see. Man, you really got me that time―you suddenly took the money and disappeared. I was left waiting at the tavern.<end>"
KO: "오, <value:$28>. 오랜만이야. 그런데 그때는 참 난감했어. 갑자기 돈 들고 사라져 버리니 말이야. 난 술집에서 하염없이 기다렸다고.<end>"

## 700013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "んで、ま、さよならさせてもらった。　<line-break>お前さんとは一緒に冒険できない<line-break>感じがしたんでな。悪いな。<line-break>今日はこの奥にいる闇の怪物退治かい？<line-break>余計なお節介かもしれないが<line-break>強いぜ、あの怪物は。<end>"
EN: "So I decided to say goodbye. I got the feeling we couldn't adventure together. Sorry about that. Here to take down the dark monster farther in today? Maybe it's none of my business, but that thing's strong.<end>"
KO: "그래서 뭐, 작별했지. 너랑은 같이 모험하기 힘들 것 같았거든. 미안하다. 오늘은 이 안쪽에 있는 어둠의 괴물 퇴치냐? 괜한 참견일지도 모르지만 그 괴물, 강해.<end>"

## 700014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだ、あんたか。　　　　　　　　　<line-break>また会ったな。<line-break>　<line-break>今日はこの奥の怪物退治かい？<line-break>余計なお節介かもしれないが<line-break>強いぜ、あの怪物は。<end>"
EN: "Oh, it's you again. Are you here to take down the monster farther in today? Maybe it's none of my business, but that thing's strong.<end>"
KO: "뭐야, 너였나. 또 만났군. 오늘은 이 안쪽의 괴물 퇴치인가? 괜한 참견일지 모르지만 그 괴물은 강해.<end>"

## 700015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺でよければ、<line-break>お手伝いさせてもらうが、<line-break>どうだい、<value:$28>？<end>"
EN: "If you'll have me, I'd be glad to help. What do you say, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "나라도 괜찮다면 도와주지. 어때, <value:$28>?<end>"

## 700019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうかい。<line-break>お節介なことを言って悪かったな。<end>"

KO: "그래. 참견해서 미안했어.<end>"

## 700020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう、<value:$28>。<line-break>奇遇だな。こんな所で。　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>俺も暇だったから、ちょっと<line-break>冒険をしていたのさ。<line-break>この奥にいる怪物が仕事か？<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>. Fancy meeting you in a place like this. I was bored, so I've been doing a little adventuring myself. Are you here on a job to deal with the monster farther in?<end>"
KO: "오, <value:$28>. 이런 데서 만나다니 우연이군. 나도 한가해서 조금 모험하고 있었어. 이 안쪽의 괴물이 네 일거리냐?<end>"

## 700021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか、だったら俺もお手伝い<line-break>させてもらおうかな？<line-break>どうだい、<value:$28>？<end>"
EN: "In that case, maybe I'll lend a hand. What do you say, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "그래? 그렇다면 나도 도와줄까? 어때, <value:$28>?<end>"

## 700022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml

KO: "좋아. 그럼 한동안 같이 잘 부탁해.<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうかい。<line-break>んじゃ、がんばれよ。<end>"
EN: "All right. Good luck, then.<end>"
KO: "그래. 그럼 힘내라.<end>"

## 700029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "確かに世界は広い。じゃが、<line-break>この広い世界にお前の居場所はない。<line-break>　<line-break>人間どもにとって<line-break>お前は恐怖以外の何ものでもない。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "The world is vast, that much is true. But in all this vast world, there is no place for you. To humans, you are nothing but an object of fear.<end>"
KO: "확실히 세계는 넓다. 하지만 이 넓은 세계에 네가 있을 곳은 없다. 인간들에게 너는 공포 그 자체일 뿐이다.<end>"

## 700030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前が、住むべきわずかな場所と、　<line-break>生きていくのに必要なだけの<line-break>肉を求めたとしても、<line-break>奴らの頭の中では、<line-break>お前は凶悪な侵略者でしかない。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "You may seek only some small place to live and enough meat to survive, but in their minds, you will be nothing more than a vicious invader.<end>"
KO: "네가 살 작은 장소와 살아가는 데 필요한 만큼의 고기를 원할 뿐이어도, 녀석들 머릿속에서 넌 흉악한 침략자일 뿐이다.<end>"

## 700031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "奴らはお前の力に嫉妬し、恐怖し、　<line-break>憎悪し、そしてお前を殺しに来る。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Your power fills them with envy, fear, and hatred, and they come to kill you.<end>"
KO: "놈들은 네 힘을 질투하고 두려워하며, 증오하고, 결국 널 없애러 올 거다.<end>"

## 700032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前が身を守れば、お前は奴らの<line-break>敵だったことが証明される。<line-break>　<line-break>かくて、正義の名の下に<line-break>奴らの刃がお前を襲う。<line-break>…つぎつぎとな。<end>"
EN: "If you defend yourself, they will take it as proof that you were their enemy. And so, in the name of justice, their blades will fall upon you... one after another.<end>"
KO: "네가 자신을 지키면 네가 놈들의 적이었다는 게 증명된다. 그리하여 정의의 이름 아래 놈들의 칼날이 너를 덮친다. …차례차례 말이지.<end>"

## 700033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうじゃ。なれば、<line-break>地上から奴らを消せばいい。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>奴らがお前にすることを<line-break>ただ、やり返してやればいい。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Yes. Then you need only wipe them from the face of the earth. Simply do to them what they would do to you.<end>"
KO: "그래. 그렇다면 지상에서 놈들을 없애면 된다. 놈들이 네게 하는 짓을 그대로 되갚아 주면 돼.<end>"

## 700034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "奴らが正しいならば、<line-break>お前にはそれ以上の正当性がある。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "If they are in the right, then your cause is more just still.<end>"
KO: "그들이 옳다면 너에게는 그보다 더 큰 정당성이 있다.<end>"

## 700035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そら、お前を殺そうとして<line-break>凶悪な冒険者がやってきおった。<line-break>　<line-break>まず、奴らを血祭りに上げよ。<line-break>よいな。<end>"
EN: "See? Vicious adventurers have come to kill you. First, slaughter them. Understand?<end>"
KO: "자, 너를 죽이려 흉악한 모험자들이 찾아왔다. 우선 녀석들을 피바다로 만들어라. 알겠느냐.<end>"

## 700036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、こいつも、実は<line-break>かわいそうな奴なのかもな。<line-break>　<line-break>んじゃな。<line-break><value:$28>。<line-break>また、会おうぜ。<end>"
EN: "Well, maybe this one is a pitiful creature too. See you, <value:$28>. Let's meet again.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 이 녀석도 사실 불쌍한 놈일지도 모르지. 그럼 간다, <value:$28>. 또 보자.<end>"

## 700044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0あら、かわいい坊やね。<line-break>くすくす…。<end>あら、かわいいコね。<line-break>くすくす…。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Oh, what a sweet little boy. Hee hee...<end>Oh, aren't you a sweet little thing. Hee hee...<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0어머, 귀여운 꼬마네. 후훗….<end>어머, 귀여운 아이네. 후훗….<end>"

## 700045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くすっ、おびえなくていいのよ。<line-break>ねぇ、ちょっと探してるものが<line-break>あるんだけど…。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee, there's no need to be afraid. You see, there's something I'm looking for...<end>"
KO: "후훗, 겁먹지 않아도 돼. 있잖아, 좀 찾는 게 있는데….<end>"

## 700046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "友達の宝物で<line-break>禁断の聖杯というの。<line-break>聞いたことないかしら？<end>"
EN: "It's a friend's treasure, called the Forbidden Grail. Have you ever heard of it?<end>"
KO: "친구의 보물인데, 금단의 성배라고 해. 들어본 적 없니?<end>"

## 700047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ウフフ、ご存じのようね。<line-break>じゃあ、しゃべるゴブリンは？<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Hee hee, so you do know. What about a talking Goblin?<end>"
KO: "후후, 알고 있나 보네. 그럼 말하는 고블린은?<end>"

## 700048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くすっ、なんでも知ってるのね。<line-break>頼もしいわ…。<line-break>じゃあ、私のことも知ってるのかしら？<end>"
EN: "Hee hee, you know everything, don't you? How reassuring... Then I wonder if you know who I am, too.<end>"
KO: "후후, 뭐든 다 아는구나. 든든해…. 그럼 나에 대해서도 알고 있을까?<end>"

## 700049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私がミイスの村を焼き払い<line-break>忘却の仮面を持ち去った<line-break>アーギルシャイアだってことも…。<end>"
EN: "That I'm Argyleshire―the one who burned Miis Village to the ground and stole the Mask of Oblivion...<end>"
KO: "내가 미이스 마을을 불태우고 망각의 가면을 가져간 아르길샤이어라는 것도….<end>"

## 700050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くすっ。<line-break>おとぎ話なんかでもよくあるでしょ？<line-break>知りすぎているのはよくないことよ。<line-break>でも、せっかくだから<line-break>聖杯のこと、聞かせてもらおうかしら。<line-break>あなたの記憶から、直接…。ウフフフ。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. You know how it goes in fairy tales: knowing too much can be dangerous. But since we're here, perhaps you can tell me about the Grail. I'll pluck it straight from your memories... Hee hee hee.<end>"
KO: "후후. 동화에서도 흔히 나오잖아? 너무 많이 아는 건 좋지 않아. 그래도 모처럼이니 성배 이야기를 들어 볼까. 네 기억에서 직접…. 우후후후.<end>"

## 700051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ねえ、動けないでしょ？<line-break>さあ、今私がゆっくり<line-break>なぶり殺してあげるわ。<end>"
EN: "You can't move, can you? Now I can take my time tormenting you to death.<end>"
KO: "자, 움직일 수 없지? 이제 내가 천천히 가지고 놀다 죽여 줄게.<end>"

## 700052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こういう時の人間の恐怖心って<line-break>とってもイイの。記憶をたぐる時に<line-break>ビンビンきて…。フフ、たまらないわ。<end>"
EN: "Human fear at a moment like this feels so good. I get such a thrill from it when I sift through your memories... Hee hee, I can't get enough.<end>"
KO: "이럴 때 인간의 공포심은 정말 좋아. 기억을 더듬을 때 찌릿찌릿 전해져서…. 후후, 참을 수가 없다니까.<end>"

## 700053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあ、いくわよ…。<end>"
EN: "Here we go, then...<end>"
KO: "그럼, 간다….<end>"

## 700054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誰か来る…。<line-break>無粋ね、こんな時に。<end>"
EN: "Someone's coming... How inconsiderate, at a time like this.<end>"
KO: "누가 온다…. 분위기 깨네, 이런 때에.<end>"

## 700055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まぁ、いいわ。<line-break>あせって、殺しちゃっても<line-break>物足りないもの。<end>"
EN: "Oh, well. It wouldn't be satisfying if I rushed and killed you now.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 됐어. 서두르다 죽여 버리면 시시하니까.<end>"

## 700056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今度、邪魔が入らない所で<line-break>ゆっくり殺してあげるわ。<line-break>楽しみにしてて…、ウフフ。<end>"
EN: "Next time, I'll kill you nice and slowly somewhere no one can interrupt us. You can look forward to it... Hee hee.<end>"
KO: "다음에는 방해받지 않는 곳에서 천천히 죽여 줄게. 기대하고 있어… 우후후.<end>"

## 700057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあね、<value:$28>。<line-break>その名前、覚えておくわ…。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "See you, <value:$28>. I'll remember that name...<end>"
KO: "그럼 안녕, <value:$28>. 그 이름, 기억해 둘게….<end>"

## 700058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おやおやおやおや、<line-break>どうしたんだい？　あんた。<line-break>こんな所で突っ伏しちゃって。<end>"
EN: "Oh dear, oh dear, oh dear. What happened to you? Lying facedown in a place like this.<end>"
KO: "어이구, 어이구. 무슨 일이야? 이런 데 엎어져 있고.<end>"

## 700062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほら、大丈夫かい？　　　　　<line-break>立てるかい？<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Come on, are you all right? Can you stand?<end>"
KO: "자, 괜찮아? 일어설 수 있겠어?<end>"

## 700063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大丈夫みたいだね。<line-break>物騒だからあんまりこんな所で<line-break>倒れてるんじゃないよ。<end>"
EN: "Looks like you're all right. It's dangerous around here, so try not to collapse in a place like this.<end>"
KO: "괜찮은 것 같네. 위험하니까 이런 데서자꾸 쓰러져 있으면 안 돼.<end>"
